# The Idea Registry

**This is the retrieval surface for Hornvale's speculative ideas — one line
each, scannable and greppable.** The [`frontier.md`](frontier.md) essays hold
the *argument*; this holds the *index*. Consult this table **before proposing
or reopening any idea** — an entry marked `rejected` or `ratified` is a
settled question, and reopening one needs new information, not a fresh
opinion. Then read the linked essay to understand it.
Like the essays it indexes, this chapter is part of The Frontier — the
book's clearly-marked speculative part; it records ambitions and verdicts,
not merged reality, and governs nothing.

Scope: this registry tracks the **vision/idea pipeline** — the speculative
directions that flow frontier → spec → build. Settled *engineering
constraints* (no `HashMap`, no wall-clock, typed quantities, …) live in the
[decision log](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/) and are not duplicated here; where a vision
idea *became* a decision, its row cross-links the decision instead of
restating it.

## How to read a row

- **ID** — a stable category-prefixed handle (`MAP-7`, `BIO-4`, …). IDs are
  permanent; a superseded idea keeps its ID and flips its status. New ideas
  take the next free number in their category; never renumber.
- **Status** — where the idea sits in the pipeline:
  - `shipped` — built and merged into the world.
  - `ratified (NNNN)` — settled as a decision; the number links the record.
  - `spec'd` — draining into a binding spec; **Where** points at the spec,
    not the essay.
  - `elaborated` — has a full essay in `frontier.md`; awaiting a spec.
  - `raw` — captured but not yet elaborated; a stub, not an argument.
  - `rejected` — considered and set aside; the row says why. **This is the
    anti-relitigation payload — the registry's most important status.**

  These six are the whole vocabulary and a drift-check enforces it. Category
  prefixes are open — coin a new one when an idea needs it — but statuses are
  not; do not invent a seventh.
- **Conf** — the essay's confidence tag, condensed (high / med / low /
  taste-gated), or `—` for settled/process rows.
- **Where** — the essay or record that elaborates it.

When an idea drains into a spec, flip its status and point **Where** at the
spec: the elaboration relocates, the breadcrumb stays, nothing is lost.
Repointing **Where** *replaces* a row's prose rather than appending to it —
the campaign narrative lives in the chronicle the row now links.

**Rows are capped at 600 characters of Idea prose**, enforced in CI. A row is
a shelf-mark: what the idea is, plus a pointer to where it is argued. If a row
needs a page, it needs a `frontier.md` essay and a status of `elaborated` —
not a longer row. The authoring rules live in this directory's `CLAUDE.md`.

| MAP-18 | Calendrics as computed knowledge — the sky's true periods (synodic month, year length) are exact almanac facts, but a culture *knows* them only at the precision it has attained, gated by its mathematical capability (TECH-1), its observation span (a record outlives its observers: MAP-8 writing, MEM-7 lifespan), and its method. The knowledge ladder is qualitative → counted → computed → predictive: below arithmetic a people still holds a qualitative model ("days lengthen after midwinter" — qualitative process theory is the formalism, shared with MAP-19), an unlettered people counts "about thirty days," an astronomer-priesthood holds a Metonic cycle and eventually the saros. Calendar-keeping is differentiating knowledge within the culture (a priestly caste whose legitimacy rests on intercalation authority and eclipse prediction, SOC-1; the calendar as a maintained MEM-4 artifact), the first science of UNI-1's inference program (SEQ-1's tidally-locked world is the culture with nothing to count), and natively scored: the diff between a culture's calendar constants and the almanac's true values is the TOOL-2 wrongness measure, with intercalation drift the visible failure — a festival migrating out of its season                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | raw             | high                 | [frontier §map](frontier.md#the-frontier-map)                              |
| MAP-19 | The commonsense ruleset — the "game rules" everyone takes for granted (two solids cannot occupy the same space, contained things move with their container, unsupported things fall): the sim's object-scale world-model, MAP-18's tabletop sibling (both are knowledge of the true ruleset held at culture-limited precision). Sources split by half. For the *ontology*: Inform 7's kinds-and-relations catalogue (containers, supporters, parts, doors — small and generative, built for a world observed through text), TADS 3's sense-passing model (a sealed glass jar passes sight but not touch or smell — feeding EXP-3/EXP-9 perception), Hayes's naïve-physics program (his ontology of liquids marks the classic hard case both IF libraries punt), and the qualitative calculi (RCC-8 containment/contact topology, Allen intervals, qualitative process theory) as deterministic dependency-free formalisms. For the *epistemic* half — the sharp part: individuals know the rules variously, and Baillargeon's event categories (occlusion, containment, support, collision, each with a documented acquisition order over Spelke's innate core-knowledge floor) supply an empirical curriculum for innate vs experienced vs taught; cross-linguistic spatial semantics (Korean tight-fit vs loose-fit containment) lets cultures lexicalize one physics differently (the EXP-7 render seam applied to physics); Gibson affordances make the rules body-relative (a supporter to a sprite is not one to a giant, BIO-2). Everyday competence is inference over the hidden ruleset (UNI-1 at the mundane pole), and where the metaphysics rewrites a rule (UNI-2; the incorporeal dead of UNI-4 *do* co-occupy with walls) each world's folk physics diverges with it. Cyc is the cautionary bound: enumerate nothing — keep the ruleset small and generative | raw             | med                  | [frontier §map](frontier.md#the-frontier-map)                              |

Where that line sits is a scale commitment with real cost — every container simulated, versus containment as a rule cultures merely reason over a coarser world with — and it should be drawn once, deliberately, as a spec question when a campaign first pulls for object-scale representation, not arrived at by drift. | MAP-22 | The biogeography of coexistence — placement-as-competitive-exclusion collapses a many-species world to one (Gause), so the strong quietly erase the weak; coexistence must be *held open* by pricing might. The reframe: might buys COST and RARITY, not ubiquity; weakness buys ABUNDANCE and REFUGE. Five composable, deterministic mechanisms: **carrying cost** (suitability as cell-yield-vs-species-need; the mighty viable in few cells → rare, the ecological rubber band), **niche differentiation** (cells as resource vectors, placement as matching not scalar ranking), the **might-ordered draft** (pick in decreasing might; the mighty pick first but few), **refugia + stratification** (the underdark stratum, commensal tenancy — kobolds in the dragon's shadow; a cell is a stack), **contingent succession** (biogeography as deep-time residue; incumbency shields the weak, extinctions open ground). Placement sibling of MAP-11 (habitat medium) and the biosphere carrying-capacity term. The deeper reframe: the placement stack **optimizes the wrong objective** — per-cell fitness-argmax *manufactures* monoculture; the realistic model is **diversity-under-viability**, a *clearing* problem in four layers (**substrate** = niche heterogeneity + underworld strata; **allocation** = a clearing *market* where might is a budget and cells carry emergent prices so everyone affords something, == the draft == largest-first bin-packing; **coupling** = one scalar, might, driving priority↑ footprint↑ count↓; **maintenance** = a *disturbance pump*, intermediate-disturbance + storage effect, keeping diversity alive over deep time). The minimal down-payment is a **founder floor** (reserve each people its best cell before competitive placement — the allocation layer at K=1). Surfaced in The Branches when a stronger authored people (hobgoblin) Pareto-dominated goblin off the map; the founder floor is the in-scope L1 unblock. **The founder floor shipped and migrated** to the new `demography` domain (campaign *The Gathering*, MAP-7's field half) — it now floors over attractors of that campaign's carrying-capacity flow rather than suitability scores, behavior preserved. The rest of this row's mechanisms (carrying cost, niche differentiation, the might-ordered draft, refugia + stratification, contingent succession) drained into a full design, the **coexistence-stack spec**, building on MAP-7's shipped field: footprint-as-mass-scaled-home-range and multi-grain density stacking; coexistence by a temperature-controlled `K^β` normalized share against competitors and a viability floor; bidirectional trophic coupling; soft-capacity overflow; and the derived byproducts (the strife field, wilderness-from-fragmentation, refugia). The-Gathering's interim per-species condensation loosens today's cross-tag spacing as an accepted, documented regression this spec restores | spec'd | high (mechanics) | [coexistence-stack spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-13-the-coexistence-stack-design.md) |
| MAP-23 | The Living Globe — a high-fidelity in-browser client (WebGPU/WebGL) animating winds, ocean currents, storm formation, and tribal migrations as GPU particle advection over sim-emitted fields; eyecandy is explicitly non-deterministic, licensed by the sim-emits-data/clients-render split (0022) and clients-own-toolchains (0023), so wow-factor never touches world identity. The pattern is *keyframes from the sim, life from the client*: the sim emits deterministic coarse fields and era snapshots, the client advects particles and tweens between them with free client-side randomness that never re-enters the ledger — coarse-constrains-fine at the animation layer. A v0 needs zero sim changes (static climate + the banked unrest field + crust terrain already suffice for animated wind/current flow over the epoch's lobed continents). Feeds on MAP-24; migrations additionally gated on MAP-7 | raw | med | Crust session, GPU/eyecandy brainstorm 2026-07-11 |

| MAP-25 | Domain-scoped world builds — a study that measures one domain still builds the whole world today (branches-family builds 1000 full level-6 worlds to measure only the language family battery, ~1.5h). A per-domain build that constructs only the layer a study reads plus its upstream dependencies cuts author-time census cost by most of its weight; the lab-performance lever the Crust merge made concrete, sibling of the shipped source-hash artifact cache (TOOL-12) and the census-fixture load (decision 0032). **Shipped 2026-07-13** as campaign *Lab Performance*: the view-typed build-depth ladder (`build_world_to(depth)`, `windows/worldgen`) stops the pipeline at a named rung (astronomy / terrain / climate / settlements / full), each metric carries the rung it reads and each extractor the narrowest view type that rung affords (so under-building is a compile error), and the lab runner builds each seed only to its study's deepest selected rung. A stopped ledger is a byte-identical prefix of the full build's, guarded by a metamorphic test (`windows/lab/tests/depth_ladder.rs`); measured wins are ~0% builds for all-astronomy censuses and ~4× for terrain-only. Two conditional sub-optimizations (a crust triple-read collapse, an astronomy ladder-split) were dropped by the profiler's numbers, recorded in the plan | shipped | high | [Lab Performance chronicle](../chronicle/lab-performance.md); [lab-performance spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-12-lab-performance-design.md) |
| MAP-26 | Adaptive / local mesh refinement — a non-uniform geodesic grid with finer cells near coasts or high relief. Rejected for now (Crust spec §12): it breaks the uniform-mesh assumptions the terrain/climate/placement stack rests on, for fidelity the crust fields (resampled at any resolution per decision 0038) and the render lens already provide without a variable mesh. Reconsider only if a mesh-bound quantity (drainage, placement) is shown to need local resolution a uniform level cannot afford | rejected | low | Crust spec §12, 2026-07-11 |
| MAP-27 | The verb as reaction (property-transformation chemistry) — the action-complement to MAP-19's object ontology. A verb is not a hand-authored verb×object outcome (the interactive-fiction combinatorial trap) but a *reaction*: entities are property-bags (flammable / container / locked / animate / audible / believes-X), verbs are transformations gated by required properties (preconditions → effects), and affordances EMERGE from property-matching → author M+N not M×N (systemic design, à la BOTW / Baba Is You). A verb IS a GOAP operator (PSY-6: preconditions = required properties, effects = property-change events), so the player's verb loop (metaplan Campaign III) and NPC planning (Campaign V) are ONE action system — player and NPC act through the same reactions over the same ledger (one machine, two users, like belief/motivation). Animacy is a *mutable* property verbs transform (kill: animate→inert, raise: →undead, charm: →controllable) and it selects the verb sub-language. Four chemistries over one core: physical / social (UNI-16 belief + SOC-9 graph) / epistemic (projection) / spatial (place graph). Cycle-03 of the room-scale corpus (the living layer — verbs as affordances) is a spatial/ecological elaboration of this chemistry ([cycle-03](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/design/room-scale/cycle-03-the-living-layer.md)). **The Foresight (2026-07-18)** shipped PSY-6's GOAP planner with the smallest concrete precedent for "a verb IS a GOAP operator": two hardcoded, heterogeneous actions (`MoveTo`, `Drink`), each a precondition/effect pair, exercising the kernel A* over exactly the shape this row describes at scale — but authored as Rust code, not as data. This row's M+N property-bag DSL (arbitrary authored verbs gated by object properties) remains the followup that generalizes those two hardcoded actions into the richer, author-once action set. **The Actants (2026-07-27) shipped this row's front porch, not its DSL**: the GOAP action roster is now reconciled against the concept registry in both directions — `Action::all()` + `concept_name()` behind a destructure tripwire, so a new verb cannot compile until it is named — and the four acts finally HAVE concepts (`move`/`drink`/`rest` registered, `eat` already a Swadesh entry), under a new `ConceptKind::Act` that separates what a creature DOES from what it HAS. That is the vocabulary precondition this row's chemistry needs (a verb must be nameable before it can be a reaction gated by properties) and nothing more: the actions are still four hardcoded Rust variants, still without preconditions expressed as data. When the DSL lands, the tripwire is what will force each authored verb to carry a concept | elaborated | high (framing) | [frontier §verb-chemistry](frontier.md#the-verb-as-reaction--a-property-transformation-chemistry) |
| MAP-28 | Room-scale subdivision — the room mesh: deterministic hierarchical subdivision that defines what a room *is* and gates every other room-scale primitive. Rooms are deeper triangles of the same level-5 icosphere, addressed by `(base face + child path)`, so adjacency stays in one continuous mesh and the pentagon problem is confined to 12 points. A spike proved the neighbour walk O(1) integer barycentric, not the O(depth) up-walk first feared. Refines *below* the canonical grid, unlike rejected [[MAP-26]]. Shipped, with two consumers since. Adaptive depth, chunking and concurrency deferred | shipped | high (spike) | [The Room Mesh spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-12-the-room-mesh-design.md); [The Locale Window chronicle](../chronicle/the-locale-window.md) |

The room-scale corpus's 60 ideation mechanisms reduce to **8 computational primitives** (the synthesis), each fitted to the constitution (determinism, layering, trace protocol, std-only). ADDITIVE — no counterpart elsewhere in the frontier; pure content gain riding on MAP-28's substrate. **The coarse-field inheritance path is now real, not just designed:** *The Locale Window* (2026-07-12, `windows/locale`) shipped the composition (max-weight categorical inheritance + integer-weighted continuous blend) this bestiary will sample from, plus a deliberately tiny `SubCellTexture` placeholder standing in for the descriptor grammar below — this row's ~70-entry bestiary and its 8 primitives are still unbuilt. **Natural-tier first slice shipped 2026-07-13** (campaign *The Uncommon Ground*): the `SubCellTexture` placeholder is replaced by a real `Regime` overlay spanning the natural strangeness rungs (0–30, steady/time-invariant) — the P3 negation-slot engine (one weighted draw per exclusion slot: substrate / energy / kingdom, plus an endemic toggle, so composites are coherent by construction; strangeness is the vector's derived max-departure magnitude, not a catalogue lookup), the free rung-15 derived tier (a conservative substrate proxy standing in for unbuilt DOM-14 lithology, plus a sub-cell `MicroField` — relief/aspect/wetness/openness from address noise — so homogeneous biome still varies room-to-room), and the budgeted rung-30 placed tier (P7: a bounded blue-noise `StrangenessBudget` genesis pass with a founder floor guaranteeing a geologically-warranted exotic is never missed by an unlucky draw, field-weighted acceptance, integer-hop repulsion, and an `endemic` gate on isolation). Findability rides `LocaleContext::strange_sites()`, a derived query, never stored (UNI-20). Schema bumped `locale/room/v1` → `v2`. Still unbuilt and each deferred to its named seam: rung 45 RELICT → MAP-30 palimpsest; ephemeral/phase → a future P8; rung 60 ENGINEERED → settlement + MAP-7; rungs 75/90 AETHERIC/FAERIE + the negative wing → the UNI-2/DOM-17 metaphysics gate; the radial core→margin gradient + contagion → a future P5 halo campaign; true lithology → DOM-14 | elaborated | med | [room-scale synthesis](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/design/room-scale/synthesis.md); [The Locale Window chronicle](../chronicle/the-locale-window.md); [The Uncommon Ground chronicle](../chronicle/the-uncommon-ground.md) |

| MAP-53 | **The debt is drainage — ley-lines as the erosional record of magical history, and the MAP-40/MAP-42 reconciliation.** MAP-40 banked a thaumic re-instantiation of the carve, resting on engine A's seam being written potential-agnostically *on purpose*; UNI-24's conservative magic supplies the one thing that row needs and does not have — **something that flows**. Then a caster is a **sink**, the land drains toward the working, and repeated casting at a place *incises a channel*: **a ley-line is an erosion feature**, not a drawn map — the accumulated scar of magical history, computed by the shipped carve and authored by nobody. It is also **extraction infrastructure**: the flux network is the sole exception to UNI-24's source = incidence identity, so it lets a mage draw from *elsewhere*, carrying the debt from hinterland to throne — the capital lush, the periphery ash. Core-periphery magical imperialism therefore becomes a **centrality claim about the flux graph**, the same checkable-invariant class UNI-5's stones lean on (diameter / connectivity / centrality), and Sorcerer-Kings sitting on nexuses become the *predicted* configuration rather than set dressing. **The reconciliation:** MAP-40 requires a conserved flux and MAP-42 is explicitly *ex nihilo* — the two banked rows quietly contradict, unnoticed because both are `raw`. Tiering makes both coherent: the conservative rung has a flux and therefore has ley-lines; the ex-nihilo rung sits above it and does not carve. Coarse constrains fine, applied to metaphysics — and it is an argument from two existing rows, not from preference | elaborated | med | [frontier §magic](frontier.md#magic-as-a-graph-grammar--the-debt-economy) |
| MAP-54 | **The surprisal budget — pricing fate's moves instead of gating them.** Addresses the one problem MAP-15's delimited event-game explicitly names as unsolved: the plausibility floor "has to be calibrated so that fate reads as uncanny inevitability rather than an authored cheat, and that calibration is a taste-and-Lab problem, not a formula." **The diagnosis: the floor is overloaded.** It is a *per-move* predicate being asked to enforce a property of the *whole chain* — raise it and fate is too weak to steer, lower it and fate works miracles, and neither setting can see the most common failure at all (many marginally-plausible moves, each clearing the bar, reading as relentless background rigging). That is a type error, not a tuning failure, and no value of the floor fixes it. **The fix:** give fate a **budget denominated in surprisal** (`-ln(p)` per transition) across the chain, and keep the floor for per-move magnitude — two knobs, two failure modes, each calibrating against a signal that is actually visible. A budget also produces the *right* texture where a threshold cannot: under a threshold every above-line move is free, so fate rigs constantly; under a budget **fate economises** — losing skirmishes, spending nothing on the ordinary, saving for the one branch that decides it. Fate is not strong; fate is thrifty. Implementable in existing machinery (spending surprisal *is* biasing seeded draws). It also closes prophecy into UNI-24's conservation frame with exact units — the future's optionality is entropy, a prophecy costs the bits it removes, and **overlapping prophecies compete for the same entropy**, so contradiction is detectable as a *budget overflow* before it becomes a narrative one. **Honest limit:** does not eliminate taste — B's value is still tasted — but decomposes one badly-posed taste problem into two well-posed ones, promoting the mechanism from taste-checked to calibration-checked | raw | med | ideonomy session 2026-07-16 |
| MAP-55 | **Coastline complexity as a culture driver, not a terrain stat — the thalassocracy thesis.** rift-and-fit measures coastline complexity (shoreline-development, coast-roughness) purely as an *effect* of terrain and checks it against a band; it drives *nothing*. But in every domain that has one, coastline complexity is a *cause*: it is habitat-edge (the littoral is the richest ecotone — more coast = more fishing subsistence, coastal endemism, littoral carrying-capacity), it is port geometry (indented coasts make harbors; smooth coasts don't), and — the load-bearing claim — it is the split between **maritime/fragmented/trading** polities and **continental/centralized** ones (Greece and Norway are seafaring *because* their coasts are fractal; the smooth African coast bred continental states). Re-instantiate coastline complexity as an *input* to demography and culture: a per-landmass (not whole-globe) coastal-complexity field feeding a littoral carrying-capacity bonus (MAP-7) and a maritime-vs-continental cultural axis. This is where the campaign's coastline work should have *led* — the number was never the point, the driver is. Needs per-landmass/per-region coastline measurement (not the single whole-globe scalar) | raw | high | ideonomy session 2026-07-16 (rift-and-fit G6); relates MAP-7, MAP-59 |
| MAP-56 | **Coastline provenance — every coast has an age, and rift-and-fit already drew it.** The fake rifting history (MAP-21) gives each margin a birth: a **conjugate rift scar** (old passive margin — should drown into a ria/embayed *complex* coast over deep time) versus an **active/subduction margin** (a *smooth* cliff coast). Today rift-and-fit throws this latent variable away by measuring one whole-globe average complexity. Read the provenance back instead: gate coastal *character* (ria/drowned vs cliff vs depositional-plain) on the drawn margin type and its breakup age, so a coast's shape *tells its history* and feeds MAP-55's culture split (old drowned coasts breed the maritime cultures; young cliff coasts don't). A free variable the campaign minted and discarded — the reconciliation with the plate-motion draws is the already-banked MAP-57 | raw | med | ideonomy session 2026-07-16 (rift-and-fit G6); relates MAP-21, MAP-57, MAP-55 |

Accepted incoherence recorded in the rift-and-fit spec §10 | raw | med | [rift-and-fit spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-16-rift-and-fit-design.md) |

| MAP-59 | Multi-scale coastline roughness as the PRIMARY coast measure — D measured at L4/L5/L6 gives a roughness *slope* (a fractal-dimension proxy, the honest form of Mandelbrot's "how long is the coast of Britain") immune to the single-hex alternation exploit the shoreline-development diagnostic exposed. **Shipped as the `coast-roughness-slope` companion metric in rift-and-fit.** The banked ambition (raw): *promote it from companion to headline and retire single-scale shoreline-development as the acceptance gate* — rift-and-fit proved single-scale D is near-blind to the large-scale conjugate geometry it was meant to reward and reachable to its legacy floor only by unphysical single-hex texture, so a scale-slope is the measure that actually distinguishes a fjord coast from a speckled one. The Earth-anchor evidence (Earth D=8.21 < the 9.51 legacy floor) is the case for demoting single-scale D to a mere sanity floor | raw | med | [rift-and-fit spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-16-rift-and-fit-design.md); [The Census of Coasts III](../laboratory/census-of-coasts-iii.md) |
| MAP-61 | The connection graph — society's transport topology. Social distance is *graph* distance, not Euclidean: nodes are places, edges are spatial adjacency + roads + sailing lanes + portals (a portal makes distant cells adjacent), so non-local coupling is native. Edges derived from the sandboxes below (currents→lanes, passes→roads) + built by society (a road = a baked-history event); conductance gated in time by climate (seasonal lanes), astronomy (intermittent portals), events (a curse closing a route), rarely *partitioning* the graph (isolation → divergence → rediscovery). Interactions split on the Fields/Facts seam: diffuse coupling (trade / culture / disease) = an order-independent fixed-point over the sparse graph; discrete coupling (raid / founding / plague) = events propagating with edge-specific lag. Coarse-global baked / fine-local lazy; Lorenz-safe. Triple-duty: dynamics substrate, discoverable legibility surface, player travel network. Diasporas & cultural similarity track graph-distance. Distinct from the relational [[SOC-9]]. **Slice 1 shipped** (campaign *The Connection Graph*, following *The Living Community* [[SOC-10]] C1): `domains/topology` (kernel-only) owns the graph type + pure reachability/least-cost ops (the kernel's deterministic `astar`); `windows/worldgen` derives it from real geography — bare adjacency, ocean-current sailing lanes, and bounded least-cost land corridors (a terrain traversal-cost field) — purely on demand, never committed (no epoch; every world stays byte-identical). Measured: on the icosphere, open ocean is the real hard separator, not elevation (a one-gap ridge does not isolate — a great-circle path dips through the gap "for free"; only genuinely impassable ocean does), and isolation is locally invisible — a hub can hold more local sailing-lane and land-route links than the world's best-connected capital while still being globally cut off from it, the structural precondition [[SOC-criticality]]'s divergence needs. Legible via the almanac/CLI `connections` verb and a seed-42 gallery page. **Deferred, by design, to later slices**: built roads (society's historical marks — needs history×graph co-evolution), portals (magic/astronomy-gated), conductance gating in time (seasonal lanes, intermittent portals), and diffuse coupling (trade/culture/disease as a fixed-point over the sparse graph). **Slice 2 shipped** (campaign *The Sundering*): the dynamics rewire — migration/raid/daughter-founding now follow a **time-varying** connection graph (one graph per era; a cell is ocean where its elevation lies below that era's sea level), so glacial low-stands open land bridges and the rising sea sunders the peoples who crossed. A genesis epoch (the graph stays derived, never committed, but the skeleton it drives moves). Measured: the graph works richly (11 of 12 seed-42 climate migrations cross era-only water; the glacial low-stand reconnects 755/843 refugia) and isolation genuinely diverges the peoples (four sea-bound landmasses over four peoples, three hosting only a proper subset — a people that could not cross is absent from that shore); but the diaspora *volume* is honestly modest — the old bake's larger count was inflated by unphysical ocean-walking, and the remaining lever is crowding/pressure, handed to [[SOC-criticality]]. Still deferred: built roads, portals, conductance gating, diffuse coupling. | elaborated (slice-2 shipped) | med | [The Connection Graph chronicle](../chronicle/the-connection-graph.md); [The Sundering chronicle](../chronicle/the-sundering.md); [frontier §connection-graph](frontier.md#the-connection-graph--societys-transport-topology) |
| MAP-impression | The impression — every group or individual that occupies or passes through a place leaves a trace, and the *set* of traces tells the story of who was here; the full generalisation of *The Living Community*'s minimal residue slice [[SOC-10]]. Four axes. (1) **Durability by material** — perishable (rag doll, wood, food, still-warm campfire ash: decades) / durable (potsherds, arrowheads, worked stone, bone, foundations, the ruin itself: millennia — Chaco, Mesa Verde, the tell of Jericho rebuilt at every level) / near-eternal (a magical sceptre, a dwarven vault: ~25k yr) — so an ancient ruin still yields durable finds while perishable finds survive only *recent* turnover. (2) **Character by species/culture** — elf near-invisible, orc burned forests + bone + ash + collapsed keeps, dwarf mines, human historical marks, fae nothing — the impression tells *who* passed through. (3) **Cause + geography shape it** — sacked keep vs abandoned hamlet vs plague-pit; mountain mine vs forest clearing (geographic determinism). (4) **The full trace range** — still-warm ash, a ruined castle, the scorched scattered skeleton of a dragon's last meal — not only settlements. NOT a dig-minigame: the traces are simply *there*, telling the story. The vivid perishable finds of sudden flight (a child's toy dropped as a family flees, never to return) couple with [[SOC-criticality]] conflict in a world of monstrous species contesting territory. Cross-links the species and culture domains. | raw | high | [The Living Community](../chronicle/the-living-community.md) (minimal slice shipped) |
| MAP-68 | The Overworld — rework the Orrery flat map's Pixel-art style into a procedural 16-bit RPG renderer: its own higher-res texture, a tight palette, ordered Bayer dithering, coastlines, biome outlines. **Keystones:** the map decomposes into seven components where the old had two; dithering is the hidden hub; determinism forbids hand-authored tiles, so the renderer emulates a pixel artist per output pixel. Fourth and last of the view-remake program ([[MAP-65]] → [[MAP-66]] → [[MAP-67]] → this), now **COMPLETE**. The look is not dialled in; the renderer merged as a foundation, tuning deferred | shipped | med | [The Overworld chronicle](../chronicle/the-overworld.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-21-the-overworld-design.md) |
| MAP-70 | The Excursion — real pan and zoom controls for the Orrery's flat Map rung, closing the in-map pan/zoom followup [[MAP-65]] left open. Panning mounts a same-face nine-tile neighbour ring with a two-radius hot/warm eviction policy. **Keystone:** mesh POSITION anchors to a stable origin tile fixed once per region visit, while ring MEMBERSHIP is a separate moving question keyed to the current centre — collapsing the two re-zeros the frame mid-drag. Orrery-only; cross-cube-face panning is out of scope, no adjacency remapping exists. The voxel seam its visual pass found is closed by [[MAP-71]] | shipped | med | [The Excursion chronicle](../chronicle/the-excursion.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-23-the-excursion-design.md) |
| MAP-71 | The Selvage — close the Orrery voxel Map rung's cross-tile seam [[MAP-70]] named. **The inherited diagnosis was half right, and the smaller half:** a visual pass *before* design found the band dominated by an inverted sign, not the missing wall — tile placement ran `+dy → −z` against the producer's `row → +z`. **Keystone:** the sign lived in three places, two open-coded inverses, so a one-character fix would have looked correct while the ring fetched tiles behind the travel direction. The residual wall is closed by an unconditional plinth — rebuild cadence, not triangle count, is the cost axis | shipped | med | [The Selvage chronicle](../chronicle/the-selvage.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-24-the-selvage-design.md) |
| MAP-69 | The vertical relationship — surface↔underground as its own future campaign, the richest the underworld ([[MAP-10]]) opens. Five deterministic, seed-derived mechanisms (sim-first: a mechanism whose emergent output *resembles* the trope, never an authored trope): **speciation-by-stratum** over deep time (Morlock/Eloi as one people diverged by surface-vs-depth refuge under selection, on [[MAP-22]]'s coexistence stack + [[MAP-11]]'s habitat medium); **over/under commerce** (dwarven trade — metals up, grain/timber down); **predation-inversion** (the driven-down refugee becomes the predator that hunts upward — a trophic-coupling flip, [[PSY-10]]); **chthonic emergence** (a people's *origin* subterranean — the deep as womb, not tomb); **subterranean valence** (one people's tomb is another's cathedral — [[LANG-36]]'s valence filter makes the underground culturally contested). Surfaced by The Deep's breadth atlas; gated on the ecology + features rungs of [[MAP-10]]. | raw | med | [frontier §underworld](frontier.md#the-underworld--the-vertical-dimension) |
| MAP-67 | The Diorama — a **Voxel-2.5D** style for the Orrery's flat MAP rung: the region as a flat heightfield of voxel blocks under a fixed-isometric orthographic camera, a tabletop relief model. **Keystone:** "2.5D" is defined by a non-zero camera PITCH under orthographic projection, and the voxel renderer is SCOPE-PARAMETRIC — [[MAP-66]] wrapped the same honest blocks on a sphere, this lays them on a flat grid. Reuse came from two extracted helpers, not a forced parameterization: the two top-face normals genuinely differ. Third of the view-remake program. Followups: camera orbit, 3D props | shipped | med | [The Diorama chronicle](../chronicle/the-diorama.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-21-the-diorama-design.md) |
| MAP-66 | The Massing — a switchable globe render-**style** layer for the Orrery, orthogonal to the data lens, plus a deeper-LOD ceiling: Smooth, Voxel, Terraced, Faceted. **Keystone:** "voxel" is defined by the exposed vertical inter-cell CLIFF — remove the walls and it collapses to low-poly — and an *honest* renderer draws one block per datum rather than inventing a sub-cell gradient the sim never computed. That dissolved the deeper-zoom ask into one campaign: voxel makes coarse resolution visible, so it needs deeper LOD to show. Perf was MEASURED, not guessed — YAGNI backed by a number | shipped | med | [The Massing chronicle](../chronicle/the-massing.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-21-the-massing-design.md) |
| MAP-65 | The Vantage — explicit 3-way view switching for the Orrery (System / Globe / Map), retiring [[MAP-62]] The Cartographer's zoom-driven crossfade handoff. Keystone (ideonomy abstraction-lift): a navigation system has two ORTHOGONAL axes — **which view** (discrete mode) and **how close within it** (continuous zoom) — and the zoom-handoff overloaded ONE input (the wheel) for both, coupling them (you couldn't study the globe up close without being flung to the map). **Decouple them: a dropdown owns view-selection; the wheel owns zoom** (clamped per view, no rung-crossing). The change REMOVED complexity — the dolly-limit detection + handoff intents deleted outright — a smaller codebase, more explicit. The `ZoomController` crossfade is kept but triggered explicitly. Map region = the globe's current center-facing point (orient the globe, pick Map, arrive where you were looking; a default tile if the globe was never opened). **Shipped 2026-07-21 (campaign *The Vantage*).** Orrery-only, no sim/wasm change. The FIRST of a four-campaign program to remake how the Orrery is seen: view-switching (this) → voxel globe + deeper LOD → voxel 2.5D map → pixel-art renderer rework (pixel-art is retired from the SPHERE — it fights the medium — but stays a map style to improve). Followups: ~~in-map pan/zoom~~ shipped by [[MAP-70]] The Excursion; Map URL-addressability (still open). | shipped | med | [The Vantage chronicle](../chronicle/the-vantage.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-21-the-vantage-design.md) |
| MAP-64 | The Freshwater — surface the inland water the sim already computes (`WaterKind`, waterfall sites) so the client can DRAW it. **Keystone (the autonomy axis):** the client renders emitted water, never derives it — re-deriving rivers from elevation would be non-authoritative and non-deterministic. Water is static per world, and river connectivity is implicit in cell adjacency; a vector flow-graph is deferred. Shipped additively — a pure provider read, no new seed draw, no epoch. The map colours the full network, the globe only major water, so the overview stays uncluttered | shipped | med | [The Freshwater chronicle](../chronicle/the-freshwater.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-21-the-freshwater-design.md) |
| MAP-62 | The Cartographer — the mark-owning cartographic renderer MAP-60 reserved, realized. **Keystone:** what separates a render *engine* from a filter is salience-budgeted feature selection per zoom — a filter reads finished pixels, an engine reads the scene datum and places symbols. Shipped **with a mid-campaign pivot that is the whole lesson:** pixel-art first shipped *on the globe*, then the visual pass found it fights a sphere, so it moved to a new flat map rung reached by zooming in — a discrete crossfade, not a geometric morph. **The visual pass is a design instrument, not just a bug-catcher** | shipped | med | [The Cartographer chronicle](../chronicle/the-cartographer.md); [transition spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-20-the-cartographer-transition-design.md) |
| MAP-63 | A seasonal-return prediction ladder — [[LANG-49]]/[[LANG-51]]'s falsification-and-revision mechanism (a naive extrapolated model, sometimes genuinely wrong, checked and revised), re-instantiated in climate rather than astronomy so it fires on worlds without eclipses or even moons (motivation: a much larger sample of worlds than the eclipse case reaches). Checked directly against the code before naming this "just a reuse": it is NOT. Two real, separately-sized pieces of new ground surfaced researching it. (1) **Physics**: today's seasonal temperature swing (`domains/climate/src/provider.rs`) is an EXACT sinusoid on a fixed `year_length_std` — no drift, so a naive model of it would never miss; weather (`domains/climate/src/weather.rs`, The Firmament) is pure Fbm noise with NO seasonal tie at all (`storm_propensity` is a static per-cell constant). Neither has eclipse's essential shape (almost-periodic, with real structured drift). A real fix ties storm propensity to the seasonal phase, reusing `PrecipRegime`'s existing SummerMax/WinterMax/Monsoon/Uniform classification and the same hemisphere-signed sinusoidal convention `temperature_at` already uses — genuinely scoped, but touches shipped weather generation (byte-identity impact on every Generated-sky, non-zero-obliquity world; likely needs a real epoch and a census regen). (2) **Fact-commit infrastructure**: `domains/climate` registers CONCEPTS (a culture can hold a word for "rain") but has NEVER committed a single FACT to the ledger the way astronomy's `moon-count`/`day-length-std` do — none of the Account/Observability/chorus machinery this idea needs exists for climate yet. Building this for real is climate's first-ever fact-commit infrastructure, comparable in size to LANG-49's own astronomy half, from scratch. Deliberately split out of LANG-49 rather than folded in (decision ledger #4) — sized as its own campaign, not a task | raw | med | LANG-49 brainstorm 2026-07-20, post-G3 (Nathan asked about wiring in weather prediction / rain dances; decision ledger #4) |
| MAP-60 | The Idioms — a swappable render-STYLE layer for the Orrery, orthogonal to the data lens: the lens chooses *what* is coloured, a style chooses *how* the world is drawn. Every pixel carries real sim meaning, so a style is a faithful re-documentation in another idiom, each implying a maker and era. Shipped: the `StylePipeline` plus four screen-space skins, each with one cheap data-aware hook. **The recurring lesson:** shader failures render a black globe invisibly to green tests, caught only by the visual pass. The mark-owning cartographic renderer, [[LANG-35]]'s self-drawing twin, stays open | shipped | med | [The Idioms chronicle](../chronicle/the-idioms.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-20-the-idioms-design.md) |

| LANG-2 | Non-vocal communication as a language-cluster boundary — telepathy (illithid), spore/chemical signalling (myconid), and gesture have no phonology to drift (MAP-3), no articulation envelope (EXP-7), and no L2-acquisition barrier, so LANG-1's pidgin/creole/lingua-franca mechanics — which exist *because* learners cannot reproduce a sound system — simply do not apply. The whole language program silently assumes articulated speech; a telepathic species marks the boundary of that assumption | raw        | med              | [frontier §map, item 3](frontier.md#the-frontier-map) |
| LANG-3 | Conversation and the bidirectional grammar — the content→render seam run in reverse, so comprehension and production are one grammar in two directions (Fluid Construction Grammar as precedent); NPC dialogue in the speaker's own tongue, the player an L2 learner inducing it from noisy input — tractable, because comprehension recognizes only what the grammar generates. Its two failure modes are the mechanics: an unknown token leaves a typed hole rendered as an echo question; a production gap becomes circumlocution. [[LANG-4]] supplied the closed concept layer; the grammar is unbuilt | **shipped (floor: The Echo)** | med              | this conversation; [frontier §map, item 3](frontier.md#the-frontier-map); [The Echo spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-the-echo-design.md); [The Echo chronicle](../chronicle/the-echo.md) |
| LANG-4 | The Words — kernel concept layer (ConceptDef beside PredicateDef, closed inventory, Swadesh packs with Berlin & Kay ladders keyed to perception vectors) + per-language lexicon with own-line etymology (proto-roots, drawn Neogrammarian cascade landing on the shipped phonology, compounds under drawn headedness), exposure-derived gaps with two provenances (experiential / perceptual), glossed proper names over site facts; name-collision posture ratified (0024): uniqueness is reference-time; the base rate was 4.91% at ratification, re-pinned at 4.70% after the merge's per-settlement observation vantages widened the descriptor space | shipped | high | [The Words spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-09-campaign-the-words-design.md); [decision 0024](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0024-settlement-name-uniqueness-is-reference-time.md) |
| LANG-5 | The Branches (L1) — the world's first language family: one authored **proto-goblinoid**, three daughters (goblin, hobgoblin, bugbear) descending through per-lineage regular Neogrammarian cascades that land on each daughter's articulation-vector-constrained present phonology, so cognates are true by construction (`*gʷat` → wat-/vad-/gwad-). The delta on LANG-4 lifts proto-roots from *per-species* to *per-family*; kobold is a family-of-one outgroup (byte-stable — the negative control); goblin's lexicon re-baselines under epoch discipline. Nativization added a second, measured divergence driver beyond the cascade (a daughter's own loudness-shaped inventory decides how much of the shared ancestor it keeps distinct); a seed-swept battery (1,000 seeds) confirms regularity, monophyly, and inventory closure with zero exceptions, and reports the clean outgroup (998/1,000) and the divergence ordering (holds in aggregate, not per-seed) exactly as measured. Adding two peoples also surfaced MAP-22 (a placement Pareto-dominance defect), fixed in-scope by a founder floor. The tree L1 plants; LANG-6/7/8 ride on it | shipped | high | [The Branches chronicle](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/book/src/chronicle/the-branches.md); [The Branches spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-09-the-branches-design.md) |
| LANG-6 | The Drift (L2) — differential lexical replacement: per-lineage, per-concept root innovation and loss at rates driven by concept properties (frequency, concreteness, perceptual salience), so cognate retention varies by concept and the world grows its own **Dolgopolsky list** (a replacement-stability ranking) as ground truth. Needs the LANG-5 tree first; feeds LANG-8 | raw | med | this conversation; [The Branches spec §8](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-09-the-branches-design.md) |
| LANG-7 | The Weave (L3) — contact and borrowing: neighbouring daughters admit loanwords and areal features (a Sprachbund), reticulating the LANG-5 tree; per-concept borrowing resistance grows the world's own **Leipzig-Jakarta list** (a loanword-resistance ranking) as ground truth. Rides on the adjacency-derived contact substrate LANG-1 describes (the exposure pattern); the wave to LANG-5's tree. **Doublets are the register payoff (2026-07-18):** a loanword accepted for a concept the borrower ALREADY holds breaks the one-concept-one-entry invariant deliberately — the native and borrowed words coexist as a register-split synonym pair (cleanse/purify, ask/inquire), each `LexEntry` tagged with its stratum (native vs. source-family), and VoiceParams' register selection finally has real lexical material to choose between: formal speech reaches for the borrowed stratum exactly as English reaches Latinate. The strata also hand [[LANG-8]]'s comparative method its hardest honest confound for free | raw | med | this conversation; [The Branches spec §8](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-09-the-branches-design.md) |
| LANG-8 | The Reckoning (L4) — the comparative method run blind on daughter wordlists: reconstruct proto-forms, the family tree, and the stability rankings from surface evidence alone, then **score against the ground truth** LANG-5/6/7 hold — the validation Earth linguistics can never run. The borrowing-as-confound instrument: turn contact intensity up and measure how far undetected loans degrade recovery of the true replacement ranking. The lexical sibling of MAP-4 (myth phylogenetics scored vs the true tree); later couples the language tree to the deep-time species phylogeny (gene-tree/language-tree, Cavalli-Sforza) | raw | med | this conversation; [The Branches spec §8](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-09-the-branches-design.md) |
| LANG-9 | Descriptor breadth for toponymy — the collision cure that needs no engine change: every substrate that adds per-place facts widens the site-concept space names draw from (local flora/fauna once a biosphere exists, historical events once deep time runs, founders once characters exist, terrain features once they carry identity), so meaningful names grow MORE unique as the world grows denser; the naming engine already consumes whatever site facts the composition root offers. Cross: MAP-3 (sound change differentiates collided names into distinct fossils over historic time), decision 0024 (reference-time uniqueness) | spec'd (The Wearing) | high | this conversation; [decision 0024](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0024-settlement-name-uniqueness-is-reference-time.md) |
| LANG-10 | Exonyms and endonyms — a settlement has as many names as there are languages that refer to it (Wien/Vienna/Bécs): the endonym is the current committed name; an exonym is the SAME site facts rendered through the naming language's own lexicon and phonology (a kobold exonym for a goblin town compounds kobold words, or descriptively names the inhabitants: "goblin-water-place"). Needs zero new engines — two lexica and cross-species kind-words shipped with The Words; cross-species name collisions vanish by construction since each language names in its own words. Feeds the epistemic layer (whose map are you reading?) and LANG-1's contact mechanics (an exonym is contact archaeology) | raw | high | this conversation; ideonomy session |
| LANG-11 | The name cycle — a toponym is a description slowly promoted into a pure identifier: baptism (transparent, glossable — The Words' shipped era) → conventionalization (referent drifts; the name goes quietly stale: Ice-Home after the ice melts — a stale name is the cheapest fossil the sim will mint, history made audible) → opacification (MAP-3 sound change grinds Ice-Home into Isham; gloss lost; two collided Ice-Homes diverge under local changes — the chronic cure for name collisions, vs. decision 0024's acute reference-time posture) → reanalysis/renewal (folk etymology reads false-but-meaningful parts back in — the COHERENT version of random-juxtaposition naming, since the misreading recounts to the reanalyzing language's own lexicon; or a renaming event — conquest, commemoration — restarts the cycle, negating "named once at genesis"). The shipped system is deliberately frozen at baptism; each later phase's machinery maps to an existing row (paleoclimate, MAP-3, MAP-9, LANG-1) | spec'd (The Wearing — the baptism→opacification step only; conventionalization, reanalysis and renaming stay deferred) | high | this conversation; ideonomy session; [The Wearing spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-27-the-wearing-design.md) §5 |

| LANG-13 | Contrastive vowel length (short/long) — the cheapest clean multiplier of the nucleus factor; renders in all three views | elaborated | high (mechanics) | this conversation; [frontier §channel-capacity](frontier.md#the-channel-capacity-frontier--enriching-a-constrained-phonology) |
| LANG-14 | Vowel nasalization as a tier — a lost nasal coda reborn as a nasal vowel (the historically real path tonogenesis currently folds into the Low bucket); velum-control body-plan capacity; espeak-weak | elaborated | high (mechanics) | this conversation; [frontier §channel-capacity](frontier.md#the-channel-capacity-frontier--enriching-a-constrained-phonology) |
| LANG-15 | Contour tones (rising/falling) — unbank the contour dimension over the shipped level tones; multiplies the tone factor for high-tonality species; IPA tone letters | elaborated | high (mechanics) | this conversation; [frontier §channel-capacity](frontier.md#the-channel-capacity-frontier--enriching-a-constrained-phonology) |

| LANG-32 | Phonotactic-repair collapse — the glossed-name epoch is phonetically inaudible for any species whose drawn synchronic templates reject its own evolved lexicon: seed 42's bugbear draws only 2-consonant onset templates, no CV-shaped word parses, repair deletes everything, and every glossed deity name lands on the identical minimal-syllable fallback ("Bvaash"; goblin collapses to "Neb", hobgoblin to "Fee" — 3 of 4 shipped species are in the pathological regime; kobold is the healthy control). Glosses stay truthful; surface distinctness is destroyed, and vocabulary growth cannot route around it (new words descend to the same rejected shapes — they mint homophones, not distinctness). The fix is upstream of the packs: draw phonotactics guaranteed to host the language's own word shapes (e.g. always admit one single-consonant onset), or land the sound-change cascade inside the templates rather than merely the inventory (the gap `naming.rs` already documents), or make repair substitute toward nearest-manner legal segments / derive its fallback from the input so distinct words collapse distinctly. Spec'd as The Speakable: an attested tier — the lexicon's own root forms admitted verbatim beside the drawn canon templates (descriptive phonotactics), making repair the identity for native compounds by construction; the draw-time and diachronic directions above stay live as root-cause remodels | shipped | high | [The Speakable spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-the-speakable-design.md) |

A drawn per-language **grammaticalization-depth** vector sets how much of each filter is *obligatory morphology* vs *optional lexical choice*: Common ≈ shallow (limited-English, so the floor stays simple), Draconic/Myconid deep (the worldview is in the verb — a Draconic that grammaticalizes evidentiality renders provenance morphologically, dissolving the need for the etic margin on provenance). **Coherence constraint (NOT independent draws):** animacy links the ontology-grammar and the causal-schema weights — a culture that grammatically animates rivers must also explain floods agentively (the [[LANG-37]] agentive schema and the noun-class are one worldview-fact at two layers). The good band = high-distinctive AND high-recoverable, confirmed independently by caricature (exaggerate-yet-recognizable) and translation (literal↔free). Value is single-peaked in distortion — which is *why* it felt like taste (an optimum with no obvious formula). **Auto-calibration:** distortion should track inverse-capability per domain, so a culture off that line is a DETECTABLE bug (a high-astronomy culture rendering the sky agentively is measurably over-distorted). **Population metric:** inter-account variance (six near-identical accounts = a vacuous filter stack, the uncanny failure at scale — the [[the-concordance-campaign]] anti-vacuity check lifted to the chorus). Moves the bet from taste-gated toward **self-scorable** (Lab-measurable; re-score on the Confidence Gradient per PROC-5). Unlike LANG-48, this has no ground-truth-checkable substrate to lean on today — "did this culture discover something true" has no ledger fact to verify against the way "is this ratio close to 2:1" does — so it is named as a direction, not designed, in the same spirit [[PSY-5]]'s ideonomic-cognition essay was captured before its own prerequisite (a psychology substrate) existed to make it buildable. (2) N-ary (three-body+) resonance chains — a genuine multi-way relationship real in our own solar system (the Galilean moons' 1:2:4 Laplace resonance); a materially harder detection problem than pairwise comparison (which pairwise ratios compose into a chain, and how many bodies before combinatorial checking gets expensive). (3) An event-coincidence framing — do two moons reach full phase together on a recurring cycle, building on the already-shipped eclipse/conjunction machinery, rather than comparing raw period VALUES; more "observational" in flavor (matches how real societies without arithmetic still tracked recurring sky-events) but requires simulating phase alignment over dated observation windows, not a pure derived-value comparison — a bigger lift than LANG-48's mechanism. A culture whose naive model enters crisis could, in a later campaign, revise not by re-averaging but by reaching for a witnessed small-integer ratio between the eclipse recurrence interval and some other already-witnessed period (a year, a second moon's cycle) — the same mechanism, reapplied to its own predictive failure rather than to two raw astronomical facts. Deferred deliberately — it wants the roster it will describe | raw | med | The Vigil brainstorm 2026-07-24 (owner direction) |
| LANG-54 | **Solitary transmission — mentorship as stemmatics** — [[BIO-37]] shipped a drift *rate* (`f(sociality × lifespan)`); this is the *channel* upstream of it, which the rate leaves unspecified. Four candidates make different predictions: **innate** (hatched knowing Draconic — one frozen tongue, changing only when the species does); **vertical-only** (parent to hatchling, one transmission per centuries-long generation — what the shipped rate implicitly models); **horizontal/contact** (learned from the peoples it bargains with — predicts *convergence and loanwords*, not isolation); and **none** (three idiolects, "Draconic" a scholar's fiction). Nathan's model is a fifth: **mentorship** — a sparse dyadic lineage graph, mentor to student, same species or not (a blue dragon mentored by a gold). Structurally this is manuscript **stemmatics**, not population drift: few copies, high per-copy fidelity, innovations propagating down one branch. Its distinctive prediction is that **divergence tracks mentorship distance, not species** — which is exactly what keeps one Draconic across a growing roster (metallics, further chromatics) without anyone authoring it that way; and if mentorship sorts by temperament, the natural cleavage becomes an **alignment register rather than a species dialect**, with cross-alignment mentorship the rare edge that keeps the registers from separating. Carries a sub-idea worth its own campaign: **taught vocabulary can exceed perceived vocabulary** — a mentor teaching a word for something the student cannot perceive (how humans hold "ultraviolet"), i.e. a fourth exposure path holding words *on authority*, bypassing `GapReason::Perceptual`; sibling of the existing `KnowsOf` class. Needs placed dragons before it has a graph | raw | med | [The Vigil chronicle](../chronicle/the-vigil.md); The Vigil brainstorm 2026-07-24 (owner direction) |
| LANG-55 | **The codomain subspace — insertion-stability by dense form-space, not by ordering** — The Accession (2026-07-27) makes proto-root assignment additive by ORDERING the domain: an authored registration `epoch` sorts new concepts strictly last, where the existing insertion-stability test already proves they displace nothing. Verified byte-identical, but it buys additivity by forfeiting Zipf — a future CORE (Swadesh) word gives up its short form to arrival order, because `epoch` outranks `core_rank`. This row is the alternative that dissolves that tension instead of trading it off, found by cross-domain re-instantiation from library classification: Dewey slots `573.2` between `573` and `574` and never renumbers a book, because the CODOMAIN is dense enough to admit insertion. Translated: reserve a phonotactic shape that epoch-0 roots cannot occupy and draw later coinages from it — a new concept then cannot collide with an old one at all, additivity holds by construction of the form space rather than of the assignment order, and core keeps its short forms. The cost is that newer words are audibly marked as newer, which is loanword and neologism phonology (English `-ology` coinages, Japanese katakana for loans) — realism, not a defect. A short step from existing machinery: `PROBE_BUDGET` (`etymology.rs:376`) already lengthens a candidate by a syllable when the base space saturates, so the code already knows how to move into another region of form-space. **Owner call 2026-07-27: wanted sooner rather than later, but explicitly not in The Accession.** Siblings on the same tree, for the record: freezing the assignment as an authored table is CLOSED BY CONSTRUCTION (`assign_proto_roots` takes `seed`, so there is no finite table — the one place decision 0009 cannot apply); salience/Zipf ranking is maximum fidelity and minimum stability; declared-epoch batching is the standing policy either way, since the goal is not zero churn but churn that is never a side effect | spec'd (The Wearing stage S1 — the prerequisite: ~20 new toponymic concepts would otherwise land strictly last and take the LONGEST forms, which is the exact opposite of that campaign's purpose) | high (mechanics) | The Accession spec §6; [The Wearing spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-27-the-wearing-design.md) §5 |

## The unification thesis

| ID     | Idea                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Status     | Conf          | Where                                                       |

| UNI-1  | Science, magic, theology, roguelike ID are one mechanic — inference over a hidden seeded ruleset. **The Surmise (2026-07-19)** ships this thesis's first concrete instance, at the agent scale: an NPC's belief about where a resource lies is a lazily-populated cache over the one true ledger, populated by perception (`windows/vessel`), and the planner plans over BELIEF, not truth. Truth held static this slice, so ignorance (never perceived) is the one demonstrated divergence; staleness (a changing truth) and deception (UNI-16's credulity threshold) are reserved as later, harder instances of the same inference mechanic — [The Surmise chronicle](../chronicle/the-surmise.md)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | elaborated | — (thesis)    | [frontier §unification](frontier.md#the-unification-thesis) |
