| UNI-4  | The undead as a metaphysical-ruleset output — whether death is animate is a magic-physics selection (UNI-2), so skeleton/ghost/lich/vampire are one ruleset's taxonomy, observed per-species (EXP-3) and coupled to the corpse-as-ur-pollutant purity grid (BIO-5) and funerary religion; a phenomenon a culture infers over, never a bestiary                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | raw        | med           | [frontier §unification](frontier.md#the-unification-thesis) |
| UNI-5  | Authored transmission media (the "stones") — comms/energy/transport as independent latent graph-construction systems with exact seeded laws, decoupling a modern affordance from its earthly substrate (no thermodynamics/info-theory/tech-tree); realized = pinned world-potential × craft fraction (TECH-1/TECH-2), raw ore emits diagnostic phenomena so discovery is UNI-1 inference perception-gated by EXP-3; optional, tunable, a Lab apparatus (MAP-1/TOOL-4) for counterfactual social history (MAP-9/MAP-7/SOC-1) — UNI-2's controlled science made concrete                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | elaborated | med           | [frontier §unification](frontier.md#the-unification-thesis) |
| UNI-6  | Place-bound afterlife civilizations — where the metaphysics animates the dead (UNI-4), a persistent ghost population is not merely a phenomenon the living infer over but a *second society* with its own accreting culture and identity, and the place is its body: the architecture is the substrate BIO-22 thought incorporeal species lacked (EXP-6), and the dead are the ultimate sessile people (BIO-18), bound to the structure. Demography inverts — its births are the living's deaths (recruitment from those who die in the place), so a castle that sees war and plague accretes deep death-cohort strata, the oldest ghosts the founder-elders (MEM-7 with the floating gap never closing: firsthand deep-time memory, the only culture that has it). The memory economy runs on masonry — melt is driven not by time (immortal witnesses do not forget) but by *substrate decay*, so a fallen wing is lost neighbourhoods and lost memory, MEM-4 preservation becomes keeping the castle standing, and the quick and the dead are an unwitting mutualism mediated by upkeep (MAP-7 founding→neglect→death, mirrored on the dead's side). The change-engine under perfect memory: the dead perceive the living only as confusing glimpses (broken perception, EXP-3), never their motives, and take in the present only through newly-arrived ghosts — themselves confused, terrified, inarticulate — so ghost culture drifts not by forgetting but by *compounding misapprehension*, an inverted knowledge gradient in which the dead know the ancient past firsthand and the present worst of all. "Home by the Sea." Metaphysics-gated (UNI-2/UNI-4) | raw        | med           | [frontier §unification](frontier.md#the-unification-thesis) |
| UNI-7  | Undead as a generative axis-space, not a bestiary — the death-animate ruleset (UNI-4) yields its taxonomy from a few axes: memory/selfhood retention (zombie ≈0 → revenant fixated → ghost/lich full), volition (mindless → willed), relation to the living (hazard / parasite / guardian / vengeance / veneration), binding (place-bound / mobile / diffuse), and the currency of its melt — masonry (ghost/substrate-decay), meaning (lich/purpose-drift), or commemoration (ancestor/the living's attention), the same MEM-1 melt in three currencies, with change under perfect memory driven by the lossy intake of the newly-dead. Each type is poignant read as the human process it literalizes, not a monster; the interesting ones are uncatalogued corners. Zombies are the retention-0 pole — an epidemic hazard field (BIO-5 disease-ecology + BIO-11 conversion), the anti-UNI-6, deliberately not a culture-bearer; mummies are MEM-4 preservation-as-craft turned militant, spending themselves to hold entropy (and grave-robbers) off a tomb. Observed per-species (EXP-3), inferred-over by the living (UNI-1)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | raw        | med           | [frontier §unification](frontier.md#the-unification-thesis) |
| UNI-8  | The vampire as a concealed extraction-ecology — a predator-society feeding on the living (BIO-14 life-force) and reproducing by converting prey into predators (BIO-11), so its ethics *are* its ecology: farm the host population gently or collapse it (a people-as-prey Lotka–Volterra with a conversion feedback). An extraction regime (MAP-9a) run under concealment (MAP-2), immortal and memory-deep (MEM-7); the recurring tragedy is intimacy with what one devours (Dracula, Near Dark, Sinners)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | raw        | med           | [frontier §unification](frontier.md#the-unification-thesis) |
| UNI-9  | The lich as an immortal institution — a mortal who chose undeath to carry a work no lifespan could finish: MEM-5's durable-surplus-commitment collapsed into one agent with a time-horizon (PSY) at infinity, deep-time ambition incarnate, hoarding knowledge the living cannot retain (a MEM-1 adversary). The poignancy is the work outliving its meaning — the lich forgetting why it began, or the world it served being gone; the decay is internal, a *purpose-melt* — infinite time-horizon but the terminal goal drifts under accreting instrumental sub-goals, obsession, and rabbit-holes, so it retains every fact except *why* and burns out having lost itself (MEM-1's melt relocated from fact to purpose)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | raw        | med           | [frontier §unification](frontier.md#the-unification-thesis) |
| UNI-10 | The venerated dead — the benign, integrated counterpart to UNI-6's haunting: the dead as a continuing part of the polity, consulted not exorcised, the ultimate gerontocracy (MEM-7 death-cohort strata as *authority*, SOC-1 legitimacy) and a deliberately-maintained archive (the ancestors *are* the memory, MEM-4). What a non-European-default world (PSY-3) produces where the West wrote horror; religion as ancestor-cult. Their upkeep is paid in the living's commemoration — named, tended, storied — and when a lineage forgets them they fade: the *second death*, dying when no one says your name. Intake is the newly-dead kin (as with ghosts) but two-way and ritually mediated, so far less garbled than a haunting                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | raw        | med           | [frontier §unification](frontier.md#the-unification-thesis) |
| UNI-11 | The revenant as the repressed ledger reasserting — a single wrong so salient it resists the memory economy's melt (MEM-3) and reanimates to demand redress: the *true* ledger returning against a chronicle that buried it (SOC-5 racecraft). Elevates the revenge-plot to a cultural symptom — places thick with revenants are places thick with unresolved, suppressed injustice; the return of the historically repressed, made animate                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | raw        | med           | [frontier §unification](frontier.md#the-unification-thesis) |
| UNI-12 | Monsters as the projection layer — the guiding principle behind the undead cluster (UNI-4/6/7/8–11) and beyond: a monster manifests, as an observable phenomenon, a culture's *latent* psychology — the anxieties, resentments, repressions, ambitions the physical ledger cannot show — unattributed to its source and often unrecognised by the culture itself (the revenant is its own buried guilt; the vampire its fear of a parasitic aristocracy; the zombie its dread of lost self). Sibling to religion (phenomena → gods) and twin to humor (EXP-4 benign violation ↔ horror malign violation, one expressive organ at two valences), reading from the psychology substrate (PSY) and widening the truth-vs-chronicle gap (SOC-5, MAP-2) with the racecraft shape: the anxiety must be *derived* (from subsistence stress, a repressed ledger-injustice, the psych vector), the projection mechanical, the culture's misreading the output. Completes UNI-1 — a culture inferring over its monsters, gods, and prophecies (MAP-13) is unknowingly reading its own interior                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | elaborated | — (principle) | [frontier §projection](frontier.md#the-projection-layer)    |
| UNI-13 | The necropolis palimpsest — the dead as a parallel, place-bound society that fossilizes the living world's contact history (MAP-9 inverted). As a castle changes hands over centuries its ghost strata go multi-species and multi-era — human, orc conquerors, human, goblin — cohabiting by *forced proximity* (you cannot choose your afterlife neighbours, and they include your ancestral enemies), stratified by death-cohort *and* origin-culture, each with its own psych/perception/tongue. Their internal politics encode the living history's conquests and massacres (UNI-11 revenant-logic at population scale), so a region's undead composition is a fossil of its violent past and an *incorruptible witness the living chronicle cannot silence* (SOC-5/MAP-2/MEM) — until the substrate decays and takes them (renovation evicts the founding dead). Over deep time the strata may syncretize under UNI-6's lossy intake into an emergent posthumous culture no living people ever had                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | raw        | med           | [frontier §unification](frontier.md#the-unification-thesis) |
| UNI-14 | The undead ruleset is species-agnostic, and the type governs how much origin-species survives — zombie goblins and vampire duergar are valid, and retention (UNI-7's axis) doubles as a *species-erasure* axis: at the zombie pole selfhood *and* species dissolve (a zombie goblin ≈ a zombie elf; the plague converts *across* species, crossing the barrier a normal disease (BIO-5) respects — a pan-species horde, the ultimate leveling), while ghosts/vampires/liches keep their origin-culture whole. Cross-species predation is then legible: a vampire clan feeding on *another* species' living is an extraction regime with a species boundary (SOC-5/MAP-9a), and predator-clans of different origin compete for a shared prey base (MAP-9). Mixed types layer without competing, occupying different binding-roles (place-bound / mobile / diffuse)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | raw        | med           | [frontier §unification](frontier.md#the-unification-thesis) |
| UNI-15 | Undead gating discipline — the guardrail over the whole undead cluster (UNI-4/6–14): the metaphysics *permits* undeath (UNI-2/UNI-4) but instantiation is sparse, on-demand, and bounded, so it stays rare, salient, deterministic, and never dominates demography (MAP-7). Each type has a *derived ignition condition* — a triggering tension already in the ledger (revenant ← a buried injustice, UNI-11; ghost-civ ← death-density × persistence; plague ← an outbreak threshold; lich ← a willed choice) — so absent the wound, no undead (the projection-layer derive-don't-author logic). Each has a *sink* (substrate decay UNI-6, purpose-melt UNI-9, commemoration-lapse UNI-10, redress UNI-11/MAP-9e, destruction by the living), so the *stock* is a source-vs-sink flow balance — a field with a computable equilibrium, invoked only where salient (the MAP-15 restraint). Being a bounded, sourced-and-sunk stock is exactly what makes it a Lab object — the undead-cluster studies promoted to TOOL-8–11 (density vs. violence history, palimpsest strata vs. conquest sequence, the roster-vs-chronicle atrocity gap, the pan-species plague curve), plus prevalence vs. the death-animation pin as a TOOL-4 sweep                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | raw        | med           | [frontier §unification](frontier.md#the-unification-thesis) |
| UNI-16 | The immune model of belief — the belief tier of UNI-1 (and MAP-2's epistemic layer) re-instantiated as adaptive-immune self/non-self discrimination. Belief is a per-agent *threshold*, not a store (signal-detection): too credulous believes lies/illusions/forgeries, too skeptical is autoimmune paranoia that rejects truth — the gullible and paranoid NPC are one mechanism at two settings. Deception needs no second ledger (the matrix verdict): every trick is a *real event* spoofing perception (mimicry = disguise, illusion, forgery) or suppressing inference (charm = immunosuppression), so the false belief is *derived* with causal-chain provenance. The whole canon maps: central tolerance = unquestioned culture/species priors (the best lies hide there; deception is culturally relative), immune memory = learned skepticism, antigenic variation = the disguise arms race + detection half-life, adjuvant = fear/authority/repetition, vaccination = prebunking, herd immunity = belief-contagion (SIR/R0, shares TOOL-11's plague math). Pathologies are the richest: cancer = the trusted insider/mole (undetectable because inside tolerance — betrayal), cytokine storm = witch-hunt/moral-panic as social autoimmunity (MAP-9b), immune privilege = taboo/sacred cows, hygiene hypothesis = naive populations are fragile, exhaustion = the firehose-of-falsehood. Threshold derived from culture/species/events/MEM-1, never authored per-NPC (the anti-lookup-table discipline, 0021). **The Surmise (2026-07-19)** shipped this row's L0/L1 substrate (perception + belief-as-a-cache over the ledger, `windows/vessel`) without yet touching this row's own mechanic — no second channel, no threshold; belief still has exactly one source (the agent's own perception). The credulity threshold this row names is reserved as the next campaign, **The Discernment** | elaborated | high (mechanics) | [frontier §immune](frontier.md#the-immune-model-of-belief--deception-as-discrimination-failure) |
| UNI-17 | The immune stack — UNI-16 lifted: belief-immunity is one instance of a general boundary-maintenance / identity-defense mechanism (discriminate self/non-self, learn, remember, fail on two sides) that Hornvale runs at *every* scale — body (BIO-5), self/belief (UNI-16), culture (heresy = autoimmunity, orthodoxy = self-marker, conversion = infection, assimilation = tolerance induction, initiation = thymic training), settlement (xenophobia = transplant rejection), language (purism = inflammation vs loanword-antigens). Three axes tune each scale: rate (innate reflex → adaptive learning; moral panic = fast cultural autoimmunity), naturalness (reflex → engineered immune organ: censor/Inquisition/school), distribution (central Ministry-of-Truth → herd/gossip, a political-structure axis). Border-crossers are the drama: heretic, convert, spy, immigrant, traitor, loanword. Two refinements: the *danger model* (reject the harmful, not the foreign — Matzinger; threshold keyed to perceived harm, not novelty) and *commensal tolerance* (the noble lie / load-bearing myth as beneficial non-self the identity hosts — money, nation, rights; a people is a holobiont of truths + useful fictions; threads to the projection layer + religion). One threshold across deception, xenophobia, heresy, conversion, purism, immigration; links culture/religion/language/MAP-9/BIO-5, sits over UNI-16 | elaborated | high (framing) | [frontier §immune-stack](frontier.md#the-immune-stack--boundary-maintenance-across-every-scale) |
| UNI-18 | The transmigrating soul — the diegetic reading of the game's possession primitive (the-walk metaplan §5.1): player control as a *soul* that reincarnates through vessels, death a transition not a terminus, control immortal while vessels die. Makes the deferred diegetic-possession option (metaplan §3.5) literal metaphysics: the PC is a rider/soul, re-drop is transmigration, and a *ghost* is control+identity persisting while perception+control unbind from a living vessel — tying the possession machine to the undead cluster (UNI-4/9–14), religion (afterlife, souls), and the projection layer. Possession decomposes into four independent bindings (perceive/control/know/identify) whose combinations are play modes (full, amnesia, scrying, divine command, charm). Metaphysics-gated (UNI-2) | raw | med | [frontier §unification](frontier.md#the-unification-thesis) |
| UNI-19 | The planning engine (A* across the tenses) — abstract GOAP (PSY-6) and it's A*: least-cost path through a state space, and the same deterministic search (start→goal, seed-tiebroken) is causality in every tense. BACKWARD = confabulation (goal = present facts, operators = events, cost = implausibility; the-walk metaplan Campaign IV). SPATIAL = navigation / roads / trade routes / migration / drainage over terrain. FORWARD = NPC goals + prophecy (fate = forward A* toward the foretold outcome; MAP-15's "not too blatant" plausibility floor IS the A* cost). Also: tech tree = planning over prerequisites; etymology / comparative method (LANG-8) = least-cost proto-tree; abduction (UNI-1 explanatory pole) = least-cost hypothesis. The action-half complementing UNI-1's inference-half — one active-inference loop (perceive = fit model to world; plan = fit world to model). A* is the rare natively-deterministic game-AI primitive, so one small kernel planner serves navigation, history, prophecy, tech, reconstruction, each with its own state space + cost. Negations (PSY-6 failure modes) are the drama. **The Foresight (2026-07-18) shipped the kernel engine itself, not just the framing**: `hornvale_kernel::astar` over a `SearchSpace` trait (`State: Ord`, `successors`, `goal`, `heuristic`) — a pure, general, tense-agnostic least-cost graph search, with `BTreeMap`/`BTreeSet` open/closed sets and a TOTAL order over `(f-cost, g-cost, state)` as the tie-break (no HashMap, no RNG), proving the PATH-DETERMINISM keystone this row's "natively-deterministic" claim asserted: same successors/goal/heuristic → byte-identical path, even across planted equal-cost ties. PSY-6's GOAP is the engine's FIRST instantiation (`GoapSpace` over `PlanState{position, hydrated}`); the engine itself knows nothing of thirst, rooms, or forward time — those are GOAP's own semantics, supplied by the caller. The other tenses this row named (BACKWARD confabulation, SPATIAL navigation, FORWARD prophecy) remain unbuilt, now literally one more `SearchSpace` impl away rather than a hypothetical reuse | elaborated | high (framing) | [frontier §planning-engine](frontier.md#the-planning-engine--a-star-as-causality-across-the-tenses) |
| UNI-20 | The derived-view architecture — the synthesis of the-walk brainstorm: eight design passes from different angles kept collapsing to one shape. The game layer is ONE event ledger (the only stored truth; the keystone, metaplan §3.2 / §3.4) + a small deterministic core of readouts and engines. VIEWS (lossy reductions of the ledger): belief (UNI-16 — perceived events projected), the social graph (SOC-9 — events between a pair → an edge), emotion (PSY-7 — appraisal of the gap), salient absence (RENDER-7 — prior minus ledger), history (events + confabulated backfill). ENGINES (two faces of one active-inference loop): inference (UNI-1 — fit model to world) and planning (UNI-19 — A* / GOAP — fit world to model: motivation PSY-6, verbs MAP-27 as one player+NPC action system, causality in every tense). Consequences: determinism all the way up (surprise is epistemic), one machine many users (game AI = the core the player inhabits), cheap + provenanced (nothing stored that re-derives; `why?` always answers). The game-layer analogue of 'coarse constrains fine' — agility from there being LESS here than it looks. **The Quickening (2026-07-18)** is this architecture's first instance actually running, not just designed: a derived NPC's position is exactly a VIEW — the latest committed `agent-at` event, else the derived schedule (c5's latest-wins pattern, `kind_of`, applied here to a game-layer position predicate for the first time) — over the one session-owned event ledger, with `why` (the historiography window, unmodified in shape) recounting it. Only the discrete divergence commits; the smooth routine stays derived, never stored — the seam rule this row's "nothing stored that re-derives" already named, now with a real committed event on the other side of it. **The Wanting (2026-07-18)** adds a second instance in the same session, one layer deeper: an NPC's homeostatic DRIVE is itself a VIEW — a pure fold over that same committed `agent-at` history, never stored (drive = fold = belief = index, all UNI-20 derived views over the one ledger, event-sourcing's state-is-a-fold-over-the-log made concrete a third way) — and the drive's own decision seam reserves a `world_view` parameter as the exact slot UNI-16's belief later fills, so PSY-6's decisive "plan over belief, not truth" move lands as a body change to an existing view, not a new seam. Cycle-03 of the room-scale corpus (inhabitants as a derived view over the one ledger) is a spatial/ecological elaboration of this architecture ([cycle-03](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/design/room-scale/cycle-03-the-living-layer.md)). **The Surmise (2026-07-19)** adds a third instance, one layer deeper again: an NPC's BELIEF about where a resource lies is itself a VIEW — a pure fold over that same committed `agent-at` history, gated by (now fresh-water) truth, nearest-known-of-several — never a second ledger (belief = fold = drive = index, all UNI-20 derived views over the one ledger). The Wanting's reserved `view` seam (decision #9) took its first real body-swap here, from ground truth to belief, at zero save-format cost | elaborated | high (framing) | [frontier §derived-view](frontier.md#the-derived-view-architecture--the-game-layer-as-readouts-over-one-ledger) |
| UNI-21 | The self-reflective ledger (capability schema as a derived view) — a triple store's schema is itself data, so the system's own vocabulary — predicates, entity attributes, fields, phenomenon kinds, verbs/reactions (MAP-27), providers (which domain answers which query at which tier) — is queryable in the same shape by the same engine. NOT the development process (no wall-clock / commits — constitutional): the *compiled capability set*, DERIVED at load from the concept registry + composition-root providers, never in the world save (build-state, not world-state; no cross-version hazard). Reflection = another derived view, with a load-bearing use: GOAP's available-action set (PSY-6) IS a capability query; the manual concept-registry review (orphan predicates, unconsumed phenomena) becomes a self-query. Meta-circular payoff: the engine's self-model and an in-world sage's model of the world's laws are the same query over facts-about-the-ruleset at different vantages (UNI-1 inference over the hidden ruleset; MAP-18 calendrics; MAP-19 commonsense ruleset) — the derived-view architecture (UNI-20) turned reflexive. Cheapest instance already exists: `hornvale concepts` (the registry dump). Implicit, not stored | elaborated | high (framing) | [frontier §self-reflective](frontier.md#the-self-reflective-ledger--the-capability-schema-as-a-derived-view) |
| UNI-22 | The entity-component substrate — `SpeciesDef` is a god-struct fusing peoples-only concerns (psychology, perception, phonology, social lexicon, family proto) with biosphere traits (mass, resource niche, condition niche, potency); a dragon or a fungus needs only the biosphere subset. Restructure into components grouped by **domain**, keyed by `EntityId`, optional per entity, composed only at worldgen — an entity *is* the set of components it carries (a culture = no body; a deity = biosphere + religion; an awakened beast = biosphere + nascent psyche; border-zone entities a fat struct can't express). NOT a foreign ECS bolted on: the entity-space instance of the constitution's Fact/Field/Phenomenon trace protocol, and a convergence of four things Hornvale already runs — the append-only Fact ledger, the derived-view query engine (UNI-20/UNI-21; "entities with component X" IS a tuple query), the chunk-partitioned room ledger (decision 0037; the LSM idea at small scale, disk/compaction deferred), and the component split The Niche forced. Migrated **strangler-fig** across ~7 shippable campaigns: biosphere/peopled split (unblocks the menagerie) → `EntityId`-keyed biosphere registry → domain-owned component registries → the query engine → instance⋈ledger interop → systems & schedule → spatial partition. Firm choices: determinism > speed (BTreeMap, single-threaded sim), data-not-code (0011), open extension (add a domain, edit nothing), in-memory until measured scale demands the LSM (0037). Surfaced by The Niche's menagerie forcing a fungus to carry a proto-language. **Program metaplan APPROVED** (2026-07-15): the design converged over six ideonomy drills + a pressure-test onto one lens — every boundary is a *rate* boundary (the constitution's build-state/world-state/derived-view split applied to entities) — settling `KindId`-as-label (not position), `ComponentStore` = generalized `CellMap`, Facts-vs-registries = the build/world line, the query engine as SPO/PSO/OSP triple-store indexes, the schedule as a derived view over the capability schema, direction-split + bulk-synchronous-tick concurrency (the deterministic form of agent-based modelling), and two determinism contracts (serialized-kind-ref = label + epoch; single-writer-per-functional-predicate). **Campaign 1 SHIPPED** as *The Menagerie* (2026-07-15): `SpeciesDef` split into a universal `BiosphereTraits` + `Option<PeopledTraits>`, the 12-creature canonical menagerie authored biosphere-only (the first non-peopled entities), genesis cut over onto the competitive niche-K stack (peoples' 2-way biogeography now committed). Honest finding: fauna *resource*-strongholds need per-axis **spatial** resource fields (the single-NPP-supply limit), deferred as a named increment. **Campaign 2 SHIPPED** as *The True Name* (2026-07-15): `KindId` — a kind's authored label made a typed, save-format-contract-bound identity in the kernel (beside `EntityId`); the species registry keyed by it; the packer's positional `u32` demoted to a documented build-local dense index (never serialized); byte-identical. Foundation for campaign 3's domain-owned component registries. **Campaign 3 SHIPPED** as *The Dissolution* (2026-07-16): `SpeciesDef`/`PeopledTraits`/the monolithic `registry()` deleted — a kind is now a **set of components** keyed by `KindId`, each authored and owned by the one domain that presents it (`species` owns biosphere + psyche + perception; `language` owns articulation + lexicon + proto — its types moved bodily out of `species`), composed only at worldgen, which assembles a kind from whatever components carry its label. The kernel gained `ComponentStore<K, C>` (the generalized `CellMap`) + an open (unsealed) `Component` marker; the old `Option<PeopledTraits>` all-or-nothing became a worldgen **referential-integrity check** (peopled cluster key-set + biosphere row + multi-member-family proto, load-time, fails loudly); genesis relocated to the composition root reading the component set. The no-domain→domain layering is now **enforced by construction** — `species` cannot read `language`'s speech because it cannot depend on it. Byte-identical throughout. The design surprise, folded in: the roster *is* a component-set (synthetic Lab rosters forced it), more faithful than the canonical-lookup the plan assumed. **Campaign 4 SHIPPED** as *The Concordance* (2026-07-17): the flat `Vec<Fact>` ledger gained three triple-store permutation indexes (`SPO`/`PSO`/`OSP`) + compiler-style predicate interning, entirely as `#[serde(skip)]` derived views — `commit`'s two linear scans (idempotency + contradiction) collapse to one prefix lookup, so world-build drops from `O(n²)` to `O(n log n)`; postings are ascending fact positions, so every query preserves commit order and every artifact stays byte-identical. The keystone is the INDEX≡SCAN property (index provably equals the naive scan over random ledgers) — which the whole-branch review sharpened by catching that an Entity-only generator had exempted the one value where `total_cmp` and IEEE equality disagree, signed zero (fixed in the index key alone, save untouched). Plus a `kinds_with(component)` reflection query (UNI-21's capability read). **Campaign 5 SHIPPED** as *The Individuation* (2026-07-17): the instance half of the substrate — a canonical `instance-of` kernel predicate (non-functional, latest-wins `kind_of`, `mint_instance` as sole writer) gives every entity a day-stamped, provenanced kind history; a per-call derived lens (`instance_biosphere`) answers an instance's effective traits as its own override facts (mass, potency) else its kind's authored registry default, overrides surviving a kind change by construction; three new body-less kinds (deity, culture, material) prove the component-set matrix, and `awakened-owlbear` (biosphere + the full peopled cluster, reached by transition) proves the kind ⋈ instance join in a `from_stores` test roster; the canonical kind roster generalizes to the union of every component store's key-set. Kept strictly in **shadow** at ship time: genesis minted zero instances, every shipped-world ledger byte-identical, only registry rows drifted. (Shadow since ended by [The Predicates & the Grammar](../chronicle/the-predicates-and-the-grammar.md): genesis now mints one `instance-of` collective per placed people, named by the autonym; decision 0062 ratifies the permanent is-a/instance-of split.) The keystone "mighty things in the cold north" query (Drill 4) ships as an integration test over the c4 query engine's OSP index. **Campaign 6 SHIPPED** as *The Ordination* (2026-07-17): the order half of the substrate — a kernel `System` declaration (stable label + declared read/write predicate sets) and a `CapabilitySchema::schedule()` that derives a topological order (Kahn's algorithm, ties broken by ascending label, never registration position) over the read/write dependency DAG; the keystone is **SCHEDULE ≡ HAND-ORDER**, not literal sequence equality but that the genesis pipeline's hand-written `build_to` order is a valid linearization of the same declared DAG the schedule derives, proved with an anti-vacuity negative (a dependency-violating order is rejected, guarding against under-declared edges passing silently); the bulk-synchronous tick mechanism (decision-ledger #40) made concrete as kernel types (`TickSystem` + `tick`, frozen-snapshot reads, stable-label commit order) and proven by a toy system, with genesis reframed as that cycle's once-run tick zero; the single-writer-per-functional-predicate contract (metaplan §7) built and **measured** against the real genesis declarations (not assumed-green), which surfaced a genuine finding — `name-gloss` written by both `settlement` and `religion`, resolved by moving it to the kernel as shared kernel-core infrastructure (beside `name`/`instance-of`) with an explicit exemption list, safe because the ledger's own per-(subject, predicate) commit-time invariant already covers what the coarser per-predicate schema check cannot see, and because kernel-core predicates are high-connectivity hubs the single-writer contract was never shaped for. Byte-identical throughout — no new fact, no new predicate (`name-gloss` moved, not added), the schedule and tick mechanism are pure derived views, never serialized. Kept strictly in shadow: genesis still executes via `build_to`, not `tick`; the running liveness tick (ticks 1..N — GOAP/belief/social systems generating new sim-time facts) is deferred to a future arc. **Campaign 7 (spatial partition) is the last substrate campaign in this program.** Ahead: spatial partition (7) | elaborated | high | [ECS program metaplan](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-ecs-program-metaplan-design.md); [The Menagerie chronicle](../chronicle/the-menagerie.md); [The Dissolution chronicle](../chronicle/the-dissolution.md); [The Concordance chronicle](../chronicle/the-concordance.md); [The Individuation chronicle](../chronicle/the-individuation.md); [The Ordination chronicle](../chronicle/the-ordination.md); [decision 0037](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0037-the-room-tier-ledger-is-chunk-partitioned.md) |
| UNI-23 | **Magic as a graph grammar** — the missing answer to "what is a *rule*" in UNI-2's second physics. **The Fact ledger already IS the graph** (subject/predicate/object = an edge-labelled digraph), so a production is a **triple-pattern match** (preconditions) plus an **append** (effects), and the matcher is UNI-22's approved SPO/PSO/OSP query engine — no new storage, no new addressing, just a rule table. **A spell is a *derivation*, not a production**: primitives compose, so a finite drawn table yields an unbounded spell space, research is proof search, a grimoire is a lemma cache, and **spell level = derivation depth** (formal, unauthored, unbalanced-by-hand). The catalog is **authored and committed** — decision [0009](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0009-models-author-dice-roll.md) names "expert-system rule bases" verbatim — and the world **draws a subset + parameters**; a freely-drawn grammar would generate semantic noise, buying combinatorial size at the cost of meaning. Addressing is **sympathetic** (similarity = trait-vector proximity; contagion = reachability through the ledger's past, free because append-only) plus a third mode, **presence** (EXP-3-gated direct awareness — what psionics uses, which is why it feels categorically different without being different physics). Effects are **licensed violations read off the property batteries**: every invariant the sim enforces is a candidate magic, so the catalog is meaningful *and* checkable by construction and **grows with the sim's fidelity** (UNI-21's shape). Sourcing discipline: Frazer's two laws as **cognitive** primitives (Rozin & Nemeroff), never his discredited evolutionist ladder; Evans-Pritchard marks the limit (magic-as-explanation ≠ magic-as-technology; both must exist). Realizes UNI-2; gated by DOM-17, downstream of UNI-22's query engine | elaborated | med | [frontier §magic](frontier.md#magic-as-a-graph-grammar--the-debt-economy) |
| UNI-24 | **Conservation, incidence, and the scar** — *the debt is not the environmental cost*. Conservation (a production takes from somewhere) is the candidate universal; **incidence** (who bears it) is drawn, and the land is one of five (self / land / others / the future / the dead) — a self-incidence world has burnout mages and no defiling at all. **The scar is universal; where it lands is drawn**: one parameter, five recognisable worlds. Conservation forces **source = incidence** (you cannot take from one place and charge another) with one exception, a flux network (MAP-53). **The debt is the diagnostic phenomenon**, the cluster's best result: ex-nihilo magic leaves no residue and is therefore *undiscoverable*, while conservative magic leaves a scar that *is* the evidence cultures infer over — so the property that makes magic **checkable for us** is the property that makes it **discoverable for them**, and verification surface and in-world epistemology collapse to one axis (the governing principle stops being methodology and becomes a fact about the fiction). The dial therefore selects **what kind of civilisation can know things**: conservative → residue → inference → mages, schools, experiment; ex-nihilo → nothing to reason from → magic can only be *received* → priests and no mages. UNI-1's science/magic/theology trio as one scalar. Answers "environmental cost must not be in every world" without losing discoverability | elaborated | med | [frontier §magic](frontier.md#magic-as-a-graph-grammar--the-debt-economy) |
| UNI-25 | **Stock, flow, and posture** — every incidence offers two things to take, and the choice is a magic system's entire moral structure asked five times: *the interest, or the principal?* **Druid and defiler run the same production against the same land; the only difference is rate**, measurable against the shipped NPP field (a druid's cap is the regeneration rate; a defiler eats the principal) — so patience-versus-urgency is derived, and the necromantic line (a renewable corpse vs a non-regrowing soul) falls where fiction always drew it. **Two reversibilities**: grammar-invertibility governs whether a *spell* undoes (dispel); whether the *world* restores is a property of **rates** — accumulation is regeneration-bounded, liquidation is not — so **a defiled world never recovers even if every production in its grammar inverts**. The asymmetry is thermodynamic, not metaphysical: the dispel works, the desert stays. Lifted, traditions are not classes but **postures toward a balance sheet** (savings / income / liquidation / self-employment / credit / balloon-credit / theft / salvage / distributed accounts), and the **empty cells predict rather than accommodate**: *mutualisation* = the **coven** (insurance pools *variance*, not mean — hence a circle, hence oaths against free-riders, size bounded by `in_group_radius`); *taxation* = the **templar**, with the Sorcerer-King as **issuer, not tyrant** (he aggregates capacity and lends it — and if he has issued more than he holds, a test of his true capacity is a **magical bank run** that fails every templar at once); *default* = **corruption / the lich**, a margin call whose collateral was you. Complements the already-banked UNI-8 (vampire-as-extraction-ecology) and UNI-9 (lich-as-immortal-institution) rather than restating them | elaborated | med | [frontier §magic](frontier.md#magic-as-a-graph-grammar--the-debt-economy) |
| UNI-26 | **Correspondence is universal; whether the world honours it is drawn** — the two sympathetic laws are facts about *minds*, not about worlds (Rozin & Nemeroff find them live in modern subjects who believe none of it), so every world's peoples reason by them. **Superstition is the universal; magic is the special case where superstition happens to be right.** The consequence is what earns the cluster its cost at *zero* fidelity: a world with an **empty violation catalog** still derives correspondence-thinking, false causes, relic-veneration, rival schools and lost arts — so the "magic system" is really the **culture-and-knowledge engine**, and magic is one setting of it. **Earth is a draw, not an absence**: similarity is honoured in heredity ("like begets like" *is* the law of similarity, and it is correct — breeding is sympathetic magic that happens to work) and denied in effigies; contagion is honoured in pathogens and denied in essence. That the intuitions are right often enough to be worth having is *why they persist* — and it puts the mundane tier within reach of real ethnographic and psychological data, i.e. **calibration-checked rather than taste-checked**. The universal/drawn sort is UNI-1 and UNI-2 restated: the *mechanic* is universal, the *ruleset* is seeded | elaborated | med | [frontier §magic](frontier.md#magic-as-a-graph-grammar--the-debt-economy) |
| UNI-27 | **The epistemic organ has two faces** — historiography and prophecy are one organ performing **dual operations on one lattice**: historiography **computes meets** (given residue, the most specific past consistent with all evidence; more evidence → tighter meet — archaeology as a greatest lower bound), prophecy **computes joins** (a node all branches reach; "inescapable" = a join at the top of the lattice; fate-steering = *forcing* a join, and MAP-54's budget is what forcing costs — restating MAP-14's rigidity as a lattice property). **In Hornvale the symmetry is exact**: the world is a pure function of the seed, so past and future are *both* chains ontologically and the lattice is **purely epistemic** — the two faces differ only in what evidence an inside observer has (**residue** backward, **dynamics** forward), which is why forward inference must fall back on extrapolation exactly as the prophecy section already insists. Hence **a scar is residue, so researching magic *is* doing history** — the mage inferring what was cast in a blighted land and the historian inferring what happened in a ruin run the same operation. UNI-1's list does not gain historiography as a fifth member: **historiography *is* the mechanic**, and science / magic / theology / identification are it pointed at different residues. `windows/historiography` is already domain-agnostic ("a new domain's facts are recounted the day they are committed") and would recount a spell's provenance for free | raw | med | ideonomy session 2026-07-16; [frontier §magic](frontier.md#magic-as-a-graph-grammar--the-debt-economy) |
| UNI-31 | **Personhood is a region of component-space, not a node.** One inheritance tier survived the entity-component substrate: worldgen's all-or-none peopled cluster welded has-a-mind onto is-a-settling-people. A dragon breaks it — sapient yet solitary: the line is communal-vs-alone. The repair makes sociality a first-class axis. Shipped as The Eremite (0065): `SocialForm` plus the lattice `speech ⊆ perception ⊆ mind`. The program has since shipped its language half (0066), the mind/society split (0067) and dragon perception. Next: placement via [[BIO-35]]'s prey field | shipped | high | [The Eremite chronicle](../chronicle/the-eremite.md); [The Eremite spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-22-the-eremite-design.md); [The Vigil spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-24-the-vigil-design.md) |
| UNI-32 | **Presence induces forward-integration** — the game's central thesis. Unobserved, the world is a statistical *prior*: drives, weather, an NPC's aggression are sampled from noise. Possessed, a region is promoted to integrated — a bubble travelling with the player where causality runs forward. The honesty invariant: presence **biases latent tensions, never manufactures them**. **Shipped** as The First Mark — building it found a world at rest has *no latent ambient tension* to tip, so the slice pivoted to direct social consequence: the mark IS the tension. An un-provoked NPC stays byte-identical | shipped | high | [The First Mark chronicle](../chronicle/the-first-mark.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-22-the-first-mark-design.md) |
| UNI-33 | **The world remembers by hysteresis** — resolves the "remember vs breathe" fork into one mechanism. Play commits **ordinary facts** (UNI-32) that **condition all future sampling** — "coarse constrains fine" run forward through *time*. Shallow marks relax back toward the noise prior; **deep marks permanently move the attractor** and never fully heal (decay-rate = f(mark depth, site receptivity — see UNI-35). The world breathes back toward a prior the player's deep marks have *moved*. The First Mark ships the **degenerate case** (a committed mark simply persists); the decay curve is the follow-on. Companion to UNI-32; deepens the arc's move #4 | raw | med | founding brainstorm 2026-07-22; [The First Mark spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-22-the-first-mark-design.md) |
| UNI-34 | **See your butterfly** — the arc's move #5, and the reframe that *the wake is the content, not a reward for play*. "Seeing your butterfly" is **contact-tracing on the provenance graph**: point the already-shipped organ (`why`, `windows/historiography`, the Connection Graph) at the **player's own committed facts** and walk the forward chain to the consequence they seeded. Epidemiological reading: some NPCs/sites are **superspreaders of consequence** (high centrality → far-reaching butterfly); others are dead-ends — the wake's *reach* is the centrality of whom you touched. No new engine; a new *subject set* for provenance. **SHIPPED** as *The First Mark* (2026-07-22): the in-session `why`/`recount` already read the session's evolving ledger and already render each fact's provenance, so tracing the fired `turned-hostile` consequence back to the player's own `provoke` needed no new engine — only the minimal one-hop trace, locked by a control test that proves `why` fabricates no chain for an un-provoked NPC. Superspreader-of-consequence reach stays a follow-on | shipped | high | [The First Mark chronicle](../chronicle/the-first-mark.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-22-the-first-mark-design.md) |
| UNI-35 | **Soft and inert ground** — consequence has a *map*. From the geology re-instantiation (a footprint in wet clay that lithifies): the world has **receptive sites** (soft clay — where a tension is already latent) and **inert sites** (dry rock — no lasting trace). Presence at a soft site → deep mark; at an inert site → none. Markability is a **spatial receptivity field**, and the latent-tension regions *are* the soft clay (ties UNI-32's bias-not-manufacture to a place). Sets UNI-33's per-site decay rate. Deferred; The First Mark treats receptivity as uniform | raw | med | founding brainstorm 2026-07-22 |
| UNI-36 | **The mode matrix as binding configurations** — hardcore / normal / creative are not three games but three **bindings of the one possession primitive** (UNI-18's four unbundlable bindings: perceive / control / know-as / identify-with). *Hardcore* = all four fused (death ends it); *Normal* = identify loose (death → the soul moves on, the world persists); *Creative* = the unbound witness (drop in/out, no permadeath). The **witness pole already ships** — `possess`/vessel is the read-only observer — so the modes also **stage the game's construction**: witness (shipped) → mortal-who-leaves-marks (The First Mark) → permadeath-with-consequence. Cross-links UNI-18 | raw | med | founding brainstorm 2026-07-22 |
| UNI-37 | **Scale-of-possession** — the game's long-term growth axis. The sequel to The First Mark, not part of it | raw | low | founding brainstorm 2026-07-22 |
| UNI-38 | **Pooled versus personal observation** — a people's sky-knowledge is many eyes across generations; a dragon's is one pair of eyes across centuries. `chorus::sky_capability` currently derives a culture's observational reach from its perception vector alone, and explicitly names a TECH-1 seam for "instruments, recorded observation" — but the *biological* twin of that seam is untouched: transmission and lifespan are themselves an observational instrument. A long-lived solitary is the only observer who has personally witnessed a rare celestial event (an eclipse season, a Long Count alignment); a short-lived people can only inherit it. This is the structural mirror of [[BIO-37]]'s shipped drift law — the same `sociality × lifespan` pair, applied to *observation* instead of *language change* — which is why it is worth building as a pair rather than a one-off. | EXP-10 | Culture as tension→form transformation (the sublimation thesis) — the roof over humor (EXP-4), drama (EXP-5), and the projection layer (UNI-12): every expressive form transforms a *latent tension* into an *observable artifact* that manages it, and the modes are enumerable — discharge (catharsis), inversion (carnival), mediation (myth), projection (monsters, the Other), wish/warning (utopia/millenarianism), solidarity (ritual), aestheticization (the sublime, impermanence), rehearsal (games), mourning (elegy). The sim derives the tension, mechanizes the transform, and manifests the artifact as a phenomenon inferred over without its source seen (SOC-5's honesty constraint). Freud's *sublimation* is the psychoanalytic case; this generalizes it across the critical traditions. Frontier-essay candidate | raw        | — (framing) | [frontier §expressive](frontier.md#the-expressive-culture-cluster)                        |
| EXP-11 | Carnival and ritual inversion — Bakhtin's sanctioned overturning of hierarchy (Saturnalia, the Lord of Misrule): a homeostatic pressure-release a rigid social order (SOC-1) generates to survive, the more rigid the more needed — a revolt-damper (MAP-9d) and, via Durkheim's collective effervescence, a solidarity-generator. Derived from hierarchy-rigidity × repression, manifested as a recurring festival                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | raw        | med         | [frontier §expressive](frontier.md#the-expressive-culture-cluster)                        |
| EXP-12 | The wish and the warning — utopia as projected desire, dystopia as projected fear, aimed at the social *future* (the normative sibling of MAP-13 prophecy). Under deprivation/subjugation (MAP-9a) a culture generates millenarian phenomena — a promised reversal or paradise (cargo cults, the Ghost Dance, millenarian revolts; historically grounded, semi-checkable): projected hope, source unseen; religion's revolutionary arm                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | raw        | med         | [frontier §expressive](frontier.md#the-expressive-culture-cluster)                        |
| EXP-13 | Myth as the mediation of contradiction — Lévi-Strauss: a myth makes an irresolvable structural contradiction bearable (life/death, nature/culture, kin/stranger) by narrating a symbolic mediator (trickster, culture-hero). Projection at the *structural* level where UNI-12 projects at the emotional; the mediator is the artifact, derived from a real contradiction. Distinct from MAP-4 (myth phylogenetics is propagation, not function)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | raw        | med         | [frontier §expressive](frontier.md#the-expressive-culture-cluster)                        |
| EXP-14 | The projected Other — Said's Orientalism, Pliny's monstrous races: a culture projects its disowned traits onto an out-group or the map's edge (dog-headed men, antipodeans), xenophobia rendered as cartography and travelogue. The imaginative arm of SOC-5 racecraft and MAP-9b persecution — the ethnographic monster whose true subject is the in-group's anxiety, not the neighbour                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | raw        | med         | [frontier §expressive](frontier.md#the-expressive-culture-cluster)                        |
| EXP-15 | The aesthetic of impermanence — mono no aware, ubi sunt, the beauty of the ruin: how a culture aestheticizes *its own melt*, turning transience into form. Lifespan-coupled (EXP-5's channel; a mayfly and an immortal feel transience oppositely) and the emotional face of the memory economy (MEM) — a culture's stance toward its own forgetting, made beautiful                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | raw        | med         | [frontier §expressive](frontier.md#the-expressive-culture-cluster)                        |
