## The species-psychology substrate

| ID    | Idea                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Status     | Conf                     | Where                                                                                               |
| ----- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------- | ------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| PSY-1 | Culture as scaffolding around psychology — a bounded per-species vector (threat response, deliberation latency, sociality mode, in-group radius, time horizon, status basis) upstream of the culture campaigns                                                                                                                                                                                                      | elaborated | high value / weak verify | [frontier §psychology](frontier.md#the-species-psychology-substrate--the-layer-beneath-the-cluster) |
| PSY-2 | De-privilege the goblin baseline — identity-vector-at-goblin and unqualified legacy stream labels are a measurement frame, not a design commitment; fix via epoch-suffixed label migration (ADR 0006) + an abstract reference baseline no species need occupy                                                                                                                                                       | raw        | high                     | [Campaign Y2-2 spec §11](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-07-campaign-y2-2-the-eyes-design.md)          |
| PSY-3 | No default-civilization attractor — the culture stack must land as readily near a non-European analogue (Heian Japan, Ptolemaic Egypt, Classic Maya) as near medieval Europe; the anti-goal generalizes PSY-2 from the goblin baseline to the whole output space, guarded by derive-from-psychology×subsistence×environment rather than templates (SOC-6), eventually a Lab distribution over derived-culture space | raw        | med                      | [frontier §psychology](frontier.md#the-species-psychology-substrate--the-layer-beneath-the-cluster) |
| PSY-4 | Deity valence — a phenomenon's deific *affect* (loved / feared / demonic), not just its salience, derives from the observer's perception + psychology vectors: a nocturnal keen-eyed prey species reads light-emitters as hostile (the sun a demon, the *new* moon a companion), and high `threat_response` × keen `night_vision` can flip *all* emitters negative. The affective sibling of the salience-lens *attention* axis already shipped in `worldgen::perception_lens` (which sizes gods but not their goodness); today `religion::Sentiment` is temporal-only (Eternal/Cyclic/Ambient), carrying no love/fear. Kin to UNI-12 (projection at two valences) and BIO-14 (a fear-eater deified as devil). A religion/psychology consumer feature — not deep-time substrate; Firm Ground II (Campaign 19) proves the placed observer but builds no such consumer | raw        | med                      | [frontier §psychology](frontier.md#the-species-psychology-substrate--the-layer-beneath-the-cluster) |
| PSY-5 | Ideonomic cognition — novel thought as seeded combinatorics (Gunkel's ideonomy via Grace Kind): ideation as a closed, authored catalog of structural operators (negate / substitute / combine / abstract-and-reinstantiate) firing on seeded draws over a creature's held concepts — possibly the only theory of creativity that is constitutionally legal (models author, dice roll; REJ-6). Intake is phenomena (salience doubles as provocation), output is expressed behavior transmissible only by observation; an idea's lifecycle is a state machine (latent → noticed → held → expressed → transmitted → ritualized) whose held → mutated edge is where operators fire — taboo-negation as inversion, substitution as variant technique, combination as syncretism, mis-instantiated abstraction as superstition (UNI-1's error term, UNI-12's generator), ritual stickiness as cultural conservatism. Tier 0 is drift-without-ideation (MEM-3 noise); open primitives are evaluation (keep vs discard), the per-creature repertoire, and target selection. Downstream of PSY-1 (drives) and LANG-4's concept layer | elaborated | med                      | [frontier §ideonomic](frontier.md#ideonomic-cognition--novel-thought-as-seeded-combinatorics) |
| PSY-6 | The motivation engine (GOAP over belief) — rank motivation by autonomy (reflex → drive → emotion → goal → value → project) and every rung is a *setpoint*; an agent acts to close the gap between its setpoints and its *perceived* world. The algorithm is Goal-Oriented Action Planning (Orkin / F.E.A.R.), and every part already exists in the kernel: world-state = the fact ledger, actions (preconditions→effects) = authored verbs whose effects are events (models author, dice roll — MAP-5), goal = the ladder's setpoint, planner = A* (deterministic, seed-tiebroken — constitution-native, the one game-AI with no RNG). Decisive move: the planner reads the *projection* (belief, UNI-16), not truth, so plans can be WRONG — deception / charm / rumor steer behavior for free, and motivation + belief are one active-inference loop (perceive = fit model to world, act = fit world to model, same gap). Cost function = species / culture psychology vector (personality, deterministic, per-agent). Reactive rungs = utility-AI floor; deliberative = GOAP. Runs lazily for observed agents; closes the observation cycle for NPCs. Milestone-2 liveness engine, twin to the social graph (SOC-9), downstream of PSY-1 drives, deliberative complement to PSY-5. **The Quickening (2026-07-18) shipped Milestone 2's first liveness slice without this engine**: an NPC's setpoint is honored only conceptually — a fixed activity-cycle phase (diurnal/nocturnal/crepuscular) standing in for a drive, not a GOAP-planned goal — and its route is a hand-authored two-point schedule (home ⇌ one fixed neighbor), never a planned path. The planner, the needs, and the autonomy ladder above reflex are all still exactly as deferred as before; what changed is that a real committed event (a derived NPC's dated `agent-at`) now exists for a future planner to eventually act *into*. **The Wanting (2026-07-18) shipped the DRIVE rung** — one rung up from The Quickening's bare activity-cycle phase, and the campaign this row's ladder was written to anticipate: a real homeostatic setpoint (a single sustenance drive) is a pure FOLD over an NPC's own committed `agent-at` history (UNI-20 — a drive is a derived view, structurally identical to belief and the c4 query indexes, never a second source of truth), rising away from its resource and falling at it, with a hysteresis dead-band (two thresholds, not one) preventing the thrash a memoryless drive would produce — which is exactly why the drive rung needs MEMORY and reflex does not. Cardinality-1 by design: ONE drive needs no arbitration, and arbitration between COMPETING drives is precisely what the GOAL rung (A* search, the psychology/culture cost function below) exists to resolve, so this slice deliberately stops one rung short of it, not for lack of ambition but because building the planner now would have nothing to arbitrate. The seam is now RESERVED, not built (decision-ledger #9): `decide(npc, world_view, t) -> Intent` is the degenerate one-goal case of goal-selection-then-planning, and `world_view` abstracts what the agent perceives — ground truth today, UNI-16's belief later (the same `view` slot, a body change not a seam change) — so the eventual multi-drive GOAP planner replaces `decide`'s body and adds a second drive, never the tick/commit/read architecture around it. **The Foresight (2026-07-18) shipped the GOAL rung** — the reserved seam's first body-swap: `decide` now runs the kernel A* (`hornvale_kernel::astar` over `SearchSpace`, UNI-19's general engine — this is its first instantiation) from the current world-state to the drive-generated goal (`hydrated`) and returns the plan's first action; `Intent` generalized from `{GoTo, Hold}` to `{Do(Action), Hold}` exactly as the seam anticipated, with the tick still depending on nothing but that output. The essence that makes this GOAP rather than pathfinding is a PRECONDITION CHAIN: the goal is `hydrated`, not "at the water," and the only action achieving it (`Drink`) is gated by an unmet precondition (at-water) the planner must establish with a run of `MoveTo`s first — `[move*, drink]`, discovered by search, over a deliberately minimal two-action space (the MAP-27 verb-chemistry DSL is a named followup, not built here). Still cardinality-1 (one drive, no arbitration), still uniform action cost (no psychology vector), still ground-truth `view` (no belief) — each the next body-swap this campaign leaves reserved, not a regression from ambition. Failure modes are the drama (from a negation pass): satisficing not optimizing, no-plan-found → despair / substitution / delusion (replan over false belief), anti-optimal ordeal, adversarial planning (the con), and plan side-effects minting SOC-9 enmity edges. Cycle-03 of the room-scale corpus (encounters / trophic / inhabitants-as-agents) is a spatial/ecological elaboration of this engine ([cycle-03](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/design/room-scale/cycle-03-the-living-layer.md)). **The Temperament (2026-07-20) shipped the ARBITRATION rung** — the competing-drives resolution this row named as the goal rung's reason to exist: a second drive (thermal comfort, a flow-drive reading the cell temperature against the species niche) meets thirst in one action-centric `arbitrate` — utility = Σ_active capped-urgency × serviceability, survival capped above comfort (soft Maslow, no priority table), committed with hysteresis. Psychology enters as ONE dial — `deliberation_latency` slides *grab* (loudest drive only) ↔ *weigh* (full weighted sum) — the psychology/culture cost function this row anticipated, in its first runtime form. `decide` is the degenerate one-drive case, byte-identical to The Foresight's. Still ground-truth `view` (belief is the next body-swap). The affect read (PSY-7) and a self-scoring population health metric fall out of the same arbitration, and cold/heat earn their cognitive handle (the correspondence audit's cognition column 0/76 → 2/76 — the wave this campaign opened). **Followup #4 (2026-07-20) wired the SECOND psychology dial, `time_horizon`** (anticipation): a foresighted creature pre-empts a projectable STOCK drive, engaging it before its urgency crosses `act` by a lead proportional to the urgency it will accrue over its planning horizon (`Drive::anticipation_lead`; thirst climbs predictably so it leads, a FLOW drive has no trajectory to project so it grants none). Zero foresight is byte-identical to the pre-anticipation model. The psychology/culture cost function this row named now has two runtime dials (grab↔weigh, and myopic↔foresighted), the arbitration's felt-state and health readouts unchanged. **The Slumber (2026-07-20) shipped the THIRD drive, fatigue, and woke the dormant activity cycle**: a stock sleep-debt drive (Process S) + a wake-gate (Process C) that engages a creature only while awake — thirst/thermal fall silent asleep (a survival override wakes a dying creature), a creature rests in place, and the health metric samples at a waking moment. Shipped as the Tier-0 fractional-day wake cycle; the general chronobiology engine (PSY-9) is the registered followup. **The Provender (2026-07-20) shipped the FOURTH drive, hunger** — the thirst homeostat parameterized by the species' diet niche (the `ResourceVector` as the diet dial, read as a continuous mix, never branched on a diet type), reusing The Kindling's temperature-coupled path integral as a second consumer and food as a graded productivity field the drive grazes toward. The general trophic engine (PSY-10) is the registered followup; active predation is deferred to it. **The Dread (2026-07-21) shipped the FIFTH drive, danger/fear** — the avoidance twin of hunger (`threat_niche · hazard_field` = `diet_niche · resource_field` sign-flipped): a FLOW drive (like thermal) that flees a hazard field (v1: the uncanny — placed-exotic strangeness — plus a lethal-temperature backstop) down its gradient, with SIGNED (unclamped) serviceability so a step into worse danger scores negative and danger reshapes the other drives' paths (a thirsty creature routes around a hazard). Urgency is anticipatory (max threat over the cell + neighbours) so the veto engages while a hazard is adjacent. No new fact, no new action (flee is a `MoveTo`) — the leanest drive, genesis AND the seed-42 possession walk byte-identical. v1 niche-less (ametabolic → fearless); the threat niche, boldness dial, and predator-as-hazard are reserved (PSY-11 / PSY-10). **The Belonging (2026-07-21) shipped the SIXTH & LAST drive, social affiliation** — the first drive whose field is OTHER AGENTS, shaped like thermal comfort (sociality has an OPTIMUM, not monotone), company proxied in v1 by HOME-PROXIMITY. Comfort-tier, reachability-gated (a lonely creature within homing range heads home reading *Searching*; beyond reach the drive goes DORMANT, not distress — an unreachable home is a relocation, not a death), so a natural world is never chronically lonely (null-control holds) and a stranded creature's survival distress stays unmasked. No new fact/action; genesis + seed-42 possession walk byte-identical. The population field, attachment (true multi-agent perception), the gregariousness niche, status, and out-group aversion are reserved (PSY-12). **The six Temperament drives are complete: thirst / thermal / fatigue / hunger / danger / social.** The `arbitrate` `Disposition`-struct tidy (six drives deep) was done by **The Disposition (2026-07-21)** — a pure byte-identical refactor bundling the four loose disposition scalars (latency/horizon/helpless/awake) into a `Disposition` struct, dropping arbitrate from 9 args to 6 and retiring its `#[allow(too_many_arguments)]` | elaborated | high (mechanics) | [frontier §goap](frontier.md#the-motivation-engine--goal-oriented-action-planning-over-belief) |
| PSY-9 | **The chronobiology engine — behaviour as an entrainable oscillator stack.** The generalization The Slumber's wake-gate is the first instance of: a creature (or place, or culture) carries oscillators, each a `(zeitgeber, period, endogeneity)` triple — the time-cue it locks to, its cycle length, and how far it free-runs without the cue — coupled to homeostatic pressures (Process S). Lifted, Process C IS active inference (UNI-1): the agent predicting *when* conditions are good. The **zeitgeber is a per-kind field**, which is the generalization to exotic creatures across the animacy axis — alive (a biological solar clock), mechanical (a construct's programmed free-run), informational (an undead's place-entrained death-hour), abstract (a culture's canonical hours / festivals) — the MetabolicClass "per-kind datum, one machine" pattern, for rhythm. Negating "circadian" opens the space: any period (ultradian→circannual), any zeitgeber (sun/moons/tides/temperature/magic-flux/place/none), any gated behaviour (sleep/feeding/breeding/potency/manifestation), nested oscillator stacks. A periodic-grid of zeitgeber × gated-behaviour PREDICTS creatures from its empty cells (a moon-waxed mage, a tidal spawner, a clockwork war-idol). One engine, many users (like the A* planner UNI-19): creature sleep, cultural festivals, ecological temporal-niche partitioning (diurnal predator vs nocturnal prey), magical potency cycles, and eclipse-as-false-zeitgeber (a midday night → omens). **Shipped: the `(sun, circadian, masked/Tier-0)` instance** (The Slumber). **Tier-1 solar masking SHIPPED (The Wakeful Sun, 2026-07-20)** — the `is_awake` body-swap against the real astronomy altitude (`Terrain::solar_altitude` + `LocaleTerrain::with_calendar`, latitude×season×terminator, polar-night hibernation, locked→fatigue-only). Still deferred: the **endogenous free-running oscillator** (Process C Tier-2 — jet lag on travel, drift in caves/polar night, chronotypes; Lorenz caveat: full-precision phase integration, never re-seeded from quantized floats); the other zeitgebers/gated-behaviours; the cross-domain reuse | elaborated | high (framing) | [The Slumber chronicle](../chronicle/the-slumber.md); [the-slumber spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-20-the-slumber-design.md) |
| PSY-10 | **The trophic engine — feeding as one resource-homeostat, many instances.** The generalization The Provender's hunger drive is the first instance of, the sibling of PSY-9's chronobiology engine. Lifted, hunger IS thirst IS sleep-debt: a resource stock depleted by living, refilled by contact with a source — differing only in `(resource axis, source, rate, acquisition action)`. So the drive layer wants ONE resource-homeostat *parameterized*, with thirst, hunger, and a caster's mana-hunger (PSY-8) as instances. The `ResourceVector` niche IS the diet dial (a continuous mix over PHOTOSYNTHATE/FORAGE/PREY/DETRITUS/MINERAL — omnivory and mixotrophy are band-occupants, not types), which dissolves the metabolism gate: a creature hungers for an axis iff its niche weights it AND the world serves it — metabolism sets the RATE, the niche PRESENCE, the source SERVICEABILITY. A tree of trophic modes negation opens: AUTOTROPHIC (self-feeding — phototroph=light/the sun couples feeding INTO the wake cycle PSY-9; chemotroph=vents; **lithotroph=the xorn's minerals**; radiotroph/aethertroph=magic, a PSY-9 zeitgeber); HETEROTROPHIC (herbivore/carnivore/detritivore — the carnivore HUNTS, GOAP-complex); META-TROPHIC (the speculative frontier — emotivore=a fear-eater deified as a devil BIO-14; **theophage=a deity fed by WORSHIP, starving when forgotten — UNI-10's second death**). The eater-eaten symmetry spans predation (anti-symmetric, one dies) → parasitism → mutualism. **The Provender shipped the `(niche·biome-productivity, graze)` instance** for the settled omnivores. **The Quarry (2026-07-21) shipped the PERCEPTION-side of predation** — the first BIOTIC hazard: `worldgen::predator_pressure` (carnivore REALIZED density from the coexistence stack, competition-thinned so it concentrates on wild territory) injected into `LocaleTerrain` as a PREDATOR axis on The Bane's `Hazards`; the quarry's dread derives from carnivory (`predator_weight = 1 − ANIMAL_PREY diet weight` — the eater-eaten link, appetite forward = terror backward). LATENT-scaled so the current bold omnivorous peoples shrug it off (byte-identical), wired-and-correct, waking for a vulnerable creature (a timid herbivore) when one becomes an agent. **The Wilding (2026-07-21) agentified the wild — realizing exactly that clause:** `worldgen::wild_concentrations` derives the world's beast concentrations (mobile non-autotroph species, top-k by realized mass off the coexistence stack — The Quarry's demography-fit shape reused) and `vessel::derive_wild_npcs` mints them as NPCs appended to the peopled roster in BOTH the possession session and the health sim, so the latent predator hazard finally has a vulnerable agent to wake — a herbivore beast (giant-elk, mammoth) now fears predator ground *live*, a carnivore beast (rust-monster) does not. The derivation gives a beast its biosphere NATURE (the threat niche, derived as a person's is) but the DEFAULT temperament (a wild species is absent from the psyche registry, so steady boldness / mid dials). Genesis byte-identical (derived from committed facts — no draw, no predicate, no epoch); the possession galleries drift (the fauna walks alongside the peoples). The wild that HUNTS — the visible GOAP predator, Lotka-Volterra prey cycles — is still the reserved body below. **The Teeth (2026-07-21) shipped the FOOD/APPROACH side — the anti-symmetric dual of the fear:** `worldgen::prey_pressure` (the mirror of `predator_pressure` — realized density of the prey base, mobile-beast non-carnivores, competition-thinned, `[0,1]`) injected into the hunger drive's `ANIMAL_PREY` axis via a new `Terrain::prey_value`, so a carnivore's food is *other creatures* and it is drawn UP the prey gradient. Predation-approach is NOT a new drive — it is the (diet · PREY) cell of the niche × field cross-product = hunger's prey axis, finally given a spatial field. ADDITIVE + LATENT (food_value only rises) → the settled peoples are byte-identical *by construction* (seed-42 genesis AND possession galleries unchanged), waking only a foraging wild carnivore (the rust-monster on prey-poor ground, drawn to the herds). Genesis byte-identical (derived, no draw, no epoch). Deferred still: the **kill** (the anti-symmetric outcome — one dies, one eats); the **visible agent-predator** (targeting and PURSUING a specific prey NPC, hunting as GOAP, acute/survival-tier); **Lotka-Volterra** prey cycles; **subsistence modes** (forage→farm→herd — hunger at SOCIETY scale, into the culture stack, `intentionality` axis: emergent foraging → designed agriculture); the exotic trophs and meta-trophs; ontogenetic diet shifts (a larva scavenges, the adult hunts — BIO-21). Feeding `visibility` spans visible (hunt) → latent (bask) → invisible (faith) — a rendering seam. Sibling of PSY-9; one engine, many users, like the A* planner (UNI-19). **The Dread (2026-07-21) built predation's PERCEPTION-side first**: the danger drive (PSY-11) reads a hazard field a predator agent will later join as a moving source — the hunter is a threat before it is a meal | elaborated | high (framing) | [The Provender chronicle](../chronicle/the-provender.md); [The Quarry chronicle](../chronicle/the-quarry.md) (predation's perception-side); [The Wilding chronicle](../chronicle/the-wilding.md) (agentified the wild); [The Teeth chronicle](../chronicle/the-teeth.md) (the approach side); [the-provender spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-20-the-provender-design.md); [the-quarry spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-21-the-quarry-design.md); [the-wilding spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-21-the-wilding-design.md); [the-teeth spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-21-the-teeth-design.md) |
| PSY-11 | **The threat/fear engine — danger as one hazard-homeostat, the avoidance twin of hunger.** The generalization The Dread's danger drive is the first instance of: where the trophic engine (PSY-10) is a creature *approaching* a resource it values, the threat engine is a creature *avoiding* a hazard it fears — `threat_value = threat_niche · hazard_field` is `food_value = diet_niche · resource_field` with the sign turned over, and `flee_step` is `forage_step` inverted. Lifted through a cross-domain pass (immunology's danger-model, robotics' artificial potential fields, finance's risk appetite, theater's foreshadowing) and a timeline of the drive's cognitive evolution (reflexive startle → bronze-age taboo → calculated risk → predator-prey game theory). **The Dread shipped the `(uncanny + lethal, flee)` instance** — niche-less, flow, signed serviceability (the repulsive-field term that reshapes the other drives' paths). Deferred, correct-by-construction: the per-kind **threat niche** — **SHIPPED (The Bane, 2026-07-21): `threat_value = threat_niche · hazard_field`, the derived twin of `food_value`, completing the diet↔threat↔sociality niche trilogy. 3 hazard axes (UNCANNY/HEAT/COLD, the split of The Dread's scalar field); the niche DERIVED from what the creature already is (HEAT/COLD from the temperature niche — a cold-adapted creature dreads heat; UNCANNY from the metabolic class — an Ametabolic elemental IS eldritch and does not fear it), no fresh authoring. v1 weights ≥0 (differential FEAR — a dragon *fearless* of its element, weight 0); the negative-weight *attraction* (drawn to the hazard — fire that is home to a fire elemental, a predator's inverted fear of its prey) is RESERVED, the shared far-shore with The Mettle's reckless pole, landing with predation-approach (PSY-10). Deferred: the general `HazardVector` over a registered `HazardAxis` basis; the reserved axes (HOLY/UNHOLY, POISON, DROWNING, PSY-10's PREDATOR)**; the **boldness** psychology dial — **SHIPPED (The Mettle, 2026-07-21): the banked `PsychVector.threat_response` read at CREATURE scope (no new field — the third dial, like `time_horizon`/`deliberation_latency`), scaling the Danger drive's felt threat `× 2(1 − boldness)` centered on 0.5 (steady/inert), landing the full coward↔brave axis (a negation pass surfaced the coward pole); the reckless APPROACH far shore (`× < 0`, negative fear, DRAWN to the hazard — the moth, the berserker, the hunter's inverted fear of its prey) is RESERVED, the seed of predation-approach landing with PSY-10**; **remembered danger** — **SHIPPED (The Haunt, 2026-07-22): `believed_hazard` — a pure FOLD over committed `agent-at` history ∩ frightening-truth (the cells a creature stood on where its own Danger reading crossed `DANGER_ACT`), the sign- AND cardinality-inverted twin of `believed_water` (a SET shunned, not a target sought). Read as a FINITE route COST in the shared planner (`GoapSpace`/`NavSpace`): a `MoveTo` into a remembered cell costs `1 + REMEMBERED_PENALTY` (=5), so a creature's thirst/homing PLANS bend AROUND ground it was frightened at — proactive whole-path avoidance, the true dual of believed-water's seeking. The Danger FLOW drive is UNCHANGED; memory lives at the PLANNING layer, where reach-beyond-sensing belongs (the ideonomy's one-hop Danger-drive term was overturned by grounding: redundant with sensing for static terrain). The finite penalty IS the survival-override (a dying-thirsty creature braves the ravine when the detour exceeds the cost). STALENESS: a cell is remembered-dangerous iff the MOST RECENT visit was frightened (a safe revisit clears it — a fold that still forgets); for v1's time-invariant terrain danger this reduces to visited∧still-frightening. **The first drive-layer FALLIBLE belief** (water is permanent; danger is transient → a danger-memory can be stale — the individual seed of phobia). **The first drive signal NOT dormant on seed 42** (decision-ledger #4, Nathan-approved): the settled peoples are never frightened, but the WILD FAUNA (agentified by The Wilding/The Teeth) roam hazardous ground and DO remember it — so the feature works on all agents and the possession-over-time gallery legitimately shifts (a few beasts detour, ~1267→1262 stirred); success is SCOPED DRIFT (only that gallery moves, by the intended fauna-avoidance) not byte-identity. RESERVED, still this engine's body: **transient-danger memory** — **SHIPPED (The Phantom, 2026-07-23): a creature remembers where a herd's ALARM frightened it and shuns the now-safe ground — fear that OUTLIVES its cause, the phobia. The alarm is never committed, so `frightened_at(cell)` generalizes to `frightened_at(cell, day)` = (terrain + `ALARM_SCALE`·alarm-at-(cell,day)) × mettle ≥ act, RE-DERIVING the alarm at each past visit-day (`alarm_field` is pure over frozen — no predicate, no epoch, belief still == fold). Read by The Haunt's existing planner. The most-recent-visit STALENESS rule (inert in The Haunt) goes LOAD-BEARING: a safe revisit disproves and clears the phantom. **The first drive-belief that is TRUE yet yields a FALSE present inference** — the exact anatomy of a phobia/superstition (the memory is faithful; "therefore still dangerous" is the mistake; the cause is abstracted away, so the creature cannot reason clear). Recursion (memory re-derives alarm → affect_of → memory) broken FREE by terrain-only replay (empty band → empty roster). Byte-identical (no primary-afraid emitter on seed 42 ⇒ transient set empty). PERF (decision-ledger #4, 2 ideonomy passes): re-derive-at-read-time was ruinous on long sims with wary fauna (>710s); fixed by a SESSION-LEVEL append-only memo of `primary_afraid(entity, day)` (SAFE by construction — a past day's verdict is stable under monotonic ledger growth, so the memo never invalidates) + a terrain-shortcut (skip the alarm where terrain alone ≥ act), reaching parity with `believed_water` (~371s). the VISCERAL FELT phobia — **SHIPPED (The Shudder, 2026-07-25): the phobia given a body. `believed_hazard`'s fold now returns `HazardMemory { shunned, dread }`, split by PROVENANCE — and the split needed no new computation, because the fold's existing terrain SHORTCUT already asks the question (skip the alarm replay where terrain alone frightens), so everything falling through it is TRANSIENT by construction. The transient half feeds back into the live `Danger` drive at the creature's OWN cell, in the SAME additive slot as The Alarm's borrowed distress and at the SAME `ALARM_SCALE` — no new constant, because the dread IS an alarm term (the one that recorded the memory), so put back through `feels_frightening` it necessarily reproduces the verdict that created it: the memory and the feeling cannot disagree. The one design fork (feel vs flee) resolved on PRECEDENT, not taste — `loneliness_from_plan`'s discipline is that a felt state with NO actionable outlet is made dormant rather than allowed to register as distress; a phantom cell is now-safe ground with NO terrain gradient (unlike an alarm halo, always within one hop of genuinely frightening terrain), so dread without its own gradient would read `Lost` and arm the health alarm. Hence `serviceability`/`flee_step` read terrain PLUS dread: fear with an outlet is wariness, the same fear without one is a pathology. **Named design principle (governs the rest of this engine): every term added to a creature's fear must preserve the existence of an experience that DISPROVES it** — which declined two candidates on one argument: self-reinforcing dread (frightening BECAUSE frightened → no visit can clear it) and anticipatory dread (felt at one remove → avoidance prevents the arrival that disproves). Both are the same non-closing loop. Contact dread closes it, and the e2e earned the arc unprompted: the creature flees the haunted cell, homing pulls it back, the safe arrival clears the phantom. Byte-identical AND bit-identical population health on all five null-control seeds (the peoples are never frightened ⇒ no emitter ⇒ the fast path returns before any dread is recorded — structural, not scale-tuned). No contagion: the alarm field's emission read passes `band = &[]`, whose bandless memory has no emitters and hence an EMPTY dread map, so ONE structural fact — the bandless replay — buys termination, byte-identity, and no contagion at once.** RESERVED, still this engine's body: time-DECAY of un-revisited fear (**and note: anticipatory dread SHIPS WITH time-decay or not at all — the longer-reach gradient opens a loop only a forgetting half-life can close**); ENTITY-keyed dread (fear of a remembered INDIVIDUAL rather than a place — the seam where this memory meets SOC-9's enmity edges); KIND-keyed dread (one bad cliff teaching fear of ALL cliffs — the generalization that makes a phobia portable); the SANCTUARY (the proseasis: a cell remembered as where fear LIFTED, read as comfort — the memory form of The Alarm's reserved reassurance, and a member of the experiential-memory family below); the generalized `frightened_at(cell, day)` absorbing the reserved live-moving-predator + diurnal-terrain sources with no new mechanism; SUPERSTITION → collective TABOO (the individual phantom shared across a herd/people); the joint incremental MATERIALIZED VIEW for believed_water+believed_hazard (the perf endgame)**; time-DECAY of un-disproven fear (a forgetting half-life atop the revisable rule); the geometric-heuristic STRONG avoidance (an admissible A* heuristic decoupling penalty magnitude from the Dijkstra expansion budget → wide-berth avoidance without plan-failure); a longer-reach GRADIENT (distance-decay repulsive field, a wide berth not just the exact cells); GREEDY-drive memory (foraging/fleeing avoiding remembered ground, not just the planning drives); COLLECTIVE / cultural remembered danger (a herd or people sharing shunned ground — TABOO & HAUNTED places, the individual flinch scaled to folklore; couples religion + The Alarm's social channel); the general EXPERIENTIAL-MEMORY family (`believed-food`/`believed-comfort` — one fold shape, many truths, the lattice hub); NARRATING the flinch.** **fear-contagion** — **SHIPPED (The Alarm, 2026-07-22): a per-tick `alarm_field` (a sparse `BTreeMap<RoomAddr, f64>`) summed over the frozen population's PRIMARY-afraid creatures (own Danger drive lit by the *terrain* hazard field), stamped as a one-hop halo, read ADDITIVELY + latently into the EXISTING Danger drive — not a new drive, the externally-driven substitution of the self-driven one (the autonomy-axis reframing), scaled by the reader's own `mettle_factor` (a bold creature shrugs off the herd's panic). Field-not-agents per PSY-12's determinism line (a field over the frozen population is order-independent; direct agent-to-agent affect reads mid-tick are not). PRIMARY-only emission — the field is built from an alarm-free `affect_of` read, so borrowed fear never re-emits — makes the epidemic `R0 < 1` BY CONSTRUCTION: a bounded, terminating halo around genuine danger (remove the hazard, the wave collapses next tick), no self-sustaining stampede. Additive-latent ⇒ EMPTY on seed 42 (the settled peoples never reach primary distress), so byte-identity is STRUCTURAL, not scale-tuned. RESERVED, still this engine's body: the damped cross-herd WAVE (secondary transmission with per-hop decay <1, R0<1 — fear crosses a whole herd yet still terminates); REASSURANCE / calm-contagion (Gunkel's proseasis, the positive dual — a calm creature DAMPING neighbours' fear, the alarm term negative, the shared far-shore with The Bane's attraction and The Mettle's recklessness); per-species SUSCEPTIBILITY (an ALARM axis in `Hazards` + a `ThreatNiche` weight — a skittish herd animal catches panic a lone predator shrugs off — once gregariousness has a per-species derivation, couples PSY-12); NON-FEAR contagion (thirst/hunger/thermal distress as its own distinct cue) + fear-from-CARCASS (a killed prey's death emits alarm, couples The Teeth's reserved kill); an ALARM-AWARE felt-state read (the post-tick `affect_of`/narration reads seeing the field — v1's mover-only signal leaves a fleeing creature narrated "calm but moving"; deferred because it risks the health baseline for only narration coherence).** Predator-as-moving-hazard is PSY-10's; danger is its perception-side. Sibling of PSY-9/PSY-10; one engine, many users | elaborated | high (framing) | [The Dread chronicle](../chronicle/the-dread.md); [The Mettle chronicle](../chronicle/the-mettle.md) (boldness); [The Bane chronicle](../chronicle/the-bane.md) (threat niche); [the-dread spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-21-the-dread-design.md); [the-mettle spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-21-the-mettle-design.md); [the-bane spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-21-the-bane-design.md); [The Alarm chronicle](../chronicle/the-alarm.md) (fear-contagion); [the-alarm spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-21-the-alarm-design.md); [The Haunt chronicle](../chronicle/the-haunt.md) (remembered danger); [the-haunt spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-22-the-haunt-design.md); [The Phantom chronicle](../chronicle/the-phantom.md) (transient-danger memory); [the-phantom spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-22-the-phantom-design.md); [The Shudder chronicle](../chronicle/the-shudder.md) (the felt phobia); [the-shudder spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-24-the-shudder-design.md) |
| PSY-12 | **The affiliation/belonging engine — sociality as a drive, the first OTHER-directed drive.** The generalization The Belonging's social drive is the first instance of: where thirst/hunger read the self and thermal/danger read the environment, affiliation reads a third field — the presence of one's own kind. Shaped like thermal comfort (sociality has an OPTIMUM — too lonely and too crowded both cost), it is the root of a whole subtree the drive layer will grow into. **The Belonging shipped the `(home-proximity, head-home)` instance** — comfort-tier, reachability-gated (reachable home → *Searching*; unreachable → dormant, since loneliness is comfort not survival). Deferred, correct-by-construction: the **population field** (Tier-1 — company as a spatial field over nearby settlement population, affiliate at *any* village, dissolving the reachability discontinuity); **attachment** (perceive and seek *specific* others — kin, mate, allies — the first true multi-agent perception, whose determinism is handled deliberately, the reason v1 uses a field not agents); the **gregariousness niche** (the sociality setpoint solitary ↔ eusocial, where the *solitary* pole flips the sign — others *repel*, crowding stress, the twin of the diet niche PSY-11 and threat niche); **status / dominance** (the hierarchical dimension, reading the authored `Sociality` Hierarchic/Communal and `in_group_radius` — the invented structure atop the discovered urge, into the culture stack SOC-9); **out-group aversion** (a stranger as a mild threat — where affiliation meets The Dread, PSY-11); and **construct/hive affiliation** (the ametabolic own-drive — a swarm-node seeking its cluster). With this the six Temperament drives are complete. Sibling of PSY-9/PSY-10/PSY-11; one engine, many users | elaborated | high (framing) | [The Belonging chronicle](../chronicle/the-belonging.md); [the-belonging spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-21-the-belonging-design.md) |
| PSY-7 | Emotion as appraisal — the affective readout of the gap. Decompose an emotion and it's a bundle of appraisals (Scherer / Lazarus / OCC), each computed by a system the game has: valence/arousal from the motivation gap (PSY-6), agency/blame from event provenance + the social graph (SOC-9), expectedness from prediction-error vs belief (UNI-16), coping from whether GOAP finds a plan. So emotion is a *derived view*, not a system. Substitution over one appraisal generates the family: same event (ally killed) → blame = circumstance = grief, blame = enemy = rage, blame = self = guilt, plan-exists = resolve (M+N over appraisal dims, not an emotion table). Discovery↔invention: species-universal basic affect vs culture-constructed emotion (Barrett; a lexical gap = undifferentiated affect, RENDER-7 / LANG — saudade / amae get a home). Feeds back as a MODULATOR: biases the belief threshold, bumps GOAP goal-priority, charges SOC-9 edges, gates salience. Lands with Milestone-2 liveness; drawn together by UNI-20. **The Temperament (2026-07-20) shipped the affect read** — this row's core, a first slice: affect is a pure function of the arbitration state (spec §7), a point in the valence × arousal circumplex (valence = making-progress vs blocked; arousal = drive urgency), labelled by region (content/eager/searching/frustrated/lost, + a persistent helpless) and carrying its intentional object — exactly "emotion is a derived view, not a system." Derived structurally from the decision (met/known/unknown/blocked), not an emotion table (the M+N-over-appraisals shape). Surfaced in narration and reduced across a population into a self-scoring health metric (a distress FAMILY: prevalence / chronicity / stuck / recovery-rate / by-cause / by-species). NOT yet the full appraisal bundle (agency/blame from SOC-9, expectedness from belief prediction-error) nor the modulator feedback — those await the social graph and belief tiers. **Followup #2 (2026-07-20) made `Helpless` BEHAVIOURAL**, not just a label: a creature whose survival drive stays unmet past an onset (`learned_helplessness`, a fold over `last_drank`) GIVES UP — it Holds regardless of any affordance (the behavioural difference from Frustrated, which still strains), reading Helpless, with a periodic probe so the scar reverses slowly rather than trapping it. The synthetic harness (#5) exercises it end-to-end: a stranded creature's felt state deepens Frustrated → Helpless, distinguishing "still trying" from "given up" on the sim's own output. **The Convalescence (2026-07-26) sharpened the health family's ALARM to the conjunction its design named**: the metric bounded `chronicity` (a distress run past the chronic threshold) alone, faithful to The Temperament §8's bullet-list LABEL — but §8 is internally inconsistent, its discriminator paragraph and evidence battery both stating the bug signal as *persistence AND no recovery*, and the campaign resolved the contradiction toward that reasoning (which gives a reason recovery matters) over the label (which gives none) — a long spike that RECOVERS is a legitimate hard patch in a varied world, and two of a creature's six drive rhythms, phase-shifted by a tick, can weld into one nine-tick run that then returns to health. The alarm is now `stuck` — long AND never ended, evaluated PER CREATURE, because `recovery_ticks` is a population mean and a population-scope conjunction would let one genuinely stuck creature hide behind nine recovering ones (a bug alarm may be noisy; it may not be silent). Short-and-still-open is right-censored and deliberately silent. `chronicity` stays computed and reported as a DIAGNOSTIC — it reads 0.0000 on all five sweep seeds exactly as `stuck` does, so nothing was loosened (decision 0080) | elaborated | high (mechanics) | [frontier §emotion](frontier.md#emotion-as-appraisal--the-affective-readout-of-the-gap); [The Convalescence chronicle](../chronicle/the-convalescence.md) |
| PSY-8 | **The psych vector already supplies magic's discount rates.** Incidence (UNI-24) *is* a discount rate — how much you discount harm you do not bear — and economics has exactly two, social and temporal; `PsychVector` shipped both. **`in_group_radius`** ("how wide 'us' is drawn", idle since the-gathering) is the **social** discount: insular → externalise onto outsiders → sacrifice, blood magic. **`time_horizon`** ("how far ahead works are planned") is the **temporal** one: immediate → borrow against a later era → Athas. **`deliberation_latency`** (explicitly "idle this campaign; banked") governs how deep a derivation search a people runs, and so answers the question the ideonomic-cognition section already preregistered — *"does a high-deliberation-latency species innovate slower but retain better?"* **`status_basis`** decides which UNI-25 posture confers standing, hence whether a civilisation grows monasteries (savings), an Athas (liquidation), or a theocracy (credit). **A species' magical style is therefore a readout of already-drawn values, not a new parameter** — the four goblinoids already carry distinct incidence signatures, and druid and defiler are opposite corners of the same two dimensions. Potency likewise: read `sovereignty_floor`'s budget `e = a·ln(mass_kg) + b·potency` for casting and **mass and potency are additive into one buffering capacity, therefore substitutes** — so **potency is the capacity to absorb your own debt**, with the bargain in the coefficients: mass logarithmic and *trainable*, potency linear, ~7× more efficient per unit, and *drawn at birth*. Hence the psion's discipline is real and rate-limited (strength buys the **stock** — how much you absorb at once; endurance buys the **flow** — how fast you refill; and asceticism is **budget reallocation**, every unsatisfied drive freeing capacity, which is why austerity is universally associated with mystical power and why in a self-incidence world that association is simply correct accounting). Crossing potency against repertoire yields **the defiler as an emergent quadrant** rather than a choice: high potency + low repertoire is the dragon, which does not defile because it need not; low potency + high repertoire is the scholar whose knowledge outruns the body that must fund it, so the debt goes outward. The frail scholar burns the world; the dragon simply is. Places the menagerie's mighty roster (`potency > 0`) as natural casters, not researchers | elaborated | med | [frontier §magic](frontier.md#magic-as-a-graph-grammar--the-debt-economy) |

A climate → demography → gender causal chain no gender-as-constant model can express; the synthesis of BIO-3 × SOC-2 × MAP-6                                                         | raw             | med         | [frontier §social](frontier.md#social-structure-as-orthogonal-axes--cashing-the-substrate) |

Topology hands over structure & story unauthored: cycles are drama (reciprocity = alliance, feud loop = vendetta, triangle = tragedy), triads *self-balance* (Heider structural balance relaxes unbalanced triangles deterministically — a liveness engine with no RNG, the social form of surprise-is-epistemic), brokers (high betweenness) are power. Reversibility asymmetric (trust breaks instant, grudges sticky, some debts never repaid) = the feeling. Re-instantiates as the food web (DOM-3 ecology, trophic cycles) and kinship/alliance theory (marriage as group-binding edge, incest taboo forcing exogamy). Derived layer over the event ledger, lands with Milestone-2 liveness. The five-level ontology (species / culture / community / persona / individual, [[SOC-10]]) does not cleanly name it: an institution is a persistent entity with *roles*, a *hierarchy*, and a *function*, cross-cutting communities (a trade guild spans ports; a queen's court outlives its courtiers). Likely a derived layer over the same event ledger — an institution = a bundle of role-handles + a standing relation among them — not a new domain; kin to the relational [[SOC-9]] but with named offices rather than dyadic bonds. Deferred: an ontology question for the living-community sequel, out of scope for the feasibility benchmark. | raw | med | [frontier §living-community](frontier.md#the-living-community--history-as-a-baked-derivable-biography) |
| SOC-belief-sharing | Belief propagation makes the *group* the error-correcting unit — the social extension of [[UNI-1]]'s belief tier. A lost creature has its belief updated by a knowledgeable neighbour, so the band's confusion stays low by circulating information. **Shipped (law only):** co-located agents reconcile belief by reachability from the receiver's current position; a creature alone keeps its memory verbatim, so the live world is byte-identical. **But belief is a per-tick home-anchored fold, so a shared belief is transient** — the vision needs [[SOC-persistent-hearsay]] | shipped (law) | high | [The Tidings chronicle](../chronicle/the-tidings.md) |
| SOC-contact | **Contact between peoples — raiding is one cell of a much larger space.** When two peoples meet, anything can happen: pillage, rent-seeking/tribute, colonization, genocide, assimilation, proselytization, employment, alliance (forced or otherwise) — and mutually beneficial trade. **The generative kernel is the *rivalry of the prize*, not a menu of behaviours:** a perfectly rival immobile good (land, a valley, a mine) admits only one holder, so contact over it is necessarily *conquest* — taking is evicting; a rival but *mobile* good (herds, grain, metal) can be seized **or swapped**, so the same covetousness yields raiding or trade; a rival, *self-moving* good (people, labour) can be taken **or hired**, so it yields captives or employment; and a **non-rival** good (gods, language, technique) costs the source nothing to copy, so contact over it is necessarily *peaceful* — diffusion, proselytization, assimilation. Trade and war are therefore not opposite moral choices but what one covetousness does to goods of differing rivalry. The material sibling of [[SOC-information-economy]], which already derives the *informational* case from "rivalrousness × scarcity"; it also recovers The Tumult's ledger-#8 **asset-mobility** rule (mobile ⇒ rustle/extract, target survives; immobile ⇒ conquer/displace), designed and then lost between ledger and spec. **The outcome space is a lattice of two independent survivals** — the *persons* and the *peoplehood*: both survive ⇒ displacement, tribute, alliance, trade, employment; persons survive but peoplehood dies ⇒ assimilation, enslavement, proselytization, colonization; persons die but peoplehood survives ⇒ attrition; neither ⇒ genocide. The shipped bake already touches three of those four quadrants (displacement via `relocate`, attrition via `WAR_LOSS`, extinction below `VIABLE_MIN`) through *one* mechanism, conquest-displacement; the unbuilt quadrant is the culturally interesting one. **The slice boundary draws itself:** one-shot outcomes are already expressible as `CauseOfEnd`/`Founding` chains in the occupation record and are cheap, whereas every *standing relationship* (tribute/Danegeld, alliance, vassalage, colonial rule, employment) needs a persistent inter-community relation — whose natural substrate is [[SOC-9]]'s derived typed-edge social graph and whose governance sibling is [[DOM-16]]. **The stated price of that was wrong and *The Tithe* re-observed it: the ledger's `Fact` envelope is ALREADY a dated entity→entity edge, so the first standing relationship cost one registered predicate, not a save-format change and a new subsystem.** That is precisely the dominance-hierarchy + collapse-release slice [[SOC-criticality]] had already deferred, rediscovered from the opposite direction — decent evidence the original cut was in the right place. Program frame for the living-community sequels; predation ([[SOC-criticality]]) is one child of it. **The deferred quadrant now has a measured argument for promotion, not merely a structural one (*The Tumult*).** The conquest-displacement mechanism was built and its cascade-size distribution adjudicated: branching ratio σ ≈ 0.051 over 2974 conquests, deeply sub-critical, because one-shot conquest supplies dissipation and stores **nothing** between relaxations. The standing relationship this row names is exactly the accumulation term — tribute concentrates value into a structure that can later topple — so the criticality result ([[SOC-criticality]]) and this row's slice boundary now agree on which subsystem is next, arrived at from opposite directions and with numbers attached. | raw | med | [frontier §living-community](frontier.md#the-living-community--history-as-a-baked-derivable-biography) |
| SOC-criticality | Conflict as self-organized criticality — raids should *emerge*, never be floored. **Built three times, FALSIFIED twice — not a power law.** Build 1 (crowding sandpile over [[MAP-61]]) had drive, no dissipation; build 2 (predation, gated by [[SOC-inhibition]]) dissipation, no accumulation, σ ≈ 0.051; build 3 ([[SOC-tribute]]) supplied accumulation and **doubled σ to ≈ 0.11 without moving the shape** — 0.48 decades of support, nothing past 3 displacements in ~22k conquests. What is missing is not storage but **conduction**: depth and collapse-release stay non-goals, so failure has no medium. | shipped (slices 1–2 — falsified twice) | low (measured twice) | [The Tumult](../chronicle/the-tumult.md); [The Tithe](../chronicle/the-tithe.md); [frontier §living-community](frontier.md#the-living-community--history-as-a-baked-derivable-biography) |
| SOC-casus-belli | **An occupation records *how* it ended but never *why* anyone did it.** `OccupationRecord` carries `cause` (`Famine`/`Burned`/`Plague`/`Fled`/`Migrated`) and `ended_by`, so "burned, at the bugbears' hand" is committed history — but no field carries a *motive*: retribution, a succession claim, a stolen relic. Grievance exists at agent scale and nothing lifts it to community scale, so a war has a perpetrator and no reason. Kin to [[SOC-contact]] and [[SOC-inhibition]], which name motive as the missing factor at another scale. History-domain work, not a rendering campaign's to carry. | raw | med | brainstorm 2026-07-27 (The Blocking) |
| SOC-dense-settlement | **At most one alive community may occupy a geosphere cell, so no two living settlements are closer than ~110 km** — `Bake::vacant_habitable` enforces it at founding. What that forbids is *rival polities in sight of one another*; it does not forbid several *sites* per cell, which are derived ([[SOC-settlement-tiers]]). Relaxing it is a small diff and a large revalidation — world identity moves, so an epoch, a census re-baseline, and study pins converted to invariants (Amendment 2 §1b.11). **Nathan wants this soon-ish (2026-07-27);** its own campaign. | raw | high | brainstorm 2026-07-27 (The Blocking) |
| SOC-flagship-selection | **A people's "chief settlement" is an identity that gets re-selected, and any metric keyed on it measures the re-selection.** `flagship_of` resolves the **first surviving settlement fact** of a species — the *oldest surviving occupation*, not the largest — and a conquest closes the raider's own occupation record and reopens it at the seized cell. So a raiding people hands its flagship on in ~35 % of worlds where a non-raiding one does so in ~6 %, and the records it loses are systematically **inland** (an inland cell has good neighbours to covet; a high-capacity coastal cell has few). *The Tumult* inverted an earlier preregistered hypothesis (kobold flagships less coastal than goblin ones) and the investigation found no one had relocated: 100 % of the coastal movement lives in the subset where the flagship *cell changed*, where a biased subset at 0.549 coastal was deleted and its replacements landed at 0.688, back at the founding-pool base rate; where the cell was unchanged the rate is byte-identical before and after. Two durable consequences. (1) **An instrument caution:** because the bake places every people from one species-blind pool ([[SOC-inhibition]]'s niche-relative gate is exactly what is missing), the shipped model predicts a **zero** difference between peoples on any flagship-cell property — so such a test measures draw noise plus survivorship, never a niche claim; the falsified test was retired with a tombstone and replaced by one on the axis the bake does differentiate (disposition). Prefer a *provenance-classified* readout — which event class the flagship arrived by — over a bare property of "the flagship". (2) **A modelling direction:** oldest-surviving is a defensible seat rule but it is an accident of records order, not a claim. A *chosen* seat — the largest, the most defensible, the one holding the shrine — would make capital cities move for reasons, and is a natural companion to the standing-relationship subsystem of [[SOC-contact]]. | raw | med | [The Tumult](../chronicle/the-tumult.md) |
| SOC-information-economy | Information has value, so sharing is strategic, not naive gossip — the value of a piece of knowledge = the **rivalrousness × scarcity** of the resource it points to (the location of grass is worthless to hoard; a gold vein or the best fishing hole is a *guarded* secret, because sharing invites competition and depletion). So creatures share selectively: cheap/abundant-resource info freely, scarce-resource info guarded; freely with kin/reciprocators, withheld from rivals — wiring in [[SOC-9]]'s trust/allegiance graph and `PsychVector.sociality`/`in_group_radius`. **Key connection — information LEAKS, it doesn't only flow:** you can be *seen* going to the fishing hole, so observation extracts a secret without consent — exactly [[MAP-perceive-apertures]]'s *"being seen is the dual of seeing."* Secrets therefore have **security** (can you reach your vein unobserved?), and stealth/exposure becomes economically meaningful. The strategic, game-theoretic (reputation, reciprocity, defection) layer above [[SOC-belief-sharing]], wired to the resource economy and the social/inequality seam; several campaigns, parked. | raw | high | design session (this conversation) |
| SOC-tribute | **Extraction as a negotiation under mutual ignorance — the accumulation term [[SOC-criticality]] measured missing.** A raid whose prize is *mobile* (its people and product) **subordinates** rather than evicts, so a dominant grows without moving; the ledger already had the shape. A patron taxes what it can **see**, a vassal holds what it **has** — assessment, concealment, shortfall, no attracting equilibrium — and tribute banks in a **store** feeding strength, never pressure. Extraction is a discount-rate family on the patron's horizon. Deferred: protection, depth, collapse-release. | shipped | high (measured) | [The Tithe](../chronicle/the-tithe.md); [frontier §living-community](frontier.md#the-living-community--history-as-a-baked-derivable-biography) |
| SOC-inhibition | **Predation's missing third factor — a raid is `motive × capability × inhibition`.** [[SOC-criticality]]'s rule has motive and capability but none, so every people that *can* raid *does* — discarding the heterogeneity criticality needs. Five vetoes, one conjunction: disposition and self-aversion (own-trait, so asymmetry is free), niche-relative ([[SOC-10]]), pairwise aversion, concealment ([[SOC-information-economy]]), no-spoils. **Sequencing matters:** every gate only *reduces* conflict, so add them in opposed pairs. Slice 1 took the cheapest two; niche-relative unblocked by [[BIO-39]] | shipped (2 of 6 gates) | med | [The Tumult](../chronicle/the-tumult.md); [frontier §living-community](frontier.md#the-living-community--history-as-a-baked-derivable-biography) |
| SOC-persistent-hearsay | Belief that survives the telling — a creature carries what a neighbour told it *after* it walks away, instead of re-deriving belief from its own perception every tick. The prerequisite [[SOC-belief-sharing]] proved necessary: under the per-tick, home-anchored belief fold a shared belief evaporates the moment co-location breaks (the told creature steps toward the water and the fold re-runs with no-one to pool from, snapping its belief back to home), so a band can *speak* tidings but cannot *remember* them, and durable band-healing is impossible. Realizing it means folding "heard" facts into belief the way [[UNI-1]]'s tier already folds perceived ones — overlapping The Echo's `knowledge.rs` "heard ≠ true" transfer — the natural next campaign after The Tidings. Also surfaced there: the *home-anchoring artifact* (a creature ignores water it stands in when that water is unreachable from its home), fitting The Surmise's reserved nearest-to-current-belief followup. | raw | high | [The Tidings chronicle](../chronicle/the-tidings.md) |
| SOC-settlement-tiers | **Commit the contingent; derive the regular.** Only the top settlement tier is contingent — a city sits where a ford or a battle put it — while towns, villages and hamlets space themselves by agronomy and walking speed, which is *why* they are derivable (Christaller, from geography's side). The arithmetic forbids the alternative: a ~10,000 km² cell holds ~1,000 villages at medieval density. So the committed tier stays one-per-cell ([[SOC-dense-settlement]]) and the lower lattice is derived. | raw | high | brainstorm 2026-07-27 (The Blocking), ideonomy pass 6 |

## The culture domain — enrichment directions

Twenty concrete enrichments of the *shipped* culture crate (`domains/culture`:
`subsistence` → an environment-and-psychology role ladder in `structure`),
generated by an ideonomy pass over its current shape. Each moves the crate
along one axis it presently pins — complexity (a flat role list), naturalness
(fully derived), homogeneity (one culture per settlement), source (environment
× psychology only), or connectivity (each settlement computed in isolation).
Several instantiate the elaborated social-structure (SOC-1) and memory-economy
(MEM) essays in the specific place the code stands today.

| ID     | Idea                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Status | Conf | Where                                                                                      |
| ------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ | ---- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| CUL-1  | Culture that contradicts its environment — `subsistence`/`structure` are today *exact functions* of biome × surplus × population × threat, so identical valleys grow identical peoples. A small drawn **founding disposition** (quantized, slowly decaying) biases structure away from the environmental optimum: path dependence and the maladaptive-but-persistent custom, the *accident* source the deterministic-derivation model forecloses | raw    | med  | [frontier §social](frontier.md#social-structure-as-orthogonal-axes--cashing-the-substrate) |
| CUL-2  | Descent as a first cultural output, orthogonal to rank — the shipped `structure()` is purely vertical (a role ladder); add **kinship/clan/moiety** as a horizontal axis so a settlement can be richly cultured yet flat. The concrete, shippable instantiation of SOC-1's elaborated-but-unbuilt descent axis                                                                                                              | raw    | high | [frontier §social](frontier.md#social-structure-as-orthogonal-axes--cashing-the-substrate) |
| CUL-3  | Roles as an obligation graph, not an ordered list — represent structure as *directed obligations* (labour/deference/tribute owed between rungs); the ladder becomes one readout of the graph, which also expresses reciprocity, patronage, and corvée a `Vec<String>` cannot. Raises culture's complexity axis from flat-list to network                                                                                    | raw    | med  | [frontier §social](frontier.md#social-structure-as-orthogonal-axes--cashing-the-substrate) |
| CUL-4  | The anti-institution / the levelling culture — negating "surplus grows a top rung": a psychology that *actively suppresses* hierarchy (levelling mechanisms, shame-the-chief) holds the ladder flat even under high surplus. Cheap (one gate flip on the `top`/`slave` rungs) and the structural sibling of EXP-11's ritual inversion                                                                                       | raw    | med  | [frontier §social](frontier.md#social-structure-as-orthogonal-axes--cashing-the-substrate) |
| CUL-5  | Culture that melts instead of freezing — the shipped structure is stamped `day: 0.0` and never revisited; wire the rungs and customs into the memory economy (MEM-1) so an unsupported caste **decays when surplus × will drops** (a shaman rung a famine cannot afford melts back to phenomenon). Turns culture from a snapshot into a maintained thing                                                                    | raw    | high | [frontier §memory](frontier.md#the-cultural-memory-economy--knowledge-as-a-maintained-thing) |
| CUL-6  | Sub-settlement culture: the unit as lineage/household — substitute the accounting unit settlement → descent group, each carrying its own structure vector; the settlement's culture becomes their aggregate or negotiation. The cheapest route to the intra-settlement heterogeneity (factions, cleavage) the one-culture-per-settlement model forecloses                                                                    | raw    | med  | [frontier §social](frontier.md#social-structure-as-orthogonal-axes--cashing-the-substrate) |
| CUL-7  | Supra-settlement culture: the unit as the people (ethnos) — substitute settlement → a culture spanning many settlements of one species, shared and slowly diverging. Gives diffusion (CUL-8) and MAP-9 contact an actual substrate to travel over, and is the connectivity-axis precondition for much of the rest                                                                                                          | raw    | high | [frontier §social](frontier.md#social-structure-as-orthogonal-axes--cashing-the-substrate) |
| CUL-8  | Cultural diffusion — a settlement adopts a neighbour's structural element by proximity × asymmetry (prestige borrowing), substituting the *source* of culture from environment to contact. Cashes the MAP-9 interaction layer specifically into culture; needs the ethnos substrate (CUL-7)                                                                                                                                | raw    | med  | [frontier §map](frontier.md#the-frontier-map)                                              |
| CUL-9  | The founding charter — a rare settlement whose structure is *authored* at founding (a colony, a utopian sect, a fortress-order) and resists environmental drift (naturalness → man-made). The designed exception that proves the derive-from-environment rule; a natural CLI pin                                                                                                                                            | raw    | med  | [frontier §social](frontier.md#social-structure-as-orthogonal-axes--cashing-the-substrate) |
| CUL-10 | Roles × affect — each rung carries an emotional stance (the artisan's pride, the slave's resentment, the shaman's awe) as a per-role sentiment field, feeding deity-valence (PSY-4) and expressive culture. The role ladder combined with the emotion catalogue                                                                                                                                                            | raw    | med  | [frontier §psychology](frontier.md#the-species-psychology-substrate--the-layer-beneath-the-cluster) |
| CUL-11 | The subsistence work-calendar — each subsistence mode implies a labour-and-ritual rhythm (planting/harvest, transhumance, tide-fishing): the temporal skeleton of a culture, and where MAP-18 calendrics couple culture to the shipped astronomy                                                                                                                                                                          | raw    | med  | [frontier §map](frontier.md#the-frontier-map)                                              |
| CUL-12 | The pollution stratum from sanitation, not ideology — attach purity/taboo rules to specific rungs (tanner, corpse-handler, nightsoil caste) so an untouchable stratum emerges from material sanitation (BIO-5), the materialist route to SOC-7's phenotype-free caste hard case                                                                                                                                            | raw    | med  | [frontier §biological](frontier.md#the-biological-substrate--body-reproduction-and-deep-time) |
| CUL-13 | Structure as the downstream of crossed thresholds — a crossed capability (pottery → storage → surplus, TECH-1/2) *retroactively reshapes* the ladder, so culture reflects thresholds crossed over history rather than only the present environment                                                                                                                                                                        | raw    | med  | [frontier §technology](frontier.md#technology-as-capability-thresholds)                    |
| CUL-14 | Close the culture→psychology loop — psychology flows one way into `structure()` today; over era-ticks let the scaffolding reshape the substrate (BIO-1), a long-stratified people's descendants inheriting a shifted status-basis. The feedback edge the one-directional model omits                                                                                                                                       | raw    | med  | [frontier §biological](frontier.md#the-biological-substrate--body-reproduction-and-deep-time) |
| CUL-15 | Era-ticked structural drift — the single largest gap: `structure()` runs once at `day 0.0`. Give it a trajectory (founding → differentiation → ossification → simplification under collapse) recomputed as paleoclimate (MAP-6) and population (MAP-7) move. The highest-leverage single change in the culture crate                                                                                                        | raw    | high | [frontier §social](frontier.md#social-structure-as-orthogonal-axes--cashing-the-substrate) |
| CUL-16 | Cultural founding as datable facts — the moment a rung first appears (first slave, first smith-caste) becomes a ledger fact, so a settlement's cultural history is legible and the almanac can narrate "in year N the smiths became a caste"                                                                                                                                                                               | raw    | high | [frontier §social](frontier.md#social-structure-as-orthogonal-axes--cashing-the-substrate) |
| CUL-17 | The ossification ratchet — a rung long in existence becomes sticky (harder to shed than to grow, MEM-3 stickiness), so two settlements in identical environments differ because they *arrived* differently: path dependence made mechanical                                                                                                                                                                               | raw    | med  | [frontier §memory](frontier.md#the-cultural-memory-economy--knowledge-as-a-maintained-thing) |
| CUL-18 | Culture-change rate as a lifespan function — a custom survives only if transmitted across the ~3-generation floating gap (MEM-2), scaled by species lifespan (MEM-7): long-lived peoples ossify, short-lived peoples churn                                                                                                                                                                                                 | raw    | med  | [frontier §memory](frontier.md#the-cultural-memory-economy--knowledge-as-a-maintained-thing) |
| CUL-19 | The cultural genotype — represent a settlement's culture as a compact serializable vector (structure axes + custom/taboo flags + descent mode + charter bit + drawn disposition), so cultures can be **diffed, inherited (a colony forks its parent), and mutated (drift edits one field)**. The notation substrate CUL-5/8/15/18 all require; keep the customs an authored catalogue with triggers and upkeep costs — data, not code, per decision 0011 | raw    | high | [frontier §social](frontier.md#social-structure-as-orthogonal-axes--cashing-the-substrate) |
| CUL-20 | A culture-distance metric for the Lab — with the genotype (CUL-19), define a distance so studies can measure the currently unfalsifiable: whether identical environments breed identical cultures (making PSY-3's no-default-attractor a number), and how sharp the diffusion gradient runs across a contact zone. A drift-checked census like the shipped calibration censuses                                              | raw    | high | [frontier §map](frontier.md#the-frontier-map)                                              |
