## The biological substrate

| ID     | Idea                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Status     | Conf                    | Where                                                                                         |
| ------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------- | ----------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| BIO-1  | The body as substrate; "the scaffolding reshapes the body" feedback thesis closing culture-around-psychology into a loop                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | elaborated | high value / calib core | [frontier §biological](frontier.md#the-biological-substrate--body-reproduction-and-deep-time) |
| BIO-2  | Life-history on one fast–slow axis — body size, offspring number, lifespan, investment covary; drama couples to the slow end; carrying capacity and EXP-6 architecture are implicitly *per-human-body*, so a giant or a sprite rescales settlement density and building dimension (Kleiber metabolic scaling). **Narrowed and spec'd**: a live collision found the coexistence stack (BIO-23) had already authored body mass, so this row drained into a design covering the layer coexistence did not build — the `MetabolicClass` clade tag and the derived metabolic-rate/lifespan/maturity/reproductive-tempo profile, Kleiber's 0.75 and the 0.25 metabolic-time exponent, kept strictly neutral (no new stream, byte-identical worlds). Mass, home-range, and footprint stay BIO-23's. **The Kindling (2026-07-20) carried `MetabolicClass` into the DRIVE layer** (Temperament followup #1): a metabolism GATE (Ametabolic → no homeostatic drives — the undead/construct/elemental simply persists, reading Content) and a temperature-COUPLED thirst (a per-class path integral of a dehydration rate over the creature's occupancy — Endotherm heat-only/sweating, Ectotherm ambient-tracking, realizing the *behavioural, water-facing* half of the spec's CAP-1 ectotherm coupling). The *energetic* allometric CAP-1 (basal-rate coupling in `allometry.rs`), Autotroph transpiration, and the ametabolic kinds' OWN drives (construct integrity, undead hunger, an elemental fed BY its element) remain deferred — the per-kind drive-authoring layer the gate opens | spec'd     | high                    | [BIO-2 design spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-bio2-life-history-allometry-design.md); [The Kindling chronicle](../chronicle/the-kindling.md); [the-kindling spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-20-the-kindling-design.md) |
| BIO-3  | Dimorphism and mating system derived (Bateman–Trivers); sex-determination as a species parameter (haplodiploidy → eusociality via Hamilton)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | elaborated | high                    | [frontier §biological](frontier.md#the-biological-substrate--body-reproduction-and-deep-time) |
| BIO-4  | Gene–culture coevolution — bidirectional era-tick; allele/trait fields under a breeder's equation; lactase/amylase/dark-vision, dated sweeps as calibration anchors                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | elaborated | high (calib)            | [frontier §biological](frontier.md#the-biological-substrate--body-reproduction-and-deep-time) |
| BIO-5  | Excretion, sanitation, purity — sanitation as a carrying-capacity term; Douglas pollution grid generating latrine/taboo/caste; nightsoil as fertility input                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | elaborated | med                     | [frontier §biological](frontier.md#the-biological-substrate--body-reproduction-and-deep-time) |
| BIO-11 | Aberrant reproduction — reproduction that converts or consumes a host (parasitoid implantation, assimilation, ceremorphosis: slaad, illithid, the xenomorph/Thing), the case BIO-3's mating-system derivation stops short of; a demographic *and* epidemic term (host conversion is population transfer between species, a disease-ecology bridge to BIO-5/BIO-6's parasitic fungus), never a horror flourish                                                                                                                                                                                                    | raw        | med                     | [frontier §biological](frontier.md#the-biological-substrate--body-reproduction-and-deep-time) |
| BIO-13 | Reproduction by manufacture — a species built, not bred (warforged, golems, the constructed people), unplugging the biological stack: no breeding system for SOC-2 to read, no genome for BIO-4 to coevolve, no descent line for SOC-1; heredity is replaced by *industrial lineage* (model, foundry, production run) and kinship by provenance-of-manufacture. The hard test of whether the culture stack runs with the biology stack disconnected; Eberron's "made for a war, now seeking purpose" lands on MEM and identity                                                                                   | raw        | med                     | [frontier §biological](frontier.md#the-biological-substrate--body-reproduction-and-deep-time) |
| BIO-16 | The individual is not the unit of accounting — oozes, swarms, colonial organisms that fission and fuse (distinct from the hive-mind, which is the sociality axis): the population is biomass, not a count of stable individuals, so SOC-4's per-individual standing vector and MAP-7's headcount both lose their referent. Demography and intersectionality would have to be re-expressed for a people whose "individual" splits and merges; a *clonal* species with no within-group genetic variation is the adjacent limit — BIO-4 evolution stalls (nothing to select) and racecraft has no phenotype to mark | raw        | med                     | [frontier §biological](frontier.md#the-biological-substrate--body-reproduction-and-deep-time) |
| BIO-17 | Permeable species boundaries — half-species (half-elf, half-orc), ring species, clines: a species is a discrete registry key, but a hybrid is a *gradient between two*, attacking the data model itself. Pulls in introgression (gene flow across the BIO-4 boundary) and gives SOC-5 racecraft its sharpest target (the "half-caste" as the specifically-persecuted category). Forces the question: is species a type, or a position in a continuous space?                                                                                                                                                     | raw        | med                     | [frontier §biological](frontier.md#the-biological-substrate--body-reproduction-and-deep-time) |
| BIO-19 | Non-natal species — a people created once and never bred (the first and only generation), a clean MAP-7 variant with natality set to zero: a population in monotonic decline from turn one, an inverted time-horizon psychology (no next generation to plan for), no breeding system for SOC-2 to read. The endling (MAP-7) made structural — always dying, from the start — and needs no new metaphysics, so it is cheap and falsifiable                                                                                                                                                                        | raw        | high                    | [frontier §biological](frontier.md#the-biological-substrate--body-reproduction-and-deep-time) |
| BIO-20 | Symbiotic dual-consciousness — one body, two selves (the Trill, kalashtar, an intellect devourer and host), distinct from the hive (many bodies, one mind) and the shapeshifter (one fluid identity). Hits two seams at once: standing (host or rider? SOC-4's per-individual vector now has two occupants per body) and memory (a symbiont passed between hosts is a *mobile* memory-store — MEM-6's aboleth but portable, carrying a lineage of borrowed lifetimes). Wants a personhood model the stack does not yet have                                                                                      | raw        | med                     | [frontier §biological](frontier.md#the-biological-substrate--body-reproduction-and-deep-time) |
| BIO-21 | Metamorphic life-stages as distinct persons — a species whose young are a different organism (feral larvae that later become sapient; insect instars as castes), breaking continuous ontogeny: culture-transmission has a hard floor (you cannot teach a larva) and personhood is a *threshold*, not a birthday. Concrete demographic and transmission effects; narrower than the origin/natality probes                                                                                                                                                                                                         | raw        | med                     | [frontier §biological](frontier.md#the-biological-substrate--body-reproduction-and-deep-time) |
| BIO-22 | Incorporeal species — minds with no body at all (ghosts, energy-beings), the limit-probe of the BIO-1 thesis (the body is the substrate culture scaffolds around): a species with *no substrate to scaffold*. Value is diagnostic — does the stack degrade gracefully when the body is null, or crash? — not a build target; metaphysics-gated (UNI-2). Captured as a degradation-test marker — but the place-bound afterlife (UNI-6) is the case where the objection dissolves: the architecture is the substrate a ghost culture scaffolds around                                                              | raw        | low                     | [frontier §biological](frontier.md#the-biological-substrate--body-reproduction-and-deep-time) |
| BIO-23 | Footprint as mass-scaled home range — the grain of the multi-species coexistence stack: a species' per-capita land claim scales with body mass (kin to BIO-2's fast–slow axis), so a cell is not single-occupancy but a *stack* of overlapping home ranges at different grains, and a settlement's true footprint is bodies × range, not a bare head-count. The down-payment the coexistence stack needs is one authored mass/scale field on `SpeciesDef`, confirmed absent today                                                                                                                              | elaborated | high (mechanics)        | [coexistence-stack spec §1.1](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-13-the-coexistence-stack-design.md) |

## The biosphere — the living substrate

| ID     | Idea                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Status | Conf | Where                                                                  |
| ------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------ | ---- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| BIO-6  | Flora & funga as fields — vegetation/fungal cover as typed functions over (space × climate) atop the biome prior; the terrestrial phenomena SEQ-4's observation path and the deep-dweller pantheon both want. **Reframed (2026-07-16):** flora is the *readout of **BIO-36**'s disturbance field*, not a climate classifier — shape it like the `language` domain (a proto-form plus a drawn cascade, present observable falling out, reconstructable backwards) rather than like `climate` (present conditions → label); the in-repo precedent for "observable = f(history)" is already shipped, in the wrong domain to notice. **Homogeneity resolves differently per scale:** a **composition vector** (60% maple / 30% beech / 10% spruce) at globe scale, individual stems *sampled from* it at room scale — the shipped MAP-28 → locale coarse-field inheritance is already exactly that joint. **Schema-adjacent, flagged not decided:** whether `Biome` stops being a stored primitive and becomes a **derived query** over the flora field (a biome as *discovered* readout of which flora each independently won, not an *invented* threshold a designer imposed) — UNI-20's derive-never-store cuts for it; `Biome` shipping in `scene/tiles/v1` as a cross-repo Orrery contract cuts against; an epoch/contract call, Nathan's. Note what the current model has no vocabulary for: **the displaced**. Nothing in `Biome` can record having been something else — the linguistic *substrate* cognate is the relict beech in the ravine the loggers could not reach, and `paleoclimate` already ships **refugia** as precisely that mechanism                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | raw    | med  | [frontier §biosphere](frontier.md#the-biosphere--the-living-substrate) |
| BIO-7  | Fauna on the life-history axis — animals/fish drawn through BIO-2's fast–slow frame; the SRD corpus already spans beasts; predator/prey/forage as fields feeding subsistence and hazard                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | raw    | med  | [frontier §biosphere](frontier.md#the-biosphere--the-living-substrate) |
| BIO-8  | Domestication & agriculture — the culture layer harnessing biosphere fields; staple crop per biome, the Boserup plough (SOC-2's input), nightsoil (BIO-5), famine as paleoclimate (MAP-6) × carrying capacity (MAP-7)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | raw    | high | [frontier §biosphere](frontier.md#the-biosphere--the-living-substrate) |
| BIO-9  | Ethnobotany & the pharmacopoeia — psychoactive/sacred substances as a ritual phenomena source for religion, gated by metabolism (BIO-4) and the purity grid (BIO-5); existence is fields, activity is genetics, sacredness is the culture vector                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | raw    | med  | [frontier §biosphere](frontier.md#the-biosphere--the-living-substrate) |
| BIO-10 | Paralinguistic vocal signaling — mating/alarm calls and dimorphic voice as a system distinct from speech (a fauna-and-body vocalization layer, BIO-3/BIO-7), feeding music (EXP-2) and register; the vocal-loudness/resonance axis The Tongues bank-authors also lives here                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | raw    | med  | [frontier §biosphere](frontier.md#the-biosphere--the-living-substrate) |
| BIO-12 | The cultureless creature as a standing cultural pressure — an intelligent, solitary, territorial *individual* (not a breeding population) that founds no culture but forces neighbouring peoples to route settlement, taboo, and myth around its continued presence (the beholder in its lair); distinct from BIO-7's superordinate-threat *field* in being singular and persistent, and a religion input (deified/demonised) sooner than a subsistence one; the *living island* (aspidochelone) is the habitat-inverted limit — the creature so large it is itself the terrain others settle on (crossing MAP-11) | raw    | low  | [frontier §biosphere](frontier.md#the-biosphere--the-living-substrate) |

| BIO-24 | Bidirectional trophic coupling — the coexistence stack's predator/prey term on top of BIO-7's fast–slow fauna frame: prey abundance caps the apex population it can support (a supported-biomass ceiling), and the apex in turn shadows (suppresses) its prey's own carrying capacity, a two-way coupling rather than a one-way subsistence read. | BIO-25 | Habitat as fit — a species' carrying-capacity field derived from its traits coupled to the world's environmental fields: `K = resource-supply(saturating weighted sum over niche uptake) × Π condition-response`. Fields are *discovered* from the shipped sim (insolation/NPP/lithology/prey from sky/climate/terrain/the stack), the coupling *invented* per species — so thousands of species tap ~10 shared fields. Built as an entity-agnostic **suitability engine** (reusable for cultures/religions/materials). Fixes the niche-blind flat-NPP K that makes every species live in the same place. **Shipped** (The Niche, 2026-07-14): the K formula built as a kernel suitability engine (`ConditionResponse`) + a worldgen K layer, validated end-to-end — seed-42's identical-everywhere composition broken, `composition-variance` 0.065 — on the four goblinoids. Two-way (not full) differentiation: same-resource omnivores are climate-limited; the menagerie (distinct resource niches) + genesis cutover fold forward into the entity-component program (UNI-22). **The Menagerie (2026-07-15)** built the distinct-resource roster + cut genesis over, and found the differentiation limit is deeper than the roster: `supply` is a **single** NPP field scaled per species by uptake, so a resource niche shifts *magnitude* not *place* — only the climate term is spatial. The suitability engine's per-axis field vision (lithology-minerals, prey) is the deferred unlock for resource-strongholds | shipped | high | [the-niche spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-the-niche-design.md); [The Niche chronicle](../chronicle/the-niche.md) |
| BIO-26 | Sovereignty (might) — a response *floor* that turns hard tolerance (excluded outside the niche) into soft preference (never excluded, merely denser at the optimum). One continuous axis carries a creature from environment-placed **plankton** through domain-holding **legendary beast** to omnipresent **god**. **Shipped** (The Niche): `sovereignty_floor(mass, potency)` built and consumed by the condition response — buffer-able axes (temperature/moisture/insolation) floored, elevation stays hard. The plankton→god span is live in the trait; the mighty roster that exercises its high end folds into the entity-component program (UNI-22) | shipped | high | [the-niche spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-the-niche-design.md); [The Niche chronicle](../chronicle/the-niche.md) |
| BIO-27 | Devotion & place-identity — preference *amplitude* (how strongly a creature favours its optimum given freedom). High-sovereignty + high-devotion → a named **domain/lair** (the dragon's crag, the god's temple); high-sovereignty + low-devotion → omnipresent-indifferent (an ambient force). Devotion is what gives even an unconstrained creature a home; ties to the religion domain at the high end. Preference is bidirectional — aversion troughs (undead shun sunlight) as well as peaks. **Devotion (the peak amplitude) shipped** in The Niche's condition response; aversion troughs and the emergent lair/place-identity are deferred (the latter needs the mighty menagerie, → UNI-22). **The Menagerie (2026-07-15)** shipped the mighty roster (chromatic dragons, treant — potency > 0), but the emergent lair still waits: under the single-NPP-supply limit (BIO-25) an apex holds no *place*, so place-identity needs per-axis spatial resource fields (BIO-35) before a dragon can own its crag | spec'd | med | [the-niche spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-the-niche-design.md); [The Niche chronicle](../chronicle/the-niche.md) |
| BIO-28 | The trade-off manifold as generative prior — specialist↔generalist with `peak×breadth ≈ const` and `Σ uptake ≈ budget`, so a random draw yields coexisting differentiated species instead of homogenising super-species. Normal creatures sit ON the manifold; the mighty spend sovereignty (BIO-26) to buy exemption. The same manifold as BIO-2's fast–slow axis | spec'd | high | [the-niche spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-the-niche-design.md) |
| BIO-29 | Phylogenetic species generation — draw new species as descent + mutation from ancestors over the `family`/proto system already on `SpeciesDef`, forming a tree → coherent **adaptive radiations** (a clade filling adjacent niches) rather than IID noise. The manifold (BIO-28) constrains *where* on trait-space; phylogeny generates *how it got there*. A new strategic trait axis and the source of living migrations | raw | med | [the-niche spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-the-niche-design.md) |
| BIO-31 | Ontogenetic niche trajectories — a species need not be one point in niche-space but a *path* through it: tadpole→frog, caterpillar→butterfly occupy different habitats at different life-stages. | BIO-32 | Niche construction & anthropogenic ecology — organisms engineer their own substrate fields (beaver→wetland, tree→soil, coral→reef: keystone engineers), a reverse entity→environment coupling. At scale, the campaign's **peoples** rewrite the fields (agriculture → NPP, cities → novel niches), spawning a whole **commensal ecology** that follows civilization — rats, weeds, livestock, pests. Couples the biosphere to the settlement layer. **Also the largest writer into BIO-36's disturbance field on any inhabited world (2026-07-16):** clearing, burning, coppicing, and grazing dwarf the physical sources, and the motivating case — New England's maple-rich recovered forest as a legacy of 19th-c clearcutting rather than a climate equilibrium — is precisely this row meeting BIO-33 through that field. Niche construction is the *reverse* edge (entity → environment); the disturbance field is how it reaches vegetation without either domain importing the other | raw | med | [the-niche spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-the-niche-design.md) |
| BIO-33 | Ecological succession — a cell's occupancy as a temporal sequence bare→pioneer→mid→climax, reset by disturbance (the strife/unrest fields), ordered by the fast–slow (r/K, BIO-2) axis with facilitation (climax can't skip pioneers). The dynamic/history counterpart of the coexistence stack's equilibrium; the "understudy takes the stage" mechanic. **Two corrections to this row (2026-07-16):** (1) **disturbance is a regime — frequency × intensity — not an event, and not only a reset.** Recurring burn does not merely restart the sequence; it *holds a system open indefinitely*. Savanna and temperate grassland are fire **disclimaxes** — stable states maintained by disturbance, not waystations en route to forest — so two shipped `Biome` variants are arguably mis-modelled today as pure climate outcomes. A model in which disturbance only resets cannot express them. (2) **Under priority effects the sequence is not a function of present state at all**: same climate, same disturbance, different forest, decided by who arrived first — alternative stable states, path dependence, ↔ BIO-34 dispersal. The disturbance term itself is **BIO-36**'s composed field; this row supplies the ordering, not the forcing | raw | med | [the-niche spec §8](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-the-niche-design.md) |
| BIO-34 | Dispersal & island biogeography — suitability ≠ occupancy when barriers (ocean, mountain) isolate: a species suited to a place it cannot reach is absent → endemism-by-isolation, relictual ranges, the empty-niche island. The reachability layer over the fundamental-niche K | raw | low | [the-niche spec §8](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-the-niche-design.md) |
| BIO-35 | Per-axis spatial resource-supply fields — the `supply` term becomes one spatial field per resource axis rather than a single NPP proxy scaled per species. *The Menagerie* proved this load-bearing: with one field, distinct niches shift a species' magnitude everywhere but never its *place*. **The abiotic half shipped** (The Demesne): material dominants rose 2→4 and the pure-mineral specialist went from noise to the world's largest domain. Two gaps survived, measured: the four small peoples still share one forage number, and the ≥6-dominants target stays unmet. Stage 2 is the prey field | shipped (Stage 1: abiotic) | high | [The Demesne chronicle](../chronicle/the-demesne.md); [The Menagerie chronicle](../chronicle/the-menagerie.md); [the-niche spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-the-niche-design.md) |
| BIO-36 | **Disturbance as the composed cross-domain field** — the hub BIO-6, BIO-32, and BIO-33 are three views of. The shipped `classify_land` is a Whittaker classifier: vegetation as a pure function of present climate, which is by definition *potential natural* vegetation — the climax. Observed cover is instead `f(climate, disturbance regime, time-since-disturbance)`; New England's maple-richness is a legacy of 19th-century clearcutting, not an equilibrium (the unlogged counterfactual is beech–spruce). The pioneer is **not a worse competitor but a specialist in exploiting shock**, so the ordering is BIO-2's *already-shipped* fast–slow (r/K) axis — the missing primitive is the forcing, not the response. Note the unintended claim the current classifier makes: **that the world has no history** — every cell at equilibrium, always. **Shape, forced by the layering rule:** this can never be a domain — terrain (volcanism), paleoclimate (glaciation), climate (fire; DOM-fire's `CLIM-hazards`), settlement/BIO-8 (clearing, burning, coppicing), culture (strife/unrest), and BIO-7 (grazing) all *write* it while vegetation alone *reads* it, so a crate would import half the workspace. It composes at the **composition root** (`windows/worldgen`), each domain contributing through the trace protocol only — the identical resolution DOM-ocean, DOM-cryosphere, and DOM-fire each independently reached. Adding a disturbance source then never edits an existing domain. **Tiering:** tier-0 is a **drawn** stationary regime (seeded noise × domain-derived rates) — the fire is invented on demand and *narrated backwards*, never forward-simulated; every observer reconstructs the same one because it is seeded, and the Lorenz guard-rail forbids the integrator alternative outright. Tier-1 refines where a domain owns real history (settlement clearing, glaciation), coarse constraining fine, the refinement never contradicting the prior. **Payoff:** answers MAP-29's 1000-bowls-of-oatmeal problem with *meaning* rather than noise-decoration — same climate, different forest, because different history — and promotes vegetation from a label to the world's **memory organ**: a landscape readable as evidence (↔ MAP-2's epistemic layer, ↔ MAP-30's palimpsest; LANG-8's blind comparative method is the exact cognate move — reconstruct the disturbance from the present composition, and score it) | raw | med | [frontier §biosphere](frontier.md#the-biosphere--the-living-substrate) |
| BIO-37 | **The sociality × lifespan roster grid** — a creature-design *generator* for what mind and language mean off-community, the content half of [[UNI-31]]. Its two axes enumerate kinds of minded being, each cell fixing a language regime: a settled people's tongue drifts into dialects, a solitary ancient's is near-frozen — intelligence with versus without cultural transmission. Empty cells are design predictions, so new kinds come from filling productive cells, not copying the bestiary. **The language half shipped** (0066) as a `CascadeRegime`; roster-generation remains for future campaigns | shipped | med | [The Vacancy chronicle](../chronicle/the-vacancy.md) (roster-generation half, first instantiation); [The Solitary Tongue chronicle](../chronicle/the-solitary-tongue.md) (language half); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-22-the-solitary-tongue-design.md) |
| BIO-38 | **Vantage as a perception dimension** — `PerceptionVector` has an organ (`night_vision`), a schedule (`activity`), and an attentional allocation (`sky_attention`), but no slot for *where the creature observes from*. Flight is the obvious case and the one that exposed the gap: a dragon on the wing sees differently not because its eye or its schedule differs but because it is a thousand feet up, and [The Vigil](../chronicle/the-vigil.md) found that the only available slot, `sky_attention`, means *celestial vs terrestrial* — the opposite of what a hunting flier does, since it trades against noticing the ground. Surfaced by a tree-finding pass over "a creature's world-access" (empty branch), which also flagged a second absent channel-family: non-visual senses (olfaction, thermoception — a pit-viper heat sense is a red dragon's obvious channel). Widening the vector is reserved to its own campaign by the type's own doc; the honest first question is whether vantage is a *perception* dimension at all or a property of the observation site | raw | med | [The Vigil chronicle](../chronicle/the-vigil.md); ideonomy pass, The Vigil brainstorm 2026-07-24 |
| BIO-39 | **A unit is not a frame — an authored optimum must be said in a frame a creature can care about.** `ConditionNiche`'s elevation axis read the raw isostatic datum — right for a crust, wrong for an organism — while its optima were authored as if fixed. Sea level differs per world (−1723 to −3478 m), so "2600" meant 5200 m above sea level on one world and 5900 on another, above the highest land either had. The kobold's highland stronghold was **unoccupiable**; its best-fit share went 0.4 % → 26.5 %. The broken datum had been an accidental land mask, repaired in [[BIO-35]]. Open: a marine axis | shipped (elevation axis; marine supply open) | med | [The Tumult](../chronicle/the-tumult.md) |
| BIO-40 | **Sovereignty should decay with distance, and the diversity it props up needs recalibrating** — `sovereignty_floor(mass, potency)` ∈ [0, 0.95] is added as an UNCONDITIONAL lower bound to every buffer-able condition response, so a goblin keeps **33.5%** of full habitat response at any temperature, moisture or insolation whatsoever and a red dragon **78.5%**. The name says *sovereignty* (imposing your conditions on a place); the mechanism implements **ubiquity** (presence everywhere at reduced strength), and its own doc calls that a virtue — "a soft preference that never excludes". The replacement was BUILT AND MEASURED in The Waterline and is preserved at commit `4f852fd2`: `value = devotion·exp(−z²/2)` with `z = (field − optimum)(1 − s)/width`, i.e. `effective_width = width/(1 − s)`, where `s` is the fraction of the environmental gap a creature closes for itself. Accommodation (a dragon warms its lair, shifting its effective optimum) and endurance (a wider tolerance band) are the SAME algebra, so the model need not choose; it needs no new tuning constant (the existing 0.95 ceiling becomes width × 20), drops `floor` from `eval`, keeps the buffered-vs-hard axis split for free (elevation passes `s = 0`), reproduces today's formula exactly at `s = 0`, and — unlike a floor — DECAYS. **Why it is deferred, and the finding that makes it a campaign rather than a patch: the coexistence diversity this project calibrated β to produce is substantially an ARTIFACT OF THE FLOOR.** With it, a badly-suited species holds K ≈ floor × conditions everywhere — comparable to the well-adapted one — and takes a real share; without it, K ≈ 1e-8 and the cell goes to a single winner. Measured: mean per-claimed-cell diversity **1.333** across five seeds (1.257–1.438) against the preregistered band **[1.5, 3.0]**, every seed failing. β = 2.0 was frozen by a 13-seed × 10-β sweep under the OLD response shape, so it no longer means what it was chosen to mean: the fix requires a re-sweep and a re-freeze. Also surfaced: `session_snapshot`'s golden bytes moved while world JSON stayed byte-identical — gameplay state depends on K where world state does not, a save-format-adjacent surface nobody has mapped | elaborated | high | [The Waterline chronicle](../chronicle/the-waterline.md); implementation at `4f852fd2` |
| BIO-41 | **Is `MINERAL` really a land-only resource?** — The Tumult's land mask (`56364acf`) zeroes all five resource-supply axes on submerged cells, on the reasoning that every v1 axis is *terrestrial* supply: what a land-dwelling forager can reach. That is right for photosynthate, forage and detritus, and defensible for prey. It is least obvious for **mineral**, which terrain defines on the seafloor because the seafloor is rock — and the xorn, an `Ametabolic` pure-`MINERAL` burrower that swims through stone, is the roster member it decides. Under the supply frame the question is not "does the xorn need a medium flag" (The Waterline's rejected answer) but "is mineral supply genuinely absent under water, or only unreachable by things that breathe?" A seafloor-defined mineral axis would give the xorn a domain without any per-species exemption, and would be the first axis to distinguish *what a resource is* from *who can get at it*. Cheap to test: unmask `MINERAL` alone and measure what moves | raw | med | [The Waterline chronicle](../chronicle/the-waterline.md); The Tumult's `56364acf` |
| BIO-autotroph-physics | **`Autotroph` is documented but not implemented** — the class doc says a phototroph's basal rate is surface-limited so Kleiber's ¾ exponent does not apply; `allometry.rs` then gives it an endotherm's `B0` and pace multiplier, so both shipped autotrophs compute as warm-blooded animals of the same mass and no test says otherwise. The fix is a modelling call (an area exponent, a calibrated `B0`), unbundled from the roster expansion so it measures against a frozen roster. Generalises: a witnessed-but-unimplemented class is invisible to every test, because its witnesses compute *something*. | raw | med | [The Vacancy chronicle](../chronicle/the-vacancy.md) |
| BIO-kind-authoring-seam | **A kind is authored in six registries and validated in a seventh** — one species means rows in biosphere, family, and (if minded) psyche, society, perception, articulation/lexicon, plus a `family_proto` once a family label is carried twice, while the invariants binding them (`speech ⊆ perception ⊆ mind`, society ⟺ minded ∧ social) are enforced in `windows/worldgen`. Could a kind be one declaration that fans out, with invariants at the authoring site? Tension: the fan-out must not rebuild the god-struct the ECS program spent seven campaigns deleting — a builder or macro, never a re-fused type. | raw | low | [The Vacancy chronicle](../chronicle/the-vacancy.md) |
| BIO-marine-trophic-split | **Trophic structure within the sea** — the ocean ships with ONE axis standing for marine production and the prey web together, because one axis needs one calibration knob. The land resolves three levels; the sea resolves one, so a reef grazer and a pelagic apex predator differ only by condition curves, not by what they eat, and marine food-chain *length* cannot emerge. Splitting it into plankton / forage / prey axes would let the same Pianka overlap that separates terrestrial guilds separate marine ones. Cheap to stage: the basis is open, so this is new ids, never a reinterpretation. | raw | med | [The Vacancy chronicle](../chronicle/the-vacancy.md) |
| BIO-chemotrophy | **Chemotrophy as a fifth `MetabolicClass`** — a hydrothermal-vent community eats chemical gradients, not light, prey, detritus or minerals: the one genuinely distinct strategy the four-variant enum cannot express, and why the vent and hadal biomes are left unoccupied rather than filled with a mislabelled ectotherm. Widening that enum makes every reader a blast radius. The payoff is ecological independence: a chemotrophic biome's carrying capacity never traces back to the star, making it the one refugium no glaciation closes. | raw | med | [The Vacancy chronicle](../chronicle/the-vacancy.md) |
| BIO-supply-drowns-niche | **Supply magnitude drowns the condition niche** — a kind's carrying capacity per biome is dominated by the NPP-linked supply term, not its `ConditionNiche`, so a species authored for hot-arid desert can be genuinely present there and rank behind kinds with no desert affinity. `K = supply × Π condition` multiplies a term spanning orders of magnitude by one bounded in `[0,1]`, so the niche can only modulate, never select. Measured on a people authored for desert that has none. Remedies: normalize supply per biome, make condition an exponent, or redefine "centred" as a kind's own best biome. | raw | high | [The Vacancy chronicle](../chronicle/the-vacancy.md) |
| BIO-insolation-is-latitude | **`insolation` is latitude, not light** — the axis species author a light preference against is a pure function of latitude, with no canopy, cloud, or terrain-shadow term, so at a given latitude a rainforest floor and an open desert read identically. Several shipped niches describe shade the model cannot represent. It barely differentiates hot biomes, which is part of why the supply term wins. A real field needs vegetation attenuation (the biome classifier already knows canopy class) and ideally cloud from the shipped precipitation field. | raw | med | [The Vacancy chronicle](../chronicle/the-vacancy.md) |
| BIO-land-animal-drives | **The walk layer has no aquatic creature, and its drives say so** — every agent the vessel mints carries a freshwater thirst drive satisfied by pathing to drinkable water, so an agentified reef shark reads 0.94 thirst-caused distress and fires the health battery's alarm. Patched by excluding predominantly-marine kinds from the wild, which is a guard, not a model. The drive vector assumes every creature is a land animal; the same assumption waits for any aerial, sessile-mobile or ametabolic agent (a xorn does not drink either). A per-medium drive set is the general fix. | raw | med | [The Vacancy chronicle](../chronicle/the-vacancy.md) |

## The cultural-memory economy

Knowledge as a thing that must be *maintained*, and decays when it isn't —
the missing inverse of MAP-8. Cut by transmission channel so it stays
tractable; the spine is checkable, the generalisation is deliberately
deferred.

| ID    | Idea                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Status     | Conf             | Where                                                                                                 |
| ----- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------- | ---------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| MEM-1 | The memory economy — every retained fact carries an upkeep cost; held only while surplus × will exceeds it, else it melts fact → phenomenon → myth (the inverse of MAP-8); institutional death is a *symptom* of regress, not its cause (Bagnall); survival curves are a Lab metric                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | elaborated | med (spine)      | [frontier §cultural-memory](frontier.md#the-cultural-memory-economy--knowledge-as-a-maintained-thing) |
| MEM-2 | The floating gap — living communicative memory reaches ~3 generations, then a memory is institutionalised into cultural memory or lost (Assmann); the precise Alexandria moment, an era-tick event gating what historiography can recover                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | raw        | med              | [frontier §cultural-memory](frontier.md#the-cultural-memory-economy--knowledge-as-a-maintained-thing) |
| MEM-3 | Cultural stickiness — emotionally salient, minimally-counterintuitive content decays slower independent of institutional support (Sperber/Boyer/Morin); a per-item modulator making MEM-1's melt selective, and item 4's principled noise model                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | raw        | med              | [frontier §cultural-memory](frontier.md#the-cultural-memory-economy--knowledge-as-a-maintained-thing) |
| MEM-4 | Preservation as craft — a conservator institution spends surplus specifically to *lower* the decay rate of chosen items; the active-voice complement to MEM-1's rot, and the memory-side reading of MAP-3's liturgical damping field (diglossia, dead languages)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | raw        | med              | [frontier §cultural-memory](frontier.md#the-cultural-memory-economy--knowledge-as-a-maintained-thing) |
| MEM-5 | Institutions at large — an institution as a durable commitment of surplus to a function (memory/legitimacy/exchange/skill), each with a MAP-7-style founding→neglect→death lifecycle; memory is one cargo among many. The deliberate deferral: captured so it is not lost, scoped out of MEM-1 as the guard against generalising into worthlessness                                                                                                                                                                                                              | raw        | low (generalise) | [frontier §cultural-memory](frontier.md#the-cultural-memory-economy--knowledge-as-a-maintained-thing) |
| MEM-6 | Memory-cost inversion — a species that is itself a durable memory store, or whose memory-upkeep cost is ~zero (immortal perfect recall: the aboleth), inverting MEM-1's central equation; an outlier that halts the melt fact → phenomenon → myth for what it holds and stands as an archive others cannot afford — the un-forgetting complement to MEM-4's conservator                                                                                                                                                                                          | raw        | med              | [frontier §cultural-memory](frontier.md#the-cultural-memory-economy--knowledge-as-a-maintained-thing) |
| MEM-7 | Generation-length scales the memory economy — MEM-2's floating gap is pinned at a *human* ~3 generations / ~80 years, but that horizon is lifespan-relative: a long-lived species (elves) whose eyewitnesses outlive the institutionalisation window may never hand living memory off to cultural memory (the Alexandria moment never arrives), a short-lived one melts everything to myth within a lifetime. The whole MEM cluster's decay constants should be keyed to BIO-2 lifespan, not constant — EXP-5 wired this coupling into drama but not into memory | raw        | med              | [frontier §cultural-memory](frontier.md#the-cultural-memory-economy--knowledge-as-a-maintained-thing) |
| MEM-8 | **The artifact channel — MEM's missing fourth transmission channel.** Inscribed rots (the unpaid scribe), incorporated dies with the last practitioner, oral drifts into myth; a **magic item / cognitive artifact** carries the knowledge in the *object*, and its failure mode is unlike all three: **the knowledge does not decay — the capacity to make more does.** The instance survives; the recipe never does. So **"they don't make them like they used to" is derived, not authored** — ruins full of working relics nobody can replicate, with MAP-30's palimpsest supplying the ground they lie in. It also cashes UNI-1's one unpaid claim, roguelike identification: a magic item is a *cached derivation bound to an object*, so **possessing is not knowing**, and that gap **is** identification (an unidentified potion is a production you can trigger but cannot read; identifying it is inferring the derivation from the effects). Gives **three roads back to a lost lemma**, each with distinct economics and failure mode: *rediscovery* (experiment, UNI-1 inference; fails when phenomena are ambiguous), *comparative reconstruction* (LANG-5's shipped Neogrammarian cascade pointed at rites; fails when only one daughter tradition survives), *artifact reverse-engineering* (fails because the relic teaches nothing). Literature-checked off Frazer entirely: Hutchins's distributed cognition, Norman's cognitive artifacts. Distinct from MEM-6 (a *species* as the store) — here the store is an inert object | raw | med | ideonomy session 2026-07-16; [frontier §magic](frontier.md#magic-as-a-graph-grammar--the-debt-economy) |
| MEM-9 | **Suppression as craft — MEM-4 negated.** MEM-4 is an institution spending surplus *specifically to lower* the decay rate of chosen items; invert the sign and you get an institution spending surplus *specifically to raise* it — book-burning, guild secrecy, enforced illiteracy, the Sorcerer-King's templars, the index of prohibited works. Same survival-curve verification as MEM-1/MEM-4, opposite direction, and the same Lab readout measures both. Sharpens the memory economy from a story about *neglect* into one that also has an **antagonist**: Bagnall's inversion says the library was let go, but some libraries are *pushed*, and the two are distinguishable in a survival curve (uniform melt vs a selective cliff on one topic). Pairs with UNI-25's taxation posture — an issuer who monopolises a capability has a positive interest in its non-transmission, so suppression is not villainy but **portfolio protection**. What a culture pays to *destroy* is as much a portrait of what it fears as MEM-4's spending is of what it holds sacred | raw | med | ideonomy session 2026-07-16 |
| MEM-10 | **The abandoned project — MEM-1 pointed forward.** The memory economy is time's backward face; run its own equation the other way and **a plan is a fact about the future, and it has upkeep too**: a work is held only while surplus × will exceeds the cost of continuing it, and when the product falls the plan is not dramatically cancelled but *silently released*. **The unfinished cathedral is the forward face of the abandoned library**, and Bagnall's inversion transfers verbatim — *the fact that the cathedral was never finished shows the decline had already begun*. Yields a distinction MAP-30's palimpsest does not yet carry and which is legible in the ground: a **ruin of abandonment** (finished, used, then let go — evidence of a completed past) versus a **ruin of incompletion** (never finished — evidence of a *released future*), which say opposite things about the era that made them; the latter is also an anti-phenomenon in the strict sense, a meaningful absence. The rest of the cluster reflects too: MEM-2's floating gap → a **planning gap** (a work must institutionalise within ~3 generations or die), MEM-3's stickiness → **millenarianism** (minimally-counterintuitive futures outlive sensible five-year plans, same asymmetry pointed forward), MEM-4 → the chapter or dynasty that spends to keep a *future* alive. UNI-9's lich is the limiting case: the one agent who solves this problem by refusing to die | raw | med | ideonomy session 2026-07-16 |

## Alchemy — matter, and the doctrines about it

| ID     | Idea | Status | Conf | Where |
| ------ | ---- | ------ | ---- | ----- |
| ALCH-1 | **The Crucible — a material ground truth plus the mostly-wrong doctrines inferred over it.** A program, not a campaign: a substance/production layer the sim adjudicates, and practitioners who induce doctrines about it and are *materially wrong*. Carved into The Reagent (ground truth), The Signature (the agent fold, and the thesis), The School (culture-scale transmission), with a UNI-2-gated violation tier named but unscheduled. The fourth site of [[UNI-1]]'s one-mechanic thesis, after The Surmise, [[LANG-5]]'s comparative reconstruction, and the [[MEM-8]] cluster \| **Campaign 1, The Reagent, shipped**: the eight latent quality axes, the five manifest signs, the seven-production grammar with mass balance as its admissibility predicate, and the composition-root carry from terrain vocabulary — the domain drawing nothing, so no save-format contract. Later rungs: The Signature (the agent fold, and the thesis), The School (culture-scale transmission), and a UNI-2-gated violation tier named but unscheduled | shipped | high (structure) | [The Crucible metaplan](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-26-the-crucible-metaplan-design.md); [The Reagent spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-26-the-reagent-design.md); [The Reagent chronicle](../chronicle/the-reagent.md) |
| ALCH-2 | **The provenance confound — the error term is derived, never authored.** Strip alchemy's vocabulary and it is *induction from a spatially biased sample of a function whose true arguments are partly imperceptible*. So: a recipe works in one valley and fails in the next because the reagent's hidden property differs, and the doctrine names the wrong cause. **The world is heterogeneous; the alchemist is local — that is the entire error term.** Lithology, ore grade, and `ConditionNiche` already vary by place, so no falsehood is ever written down; author what is true and locality generates the error. The same move [frontier §magic](frontier.md#magic-as-a-graph-grammar--the-debt-economy) makes when it reads the violation catalog off the property batteries rather than off imagination — and, like it, the content grows with the sim's fidelity instead of an author's word count. **Shipped half, measured at close:** the confound reaches APPEARANCE but not BEHAVIOUR — ore grade moves causticity and causticity moves only `hue`, so poor ore looks different and behaves identically (no production requires causticity; sweeping every commodity across the full grade range leaves the admitted-production set invariant). That is a real doctrine-of-signatures error, but not the recipe-fails-in-the-next-valley claim above. Coupling grade to behaviour via a causticity-gated production is The Signature's first task (owner decision at G6) | shipped | high | [The Crucible metaplan](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-26-the-crucible-metaplan-design.md) |
| ALCH-3 | **Substances are quality bundles, not a catalogue.** A material is a bundle of authored qualities exactly as a phoneme is a feature bundle; an atomic substance list is a lookup table, i.e. the catalogue-not-a-language failure [[CLIENT-language-not-catalogue]] and The Hearth's pattern discipline both exist to prevent. This is why `phonology.rs` is the literal template — one authored inventory, a derived per-world selection, an admissibility predicate — and its third re-instantiation after the phoneme inventory and The Hearth's spatial patterns. Shipped in The Reagent as an eight-axis `QualityVector`, with `fixity` and `volatility` deliberately not forced complements so the decomposes-rather-than-survives region stays reachable | shipped | high | [The Crucible metaplan](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-26-the-crucible-metaplan-design.md) |
| ALCH-4 | **The mundane tier ships ungated; the metaphysics gate is one predicate.** [[UNI-2]] asks whether magic is a second physics; this program does not open it and does not need to, because [frontier §magic](frontier.md#magic-as-a-graph-grammar--the-debt-economy) already argues superstition is the universal and magic is the special case where superstition happens to be *right*. A world with an empty violation catalog still derives correspondence-thinking, false causes, rival schools, and lost arts. **A production is a production whether or not it violates an enforced invariant**, so opening the gate later changes `permits` and nothing else. Reserved discard: a world with *no* consistent material rules at all — alchemy genuinely impossible, yet schools still form — a coherent tier-0 draw | raw | med | [The Crucible metaplan §5.4](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-26-the-crucible-metaplan-design.md) |
| ALCH-5 | **Alchemy is religion's fallible sibling.** Surfaced by negating "the doctrine can be wrong": that branch yields practitioners with oracular access, which is *revelation, not inference*, and `domains/religion` already occupies it. Alchemy takes the inference branch — a practitioner who samples, and can therefore be wrong. The phenomena seam (a consumer must never learn which system produced an observation) is unchanged by the whole program, and no campaign in it may weaken it. **Admissibility is not truth**: a doctrine validator asks whether a doctrine accounts for the observations its holder has *made*, never whether it is correct — collapsing the two would delete the subject matter in one line | raw | high | [The Crucible metaplan §5.3, §5.6](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-26-the-crucible-metaplan-design.md) |
| ALCH-7 | **Latent qualities, manifest signs — the split that makes the confound possible.** A substance's qualities are *latent*: a practitioner never reads composition directly, but perceives **signs** — weak, partly misleading functions of the qualities — plus reaction outcomes. Surfaced by asking whether the idea is public or hidden, and it **overturned [[ALCH-2]]'s first mitigation**, which had required qualities to be *observable*: if they were, there would be no confound and the program would have no subject matter. The constraint is *inferable*, not observable — every quality must affect at least one observable. The payoff is that `hue` can be made a deliberately weak function of the quality vector, which mechanizes the historical **doctrine of signatures** ("the root is red, so it treats blood") as a derivable error rather than an authored superstition. Reserved knob, not adopted: drawing the quality→sign projection *per world*, which would make signatures genuinely right in some worlds — cheap and powerful, but arbitrary rather than derived. Historical anchor for the whole confound: **wootz steel**, a lost art whose recipe depended on trace vanadium in one ore body, which died when that body was exhausted and has never been reconstructed. Shipped in The Reagent: `hue = 0.50·causticity + 0.45·vitality`, so a caustic mineral and an inert living thing both present 0.45 exactly — pinned by a test carrying an instruction not to repair it | shipped | high | [The Reagent spec §2](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-26-the-reagent-design.md) |
| ALCH-6 | **Doctrine accuracy is an ordinal lab metric — and the scale runs past its own maximum.** Positions: *signature* (a cause with zero predictive power) → *confounded* (a real correlate mistaken for the cause) → *overreached* (right cause, wrong scope) → *unmodified* (missing dose/purity/temperature) → *true production* (indistinguishable from the sim's rule; this position is called "science") → **accreted**, past 100%: a doctrine naming *more* conditions than the truth has. Precision without accuracy, and the mechanism behind ritual accretion — **a doctrine can be worse than wrong by being over-specified.** Preregisterable hypothesis for The Signature: doctrine accuracy varies inversely with the terrain heterogeneity an alchemist can reach — flat worlds converge on truth, fractured worlds hold doctrines at *confounded* and proliferate schools. **Constraint measured in The Reagent, to settle BEFORE this preregisters:** reachable-production count is a weak discriminator by construction — four ubiquitous source categories cover the whole seven-entry table, so every default-pin seed reaches all seven and between-world variance is ZERO. The hypothesis is untestable if exposure is "the world's reachable production set." Two fixes: measure reach LOCALLY (a settlement catchment, not a globe), and use a finer measure — distinct material categories, or ore-grade dispersion, the latter being the confound's actual mechanism and exactly what a production-set measure discards | raw | med | [The Crucible metaplan §6](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-26-the-crucible-metaplan-design.md) |

Cache misses double as instrumentation: a stage whose true input set silently grew (the naming/hemisphere-culling merge collision) forces a miss and surfaces the hidden coupling. Companion dial: verification intensity decays with code stability (an edit resets a domain's clock to full; untouched domains dilute toward nightly) — risk tracks churn. Widened (ideonomy session, test system, 2026-07-11): the same scheduled leg should run the property batteries at 10–50× their gate seed counts and every `#[ignore]`d guard — seed-breadth generally, not only the census | raw | med | ideonomy session (workflow/tooling) |
| TOOL-23 | The cross-profile determinism sweep — generalize `cli/tests/release_determinism.rs` (today two hand-picked studies) into one property test sweeping N seeds across debug **and** release, so opt-level byte-divergence surfaces broadly rather than at two chosen points; the differential-testing widening of the debug/release cross-check TOOL-12 already leans on | raw | med | ideonomy session (workflow/tooling) |
| TOOL-24 | World-derivation performance — the recompute pattern. Determinism makes every derived view a pure function of `(seed, pins)`; the recurring perf defect is such a view **recomputed redundantly over immutable data**, and a profiling campaign this session (samply flamegraphs on the real CLI/lab/possess entry points) attacked it three ways, each **byte-identical by construction** (verified via `lens_purity` + artifact drift, not just unit tests): **dense storage** (*The Lookup* — `Geosphere::coord` cached per cell, `ConnectionGraph` `BTreeMap`→dense `Vec`; ~38% off genesis), **a finer index** (*The Bearing* — `NearestCellIndex` 2-D lat/lon window with a coverage bound measured from the mesh's longest edge + a `(band,CellId)` tie-key reproducing the band scan's exact winner; 2.9× the map render), and **scoped reuse** (*The Retainer* — `climate_of` re-derived terrain internally and `sky_report` rebuilt the whole world per glance, fixed with `climate_from`/`sky_report_from` seams that thread a session's already-derived providers; 2.4× `possess`). Standing discipline: **reject a global/LRU cross-world provider cache** — the census builds each world *once* (one-shot), so it gains nothing, risks OOM across thousands of worlds, and stands up a second source of truth against seed+ledger; reuse scopes to the *unit of work* (session for `possess`, within-world for the census). Contrast [[TOOL-12]]: the cross-world memo done *safely*, keyed by a source hash. **Open levers (`raw`):** (1) `vessel::liveness` / `RoomAddr::corner_weights` (~24% of `possess` — resolves a room's globe-level ancestor-triangle corners, which *are* exact mesh vertices, via a full `nearest` scan, redundantly per NPC/tick; **option 1** memoize by ancestor `(face, path[..globe_level])` — low-risk, Retainer-shaped; **option 2** a `VKey→CellId` exact index that skips the scan entirely, **blocked** because the geosphere indexes vertices by subdivision order while the room mesh addresses them by barycentric `VKey` and *the correspondence is not stored* — the only bridge today is `nearest_to_position` itself, so option 2 needs a core-`subdivide` change to track vertex keys: a high-risk kernel campaign for a fresh session); (2) **B1** precompute a pixel→cell render table per `(level, resolution)` — seed-independent, byte-safe, the regen's ~8 maps share one; (3) tighter latitude windowing (`nearest` is still ~45% of the map render); (4) allocation churn / the `.collect()` pattern (~17% of genesis self-time in malloc); (5) `build_world` returns a derived-provider bundle (the deeper Retainer reuse; touches the world-build contract); (6) a `sky_of` per-observation memo. **Parked:** *The Commons* — `Arc`-share the per-level-cached geosphere instead of cloning it into each provider (terrain side, ~3% census), spec'd but at an **unapproved G3**. The profiling how-to (samply + a `[profile.profiling]` build + `atos` RVA symbolication + the profile-the-tree-you-ship discipline) lives in session memory, not here. | raw | med | shipped precedents: [The Lookup](../chronicle/the-lookup.md), [The Bearing](../chronicle/the-bearing.md), [The Retainer](../chronicle/the-retainer.md); this conversation |
| TOOL-preflight-merge | The merge preflight — mechanize the checkable half of the parallel-campaign integration ritual as a read-only GO/NO-GO (foreign WIP and staged-index scan of the shared checkout, main-is-ancestor check, both-sides-added slug-collision scan across decisions/chronicle/retrospectives/studies and idea-registry row IDs per decision 0026; decision records are numbered again per decision 0043, so the next free decision number needs confirming at merge), printing the judgment half it cannot score (semantic collisions under clean merges, keystone-fixture refreeze from main's tip) as reminders; the launch-commit-criteria pattern re-instantiated | shipped | high | ideonomy follow-up session (boundary rituals); `scripts/preflight-merge.sh` (`make preflight`) |
| TOOL-hash-golden-net | The hash-golden determinism net — commit one CSV of SHA-256 hashes of `world.json` over ~1,000 seeds (worlds the censuses already build), regenerated and drift-checked like any artifact: mass byte-determinism coverage in the band between the seed-42 byte-goldens (deep, narrow) and the censuses (wide, but metric-projected); doubles as a save/load round-trip battery | raw | high | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11) |
| TOOL-drift-scan-probes | The drift-scan probe window — the staleness probes always regenerate seeds 0–2, so a worldgen change touching only seeds ≥ 3 slips to CI; key the probe window off the committed fixture bytes (deterministic, no wall clock, no git state) so successive fixture regenerations sweep different slices — the survey-telescope drift scan: coverage accumulated over time with no single run paying for it | shipped | med | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11); `windows/lab/tests/fixture_staleness.rs` |
| TOOL-census-correctness-batteries | The census-riding correctness batteries — two O(1)-per-world checks free-riding on worlds a census build already pays for: universal structural invariants (every world, cheap postconditions) and a pin-reaffirmation battery (every metered calibration pin still holds against fresh worlds, gap-recording where a pin has no live check). Designed as *Lab Performance* Stage 4 to ride the local `census-lands-drift` build; **parked at that campaign's close (2026-07-14)** because decision 0046 moved census regeneration off the local machine entirely (remote-only, `HV_CENSUS=1`), so there is no local census to ride. Still wanted; needs re-scoping against the remote-census world — ride the AWS regen, or ride the depth ladder's cheap shallow builds (MAP-25) instead | raw | med | [lab-performance spec §6](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-12-lab-performance-design.md); [Lab Performance chronicle](../chronicle/lab-performance.md) |
| TOOL-deep-time-soak | The deep-time soak — a scheduled long-horizon battery advancing `WorldTime` far beyond gameplay range (10^5–10^6 days) and asserting structural invariants hold (no NaN, phenomena still rank, ephemerides still close); time-depth is the untested axis, complementing TOOL-22's seed-breadth | raw | med | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11) |
| TOOL-pin-pair-atlas | The pin-pair atlas — pins are tested solo (TOOL-4 varies one at a time) but never in combination; hash almanacs over a pairwise covering array of pin combinations (manufacturing QC's combinatorial-testing move) so pin-interaction defects land as red tests — the emptiest branch in the current test tree | raw | high | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11) |
| TOOL-refusal-census | The refusal census — every committed study's refusal column reads zero because studies are designed to succeed; a study designed to REFUSE (physically impossible pin sets) pins the negative space: every documented `GenesisError` reason reachable, its message stable, refusal rates measured | raw | med | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11) |
| TOOL-stratified-seeds | Stratified seed sampling — censuses sweep seeds 0..N contiguously, conditioning every conclusion on the low-integer corner of seed space; new studies draw seeds as hash(i), scattered and decorrelated. New studies only: for a shipped census this is a fixture epoch (preregistered, suffixed) — never churn an existing study for it | raw | med | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11) |
| TOOL-exhaustive-enumeration | Exhaustive micro-enumeration — where a pin space is finite and tiny (rotation modes × moon counts × neighbor types), enumerate ALL of it at one seed instead of sampling: total certainty for a few dozen worlds, the cosmic end of the seed-count spectrum reached cheaply wherever domains are discrete | shipped | med | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11); `windows/worldgen/tests/pin_enumeration.rs` |
| TOOL-test-binary-consolidation | Test-binary consolidation — each integration-test file is its own process, so every binary privately rebuilds its `LazyLock` worlds and pays its own link; merging per-crate test files into one binary shares worlds across what are today process boundaries and cuts link time (the process-model reasoning that rejected nextest, applied in reverse) | raw | high | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11) |
| TOOL-hot-crate-opt | Per-package opt-level for hot leaf crates — `[profile.dev.package.<crate>] opt-level = 2` on the crates where tests burn CPU (language evolution, terrain math) buys release-grade test speed inside the debug gate while everything else keeps fast compiles; measured precedent: the censuses run ~5× faster in release. Gate on a byte-identity check first — TOOL-23 is the guard shape | shipped | high | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11); workspace `Cargo.toml` `[profile.dev.package.*]` |
| TOOL-suite-timing-ledger | The suite timing ledger — a committed per-test-binary timings table with statistical-process-control tolerance bands (not byte pins), regenerated like an artifact, so a runtime elephant fails the build at birth; the 209s branches sweep lived unnoticed for a day because nothing watches the suite's own wall time — the suite instruments the world but not itself. **First step shipped 2026-07-13:** `scripts/timed.sh` + `docs/timings.md` record wall/CPU per expensive run (`make timings` to review, `make rebaseline` auto-records) — a read-it-yourself ledger with a >1.5×-median creep flag; the build-failing tolerance-band gate is the remaining step | raw | high | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11) |
| TOOL-world-pool | The canonical world pool — every test funnels through world construction, the trust graph's highest-degree hub, yet each binary rebuilds the canonical worlds privately; a test-support API exposing the seed-42 worlds behind one `LazyLock` per binary institutionalizes the hub (the in-process complement of TOOL-12's cross-run cache) | raw | med | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11) |
| TOOL-gate-fast | The affected-only gate — map `git diff` paths to crates to dependents and run only that test subset during iteration (`make gate-fast`), the full gate unchanged at commit; mechanizes CLAUDE.md's test-scoping prose the way `make preflight` mechanized the integration ritual, and is the local half of TOOL-17's CI path filter | shipped | med | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11); `scripts/gate-fast.sh` (`make gate-fast`) |
| TOOL-failure-black-box | The failure black-box — on any calibration or property failure over a swept seed, a test-support helper writes the failing seed's `world.json` and the exact repro CLI line to `target/failures/` and prints the path (aviation's flight recorder); accelerates the debugging half of test time, which no runtime optimization touches | shipped | med | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11); `windows/lab/src/blackbox.rs` |
| TOOL-pin-isolation-battery | The generalized pin-isolation battery — astronomy and terrain hand-wrote their pin-isolation tests (a pin must consume the same draws as the unpinned path, a save-format contract); lift to a data-driven battery walking EVERY registered pin, so each future domain inherits its stream-order guard with zero new code | raw | high | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11) |
| TOOL-registry-derived-battery | The registry-derived contradiction battery — the concept registry already declares each predicate's contradiction and cardinality rules, but the tests for them are hand-written per predicate; generate the checks from the registry itself and run them over the committed census fixtures, so coverage grows with the registry for free (tests authored by the institution, not an individual) | raw | med | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11) |
| TOOL-unobserved-organs | The unobserved-organs report — offline coverage (`cargo llvm-cov`, never the gate) crossed against the metric catalog: which sim code does no census metric or phenomenon ever observe? Not for a coverage percentage — for finding organs the Laboratory has no instrument pointed at; the output feeds new metrics, not new tests | raw | med | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11) |
| TOOL-concept-ownership-vs-reference | Un-conflating *register* vs. *reference* in the concept registry — `ConceptRegistry::register_concept` does double duty as *claim ownership* and *ensure present*, so whoever registers a shared concept first "owns" it and registration becomes order-dependent (A1's roster must run `language` after every domain whose concepts it names — `stone`/`god`/`home`/biomes — or `language` claims them under domain "language" and the owner's later registration conflicts). A distinct `reference_concept(name)` primitive (assert-present, claim nothing; a dangling reference → a loud error) would make registration order-free and close a latent hole that exists today: a concept no domain owns is silently claimed by whoever merely lists it. Surfaced while building A1 (the Domain-trait roster); A1 shipped the minimal registration-order workaround and deferred the primitive. Post-[[PROC-16]] (The Correspondence) the primitive is renamed `register_manifest` (`register_concept` deleted), but it still conflates *claim ownership* with *ensure present*, so the order-dependence and the latent unowned-concept hole persist — the row stands | raw | high | A1 Domain-trait session 2026-07-12 (spec `2026-07-12-domain-trait-registry-design` §4, deferred) |
| TOOL-shallow-wide-census | The depth-laddered wide census — the census-as-data periodic grid predicts an empty cell at N=10k × live: a second canonical tier sweeping 10,000 seeds at shallow build rungs (terrain and below, where coasts-tuning proved 10k affordable) alongside the 1,000-seed full-depth census; `schema.json`'s per-metric `rung` field already says which columns such a tier could carry. Kin of MAP-25 (domain-scoped builds) and TOOL-22 (scheduled full census) | raw | med | census-as-data ideonomy session (2026-07-13); [census-as-data spec §7](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-13-census-as-data-design.md) |
| TOOL-census-leaves-git | The census leaves git — at cloud scale (a 10⁶-world census under the AWS toolkit) the artifact regime flips: rows move to object storage, git keeps `schema.json` + the `rows.fnv1a64` content hash as the committed contract; the census-as-data manifest format is written so this future needs no schema change, only new paths in the harness manifest | raw | low | census-as-data ideonomy session (2026-07-13); [census-as-data spec §4](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-13-census-as-data-design.md) |
| TOOL-persistence-class-tag | A **persistence-class tag on every piece of entity state**, default-deny and drift-checked, so a new datum cannot be added without declaring whether it is `COMMIT` / `FOLD` / `SALT` / `FRAME` / `STRUCK` ([[CLIENT-persistence-notation]]). Direct analogue of `tools/type-audit`, which already forces a `type-audit:` verdict tag on every primitive at a `pub` boundary and fails on anything untagged — and the same instinct as [[PROC-18]]'s `stream_labels!` compile-time fusion: make the drift *structurally* visible rather than remembered. The problem it forecloses is specific and near-certain without it: the two-tier position law is *emergent* from determinism plus cost, so its accidental version — a codebase where some state persists and some does not because nobody decided per-datum — is the default outcome of not stating it as a law. Cheapest while the entity-state surface is still small, which is now | raw | med | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 4, 2026-07-25 |
