## Rendering — the observation stack

The rendering-strategy spec
([2026-07-09](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-09-rendering-strategy-design.md))
places every render on a four-ring axis (text in process → committed
artifacts → scene descriptions → external clients) under decision 0022's
seam: sim emits data, clients render. Rows here are the speculative rings;
the PNG migration stays TOOL-6, and the star chart is constitutionally
promised work (§3.4), not speculation.

| ID       | Idea                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Status | Conf | Where                                                                                     |
| -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------ | ---- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| RENDER-1 | Scene-description protocol — `windows/scene`, semantic-only JSON over the query surface (what an observer sees, never how to draw it; no producer identity); cartographic pole (tile lattice — where "tilemaps" lives) and situated pole (observer at a place and time — the phenomena protocol serialized); schemas are save-format-class contracts. **The situated pole was BUILT 2026-07-26** (campaign *The Purview*, [[RENDER-4]]'s first rung): `scene/surrounds/v1`, the sixth scene kind and the first egocentric one — and the first to carry an *epistemic* field, since a situated document describes what an observer knows, not merely what is there | shipped | high | [scene-protocol spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-09-scene-protocol-design.md)        |
| RENDER-2 | In-book web viewer — the atlas: a TypeScript client under `clients/` (decision 0023), unit-tested, bundled to a committed `atlas.js`; five layers, hover readout, markers, pan/zoom over the committed scene JSON; sky-scrubbing awaits a situated-pole scene kind | shipped | med  | [atlas-viewer spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-09-atlas-viewer-design.md) |
| RENDER-3 | TUI world viewer — an external repo with its own deps, driving `hornvale scene` as a subprocess; browse maps, watch the sky animate in ASCII; the bridge between REPL and game                                                                                                      | raw    | med  | [rendering strategy, Ring 3](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-09-rendering-strategy-design.md) |
| RENDER-4 | Tilemap game view — the game lens's secondary render, focalized prose staying primary. Elaborated into the game-client atlas, which takes this row's prose-primacy caveat as its sharpest risk ([[CLIENT-prose-primacy]]). **First rung shipped** (The Purview): not the view but the protocol half — `scene/surrounds/v1`, RENDER-1's long-unbuilt situated pole: egocentric, knowledge-limited, over the ROOM MESH, placed by integer barycentric coordinates because compass bearings drift with latitude. Two findings: zoom is path truncation, and a base-face seam cell carries semantics but no coordinate | shipped (first rung) | med  | [The Purview chronicle](../chronicle/the-purview.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-24-the-purview-design.md); [the game client atlas](#the-game-client-atlas--the-multi-pane-program); [rendering strategy, Ring 3](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-09-rendering-strategy-design.md) |
| RENDER-5 | Live orrery — `scene/system/v1`, a new semantic-only scene kind for a system's orbital elements, and the orrery client (`clients/orrery/`, decision 0023) that computes a smoothly animated, interactive top-down system view from it plus `scene/tiles/v1`'s real terrain; the exocentric **system pole**, sibling to the situated pole the atlas still awaits; a two-language golden contract pins the client's ephemeris to the sim's calendar | shipped → superseded (Goldengrove) | high | [decision 0055](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0055-external-clients-consume-a-versioned-wasm-catalog.md); [goldengrove spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-goldengrove-design.md) |
| RENDER-6 | Reference-time name disambiguation — when one rendered document names two same-named settlements, the render layer qualifies each from its own site facts ("Ice-Home (taiga)" / "Ice-Home of the kobolds"); pure view logic behind the content→render seam, no save-format contract touched; the lazy-qualification pattern of natural languages (Newcastle upon Tyne) | raw    | high | [decision 0024](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0024-settlement-name-uniqueness-is-reference-time.md) |
| RENDER-7 | The anti-phenomenon (salient absence) — negate the phenomena layer (surface what *is*, salience-ranked) and get the read of meaningful *nothing*: the dog that didn't bark, the altar-shaped wear with no altar, the name a village won't say. A dictionary of absence Hornvale already computes but never surfaces — lacuna, the unremarked, the withheld (taboo, UNI-17), the forgotten (MEM-1), the not-yet (MAP-13), the never-was (UNI-1 counterfactual), the unsayable (LANG lexical gap), the silenced (projection layer / MAP-9), the vanished (MAP-7 endling / deep-time ghost town). Nearly-free mechanism: an absence is legible only against an expectation, and the fields ARE the prior while the ledger is reality — so a *salient absence = where the field-prior expects something the ledger lacks* (coarse-constrains-fine run as a difference, not a constraint), a lacunae query beside phenomena, rendered by the focalizer as felt gaps. Prose is the medium where absence lands hardest: the text game's home-field advantage. Lands with the-walk metaplan's Focalized Sight (Milestone 1) | elaborated | high (mechanics) | [frontier §anti-phenomenon](frontier.md#the-anti-phenomenon--meaningful-absence-as-an-observable) |
| RENDER-8 | The world-wasm catalog — hornvale as a versioned wasm library (seed+pins → scene JSON) for external clients; one catalog, many planetaria | shipped | high | [goldengrove spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-goldengrove-design.md) |
| RENDER-9 | The client has no ground-truth view — only registered lenses, and the HUD says which one you are wearing. The "realistic" render (bathymetric tint under water, biome colour on land) has no privileged claim to truth: it is a rendering choice that merely *looks* like a photograph, which The Lens demoted from a base case to a peer named `natural` in a registry where the temperature and plate lenses are its equals. This is the phenomena rule aimed at the view layer — a consumer must never learn which system produced a phenomenon; a viewer is never handed unmediated sight — and it makes the caption, not the picture, the load-bearing honesty: what a lens exaggerates or invents is stated, because nothing on screen is exempt from having been *chosen*. Open questions: whether `natural`'s name still implies a default it no longer is; whether the situated/prose pole inherits the frame (a focalized description is a lens too, and EXP-3 species perception would make that literal rather than metaphorical); and whether a lens should be able to declare its own provenance and fidelity the way the scene documents do | raw | med | [frontier §map](frontier.md#the-frontier-map); [The Lens chronicle](../chronicle/the-lens.md) |
| RENDER-10 | The purview is per-species — the chart's sense radius as a function of the observer's own perception ([[EXP-3]]/[[EXP-9]]), not a constant. *The Purview* ships radius as a fixed 4 rings with `Agent::perception` untouched beside it; an eyeless subterranean people, a scent-primary one, or an echolocator should each see a differently-shaped neighbourhood, which makes "how far can you see" a species trait rather than a render setting. The seam is already in place — the chart is a pure function of (position, radius, depth, knowledge) | raw | high | [The Purview chronicle](../chronicle/the-purview.md) |
| RENDER-11 | The map that is WRONG, not merely stale — *The Purview*'s fog has three states and its `unknown` is **absorbing**: nothing is ever forgotten, so a `remembered` cell is always a true record of what was there. [[MEM-1]] plugs in at exactly that absorbing state, and the interesting version is not decay but **error**: a remembered cell that disagrees with the ground, discovered only on re-entry. Belief-vs-truth already runs one layer down ([[UNI-16]], The Surmise), so the machinery exists; what is missing is letting a *player's* map be as fallible as an NPC's belief | raw | high | [The Purview chronicle](../chronicle/the-purview.md) |
| RENDER-12 | An NPC's own purview, rendered — The Surmise derives a creature's belief about where fresh water lies as a fold over the one ledger; that belief **is** a map, over the same room mesh the player's chart draws. Rendering it through the same lens makes it a debugging organ and an empathy organ at once, and it is nearly free: the chart already takes an arbitrary observer and an arbitrary knowledge set. The honest question it raises is whether a player should ever be *shown* one | raw | med | [The Purview chronicle](../chronicle/the-purview.md) |
| RENDER-13 | The non-spatial purview — the chart's machinery is *(an index, a neighbourhood over it, an epistemic state per cell, a noun catalog joining it to prose)*, and nothing in that is spatial. Point it at the social graph ([[SOC-9]]), the drive stack, or the calendar and the same lens draws a different kind of surrounding. Speculative, but it is the abstraction-lift of a thing now shipped rather than a thing imagined | raw | low | [The Purview chronicle](../chronicle/the-purview.md) |

### The orrery atlas — enrichment directions

A 2026-07-10 ideonomy pass over the then-shipped live orrery (RENDER-5:
`scene/system/v1` + `clients/orrery/`) — dimension-identification along
cardinality, visibility, and size, then combination against the sim's other
surfaces (the almanac, the atlas, the Lab, the SKY atlas above), captured as
a graph. The in-book client the pass targeted is gone: the [Goldengrove
spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-goldengrove-design.md)
retired the terminal and live orreries in favor of an external 3D client
over the versioned wasm catalog, which inherits every row below. The
graph's two hubs, which price every row: **the scene schema**
(about half the rows widen `scene/system/v1`, and any widening that adds
client-side physics must extend the two-language golden-sample contract that
pins the TypeScript ephemeris to the sim), and **the time axis** (the
scrubber owns one year; several rows contend for a second, longer clock).
Rows needing neither — pure consumers of data the client already holds — are
the cheapest real enrichment, and **Conf** reads as in the SKY atlas: *how
cheap and clearly-correct the work is*. IDs are category+slug per decision
[`0026-slugs-not-numbers`](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0026-slugs-not-numbers.md).

| ID | Idea | Status | Conf | Where |
| -- | ---- | ------ | ---- | ----- |
| ORRERY-sibling-planets | The system shows exactly one planet because genesis draws exactly one: draw sibling worlds (a scorched inner rock, outer giants) into the `StarSystem`, carry them in a widened scene kind, and render them on their own orbits — the naked-eye "wanderers" that found planetary astronomy, a second event family (conjunctions, retrograde loops) for MAP-18's computed knowledge | raw | med | this conversation; ideonomy session |
| ORRERY-any-world | The client is hard-wired to seed 42's two committed JSONs; accept a dropped or file-picked pair of scene documents so any generated world's system is viewable without rebuilding the book — the orrery becomes an instrument, not an exhibit. Pure client; no schema change | shipped | high | Goldengrove campaign |
| ORRERY-system-census | Small multiples — a Lab study sweeping seeds and emitting the system-elements table (or thumbnail frames) so the *distribution* of architectures (moon counts, HZ placement, lock fraction, year lengths) is visible at a glance rather than one seed at a time; the decision-0011 posture, studies are data | raw | med | this conversation; ideonomy session |
| ORRERY-ellipse-truth | `forcing.rs` holds live eccentricity but the orrery draws a circle traversed at uniform speed; carry eccentricity in the scene kind, draw the true ellipse (star at focus, perihelion marked) with Kepler-correct angular speed, and extend the golden contract so both languages agree on the anomaly | raw | high | this conversation; ideonomy session |
| ORRERY-deep-time-scrub | The v1 non-goal made real: a second, kiloyear time axis animating the Milankovitch triad the sim already computes (obliquity nod, eccentricity breath, precession swing), the globe's ice caps breathing via paleoclimate — two nested clocks, the year inside the epoch | raw | med | this conversation; ideonomy session |
| ORRERY-ephemeris-inspector | Hover or click a body → its orbital elements and instantaneous computed state (angle, illuminated fraction, days to next full); the latent numbers behind every pixel surfaced — the atlas's hover-readout pattern re-instantiated on the system view. Pure client | shipped | high | [The Helm spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-the-helm-design.md) |
| ORRERY-scrubber-events | The year scrubber is an unmarked ribbon; tick it with computed events (solstices, equinoxes, each moon's fulls, multi-moon conjunctions) so events are seekable and the scrubber becomes the world's almanac in miniature. Pure client — all periods and offsets are already in the document. **The Shadow Track shipped the first marks — dated eclipses on the scrubber; solstices/equinoxes/moon-fulls/conjunctions remain.** | raw | high | [The Shadow Track](../chronicle/the-shadow-track.md) shipped the eclipse subset; the rest raw |
| ORRERY-scale-honesty | The moon radial axis is schematic and silently so; add a true-scale toggle plus a printed compression legend ("moon distances shown at ×1/60") so the render admits its lie — and at true scale the moons vanish against the orbit's sweep, itself the lesson the page should teach. Blocked on a local-relief data feed beyond the 512-wide global equirect (regional high-res tiles re-request, or a new catalog call) — the first real widening of the wasm catalog under decision 0055's cross-repo contract discipline; The Helm's user-driven ladder is built to take the extra rungs | raw | high | [The Helm spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-the-helm-design.md) |

Imperceptible at seed 42 (ε≈0.96°). The starfield side is the contract-faithful one; reconcile the older world-axis sign against the producer's epoch/vernal-equinox convention, guard it with a `pole.z` assertion (today `system.test.ts` asserts only `pole.y`), and verify on a high-obliquity seed. Surfaced by The Real Sky's whole-branch review | raw | med | [The Real Sky chronicle](../chronicle/the-real-sky.md) |

Needs eccentricity in the locked model | raw | med | The Wandering Sun spec §7 |

The event is hours long against a year scrubber, so it needs a "jump to the eclipse + fine time rate" affordance to be watchable — the payoff is seeing the shadow actually move | raw | low | The Shadow Track spec §7 |
| ORRERY-lunar-shading | Render a lunar eclipse on the moon/system view — the moon visibly darkening as the anchor's shadow crosses it (Eclipse Seasons dates them; The Shadow Track marks them on the clock only, since a lunar event has no ground band). The system view already shades moons from real 3-D positions, so this is a shadow term on the moon's lighting at the eclipse | raw | med | The Shadow Track spec §7 |
| ORRERY-figure-lines | Draw the derived sky figures (SKY-12) as constellation lines on the real starfield backdrop — but `figures()` aggregates membership away (`Figure` carries `member_count` and a centroid, never member indices), so this needs a producer step first: surface per-figure member indices (a pure derived read, no draws), extend `scene/neighbors/v1` additively, then render the link edges client-side. The Real Sky ships the dots; this ships the pictures a culture actually sees | raw | med | [The Real Sky spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-17-the-real-sky-design.md) §7 |
| MAP-perceive-apertures | Look-direction perception through apertures — `look <dir>` renders a *neighbouring* locale as perception actually delivers it (a distance-attenuated, aperture-filtered **exterior** view — "to the north, past the tree-line, a darker wood, and faint water beyond") rather than reciting the room's first-person self-description (the MUD/ZIL vantage failure). Model: `perceived(feature) = carry(feature, channel) × transmit(aperture, channel) × falloff(distance)`, thresholded, confidence ∝ the margin above threshold — a spatial per-channel extension of the salience-`observe` fold, not new machinery. Four channels penetrate differently (a lattice): **sight** (blocked by occlusion/darkness), **light** (a glow through trees; enhanced at night), **sound** (bends around edges), **atmosphere** (weather/temperature — [[CLIM-felt-weather]]'s phenomena — pervade) — so *dense forest at night* yields hear+feel+glow but no sight, truer than any MUD. View = backdrop (pervasive features) + details (point features, if transmitted). Uncertainty is the currency that closes cognition: a hedged glimpse → low-confidence belief (the-surmise's tier) → the planner treats it as a hypothesis to verify (the-foresight targets belief) → a closer look self-corrects. The dormant `PerceptionVector` (night-vision) gets its first job (same aperture, different observer); *being seen is the dual of seeing* (stealth/exposure, later). A pure lens (decision 0022 — read-only, non-deterministic-OK, zero save-format cost). 2 ideonomy passes, 0 overturns. | elaborated | high (fun) | design companion (this session); [The Locale Window](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-12-the-locale-window-design.md) |
| MAP-connectors-as-apertures | Exits as derived-or-declared apertures, never imperative — replaces the ZIL/Inform `room \| function \| fail-message` union, whose `function` variant maximizes flexibility at the cost of navigability/transparency/mapping (an opaque closure you can't inspect, render, or reason over). Hornvale's line: **derive** (a pure function of the world — inspectable, explainable) or **declare** (authored data), never imperative code. The union collapses: `Room(dest)` → the aperture's destination attribute; `FailMessage(str)` → the *body* channel's transmission = 0 with a **derived Reason** ("sheer cliff", "flooded at this tide", "barred") the world explains; `Function` → deleted, its jobs split into derived transmission (conditional passability), a declarative `Portal` connector (computed destination, as data), and a separate action (side-effects). Load-bearing collapse: **an exit *is* an aperture — passability is the `body` channel of the same `transmit(aperture, channel)` [[MAP-perceive-apertures]] uses** (a wall stops body+sight; a river stops body not sight; a doorway passes both). A connection is a symmetric **edge** between two rooms (a Fact-shaped relation), not a room-owned slot; transmission is per-direction only where it differs (a cliff: descend-not-climb). Authored connectors are a **closed, registered vocabulary** (Door/Portal/Gate/Bridge…), bounded not open-ended (the closed-inventory pattern). **Opens a large unexplored area — buildings, vehicles, boats, interiors are all connectors + authored spaces** (the `ExitKind` extension point in `windows/locale` is pre-shaped for it); parked as too broad for the cognition work. Open sub-questions: derived-vs-stored (a derived aperture is a pure view; is an authored Door a Fact or dungeon data?); the connector vocabulary; the transmission function's terrain inputs. 3 ideonomy passes, 0 overturns (organon×negation → exit-as-edge, uniform-not-union, discover-by-default/invent-sparsely, bounded-complexity). | raw | high | design session (this conversation) |

### The game client atlas — the multi-pane program

A 2026-07-25 brainstorm plus two ideonomy passes (abstraction-lift and
dimension-identification, captured as an atlas; then substitution and
combination, captured as a timeline) over the question of a **multi-pane
browser game client** — focalized prose beside a top-down view of the
immediate room beside health/status — built on the Casement's wasm seam
([decision 0052](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0052-deploy-built-wasm.md)),
TS-authored and JS-shipped under `clients/` per
[decision 0023](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0023-in-repo-clients-carry-their-own-toolchains.md).
Herbert Wolverson's Rust roguelike tutorial supplied the feature checklist
the second pass crossed against Hornvale's own mechanisms
([[CLIENT-coverage-matrix]]).

Costs were **measured before designing** (native release plus a Node bench
over the Casement ABI, one box, medians): a game-scale turn tick costs
**4.75 ms** in wasm, a movement turn 1.15 ms, a `locale` room describe
0.010 ms native — against a 16.7 ms frame. Frame rate is a non-question: the
sim sits in a Worker, off the frame thread, and is turn-driven, so it
contributes zero frame time. Two numbers do bind. A *simulated day* costs
516 ms, and integrating at six-second granularity costs 68,386 ms per
simulated day against bulk advance's 516 — a 130× penalty for resolution
that reproduces [[UNI-32]]'s presence-induces-integration thesis from the
cost side, unprompted ([[CLIENT-four-clocks]]). And cold start is 3.6–5.0 s,
the one red budget ([[CLIENT-cold-start]]).

Three hubs price every row. **The two-tier position law**
([[CLIENT-two-tier-position]]) is load-bearing over all of it and is
Nathan's, not the passes': an entity's *persisted* position is its room; a
tile coordinate exists only inside the presence bubble and is never
serialized. **The snapshot contract** ([[CLIENT-one-snapshot]]) prices the
panes — nearly every row is a pane, and a pane is a pure projection of one
per-turn emit, so the schema is the only thing expensive to get wrong. And
**authority** ([[CLIENT-room-grid-authority]]) settles what the sim must emit
versus what the client may invent, which is where this program stops being
"a client" and becomes producer-side work.

Scope resolves into three nested rungs with three representations, not the
single "room scale" the first pass assumed: the **settlement** carries a
creature's home range (persisted — the goblins who do not wander out of the
village), the **room** carries `agent-at` and is the *commit granularity*,
and the **tile** is the transient bubble interior that is never persisted.
`RoomAddr { face, path }` already spans the top two — a "room" is a leaf, a
"floor" or chunk is a path prefix at some depth — so the ambiguity is in the
word, not the model ([[CLIENT-tiles-as-deeper-rooms]] asks whether the tile
is simply a longer path). **Conf** reads as in
the SKY and orrery atlases: how cheap and clearly-correct the work is. IDs
are category+slug per decision 0026's still-standing registry provision
(0043 superseded only its decision-record clause).

| ID | Idea | Status | Conf | Where |
| -- | ---- | ------ | ---- | ----- |
| CLIENT-one-snapshot | **One structured session emit per committed turn; every pane a pure function of it.** The blocker on any map or HUD pane was a producer-side emit, not JS. The borrow is database MVCC snapshot isolation: independent per-pane queries ship a bug class — a map from turn T beside stats from turn T+1 — and one snapshot per turn makes pane incoherence impossible by construction. A new pane then costs zero API surface. This is [[UNI-20]] re-instantiated one ring outward. **Shipped as `vessel/session/v1`**, provenance discharged structurally by channel grouping rather than per-field tags | **shipped** | high | [The Snapshot](../chronicle/the-snapshot.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-25-the-snapshot-design.md); brainstorm + ideonomy pass 1, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-redaction-panes | **Panes partition by knowledge source, not by data type.** A roguelike client's job is as much to withhold as to show, so the pane set is a redaction boundary: prose shows what the agent *knows*, tiles what it *senses*, status what it *feels*; a world-truth pane is a *cheat* pane. "Add an inventory pane" becomes the answerable "which epistemic channel is this?", and the reader-relative Book becomes a player-relative UI. **The boundary now exists but the panes do not:** `vessel/session/v1` partitions the emit by channel, so a pane cannot read outside its own — yet there is still exactly one | **shipped (floor: the schema's channel grouping; no panes yet beyond the transcript)** | high | [The Snapshot](../chronicle/the-snapshot.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-25-the-snapshot-design.md); brainstorm + ideonomy pass 1, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-alive-map | **The map pane as the agent's own cartographic artifact, not a truthful view.** An *animacy* substitution on the client (mechanical / informational / quasi-alive / alive) applied to the map pane: a mechanical map is the truthful top-down grid every roguelike draws; an **alive** map is a partial, mis-remembered, annotated artifact the possessed agent made. [[MAP-60]] The Idioms already committed to the premise that every render style "carries an implied maker/era" — nothing in that row anticipated that the implied maker could be **the player's own agent**, which is available here and nowhere else. Independently resolves [[CLIENT-prose-primacy]]: an alive map does not compete with prose for ground-truth authority because it *is not* ground truth — it is a second focalized voice from the same agent. The convergence of a polarity flip and an animacy substitution on one answer, from unrelated operators, was the strongest signal across both passes. Descendant of [[MAP-60]]; composes with [[CLIENT-redaction-panes]] | raw | med | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 2, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-prose-primacy | **A permanently visible map pane demotes prose, through layout rather than through any ratified decision.** **RESOLVED (Amendment 1, 2026-07-25) by adopting [[CLIENT-relational-fine-layer]] + [[CLIENT-alive-map]]:** prose and picture become two renderings of ONE relational structure, so they cannot compete as rival accounts, and the map carries no truth claim (it is the agent's remembered artifact). The resolution came from restating the interface's PURPOSE — substrate for linguistic / economic / behavioural complexity, not tactical play — which showed the grid was answering an unasked question. [[CLIENT-discovery-pane]] is additive, not a resolution; [[CLIENT-as-instrument]] remains open. The polarity flip on "more panes, more legibility": Constitution §3.5 makes focalized prose primary and the tilemap secondary, and [[RENDER-4]]'s own row says so in as many words — yet every roguelike player's eye goes to the grid, and layout is not a preference the constitution can enforce. This is a constitutional-ordering flag, not an autopilot-resolvable question; it leads the spec-review package. **Four candidate resolutions, not exclusive**, in ascending order of how structurally they solve it: [[CLIENT-discovery-pane]] (a non-spatial pane a tilemap cannot cannibalize — the positive-analogue move, since Gunkel's *proseases* says the positive analogue of fog-of-war is not knowledge but **discovery**); [[CLIENT-alive-map]] (the map is another focalized voice, so it is not competing for truth); [[CLIENT-as-instrument]] (an instrument's panes compete for the *developer's* eye, and the constitutional ordering is about the reader's); and [[CLIENT-relational-fine-layer]], the only one that resolves it **by construction** — if the fine layer is relational rather than Cartesian, the prose pane and the tile pane render one structure two ways and neither can cannibalize the other | raw | high | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 2, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-action-clock | **Per-agent action cost — the one genuine gap in twelve.** Crossing Wolverson's subsystem inventory against Hornvale ([[CLIENT-coverage-matrix]]) found eleven cells answered and one hole: nothing scheduled time at action granularity. **Shipped — and that statement was too strong.** The true gap was three of five actions being free, the one cost being uniform across creatures, and the jump machinery unowned. Cost is `base × tempo × terrain` in integer ticks — quantize-at-emit applied to time. Tempo is allometric in body mass, which corrected the spec's own first answer of metabolic class | shipped | high | [The Action Clock chronicle](../chronicle/the-action-clock.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-26-the-action-clock-design.md) |
| CLIENT-four-clocks | **Four clocks, not two, and the resolution penalty that forces them.** Frame (continuous, JS, sim-free), turn (episodic, wasm, 4.75 ms measured no-op floor), bubble (fine ticks, 68,386 ms per simulated day), world (sampled, 516 ms per simulated day in bulk advance). The *cyclicity* substitutions test each: making the render episodic — redraw only on a committed turn — is the classic terminal roguelike and is nearly free, which frees the whole frame budget to **animate the last turn's result** rather than simulate ([[MAP-23]]'s "keyframes from the sim, life from the client", worth considering as the default rather than the fallback); making the sim continuous forfeits the turn⊥frame decoupling the cheapness rests on; making the world clock continuous costs 68,386 ms per simulated day everywhere. So the 130× penalty is the arithmetic proof that coarse-constrains-fine is load-bearing here and not aesthetic — [[UNI-32]] arrived at from the cost side. **Caveat:** 4.75 ms is the *no-op* floor (at six seconds the session reports "Time passes; the world keeps its shape" — the NPC layer's effects are day-grained), so the floor rises by an unmeasured amount once per-tick behaviour exists. Re-measure, do not extrapolate. **Per-tick behaviour now exists** (*The Action Clock*, 2026-07-26: every action costs, agents interleave on a queue), so the caveat's trigger has fired and **the turn-clock re-measurement is OWED and not yet done** — it wants a session-level benchmark nobody has built. What that campaign *did* measure is the world clock's neighbour, not this one: the health battery, the longest sim in the suite, ran 250.65 s against a 266.36 s baseline, so the restructure of the sim's hottest loop cost nothing in bulk advance. That is evidence the 130× penalty argument is undisturbed; it is **not** the no-op floor, and substituting it here would be exactly the extrapolation this caveat forbids | raw | high | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 1–2, 2026-07-25; trigger fired by [The Action Clock](../chronicle/the-action-clock.md) |
| CLIENT-room-grid-authority | **The intra-room tile grid is producer-side, and that is what makes this a program rather than a client campaign.** **RATIFIED and sharpened (Amendment 1, 2026-07-25): DERIVED GEOMETRY IS CAUSAL.** Incidental relations produced by the placement solve may gate outcomes — a creature is genuinely concealed because the solver put a pillar between it and the guard, and that is the point. Accepted cost: the placement algorithm becomes a determinism contract needing an EPOCH to change. History is protected because facts are committed when geometry matters (a conversation happened; these three could hear it), so worlds are reproducible WITHIN an epoch, not across one — and across a layout epoch, history survives while remembered places rearrange. The *autonomy* axis placed a question nobody had asked, though its first answer was wrong: the original argument was that creatures are ledger entities carrying positions and so must stand on an authoritative grid — which [[CLIENT-two-tier-position]] dissolves, since they stand authoritatively on *rooms* and their tile coordinate is never persisted at all. The conclusion survives on a different and firmer footing: **the sim adjudicates play on the grid.** Whether you can close with a creature this turn is an outcome, and outcomes belong to the sim. That distinction is load-bearing downstream — a purely decorative client-side grid would be admissible, an adjudicating one would not. [[MAP-23]] already carved the sanctioned autonomy band (eyecandy may be freely non-deterministic provided it never re-enters the ledger), so the client's remaining latitude is **decoration with no positional authority**: residue, weather particles, torch flicker, tweening between committed turns. The cost to state plainly is that the room grid, the object graph, and the creature-binding are all workspace work with drift-checked goldens — the JS is the small end. Governed by [[RENDER-1]]/0022 | raw | high | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 2, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-derived-builders | **BSP, cellular automata, and WFC as *derivation* are admissible; only hand-placed intent is refused.** Reading decision 0022 as "no procedural map builders" would be the wrong lesson: a builder that is a deterministic function of pins plus noise, seeded from the room's own identity, is exactly the derive-never-declare move `windows/locale` already makes for room description — the refusal is of *authored layout*, not of the technique. Distinguishes this program from Wolverson's Section 2 (a chain of map builders expressing designer intent), which stays refused, while keeping its algorithms available. Sits beside [[MAP-connectors-as-apertures]]'s derive-or-declare-never-imperative line | raw | med | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 2, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-room-paging | **Rooms are demand-paged, and a player pacing a boundary thrashes the cache.** The lifted shape of the whole client is *a narrow port onto an authoritative lazy generator, materializing space on access and integrating finely only where the observer is* — which is **virtual memory**, and the borrow brings page-fault cost, prefetching, eviction policy, working-set tuning, and **thrashing**, a failure mode no row had named. Hornvale already holds two of the answers: [[MAP-70]] The Excursion's hot-ring/warm-halo two-radius eviction policy is the eviction policy, and its eager neighbour-ring fetch is the prefetch. Cheap by measurement — a room describe is 0.010 ms native, so a 21×21 room neighbourhood is ~10 ms in wasm (inside one frame, uncached) and a 41×41 ~50 ms (once per region, cacheable) — so deriving the map from pins and noise rather than reverse-engineering it from prose is both the architecturally right call and the cheap one | raw | high | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 1, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-coverage-matrix | **Wolverson's roguelike tutorial is a coverage checklist, never an architecture template — and the cross-product is the artifact.** Twelve subsystems crossed against Hornvale's mechanisms, each composite named: projection-as-FOV (FOV is the spatial special case of the already-designed lossy perception projection — no new machinery); derived hunger (the homeostatic drive is a fold over the ledger, not a counter — shipped); identification-as-inference ([[UNI-1]]/[[MEM-8]], better than a boolean); derived encounter (who is there is who lives there — no spawn tables, and the place Hornvale beats the tutorial outright); save-as-seed-plus-marks ([decision 0007](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0007-seed-is-identity.md) and The First Mark's played-world fold — saves are tiny); body-derived equipment slots (morphology decides slots); apertures-not-stairs; client-side residue (already licensed by [[MAP-23]]); a clash on fast travel (town portals presume the `enter`/`exit` scale seam [[UNI-37]] holds deliberately shut); map builders per [[CLIENT-derived-builders]]; and the single hole, [[CLIENT-action-clock]]. Four architectural inversions to refuse explicitly: in-engine rendering (bracket-lib draws from inside Rust; 0022 is the inverse, and the tutorial's own wasm chapter ships engine plus renderer together), mutable-ECS-as-truth against [[UNI-20]], rectangular `Vec<TileType>` maps with a stairs-down loop against the cube-face room mesh, and alignment-flavoured content against [decision 0021](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0021-no-alignment-axis.md). The matrix itself belongs in the repo | raw | high | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 2, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-discovery-pane | **A pane that renders coming-to-know rather than state.** The positive analogue of a fog pane is not a knowledge pane but a **discovery** pane: what the agent has inferred, and what observation would discriminate between its live hypotheses — [[UNI-1]]'s inference-over-a-hidden-seeded-ruleset thesis given a UI. Non-spatial, so a tilemap cannot cannibalize it, which is why it is one of the two candidate resolutions to [[CLIENT-prose-primacy]]. Gated on the inference tier (belief exists as a derived view since The Surmise; hypothesis sets do not) | raw | low | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 2, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-cold-start | **Cold start is the only red budget: 3.6–5.0 s in wasm, and no client trick fixes it.** Measured native, the split is informative — `hornvale new --seed 42` is 0.53 s, while `possess --seed 42` to first prompt is 1.75 s, so roughly 1.2 s is *session start* rather than genesis (the seam The Retainer had already begun on, with provider reuse and `sky_report_from`). Three levers in precedence order: the `BuildDepth` ladder already exists (Astronomy / Terrain / Settlements / Full) and no client path uses it selectively; the session-start work itself; and the diegetic option — **genesis narration as the loading screen**, so four seconds becomes content instead of latency, which is free and the most Hornvale-ish of the three. Related: the Casement's wasm has grown past its own documented 1 MB raw ceiling (1184 KiB / 430 KiB gzip at `opt-level = "z"`), with no automated check enforcing it | raw | high | brainstorm 2026-07-25; [Casement spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-13-the-casement-design.md) |
| CLIENT-turn-cost-ratchet | **Profile as a ratchet, not a phase — and gate a ratio, not milliseconds.** `scripts/timed.sh` already exists and its own header states the lesson ("the suite instruments the world but never watched its own wall time"; a suite crept 65 s → 43.5 min unnoticed), while deliberately never gating the build because wall times are machine-specific. That posture rejects both "profile before the campaign" and "profile alongside it": record per-turn costs in the timing-ledger shape so creep is visible, and gate only a **self-normalizing ratio** measured within one run — turn cost against a baseline operation in the same process — since a ratio survives machine and load where milliseconds do not. The Retainer is the evidence that review-caught regressions do not scale across a tremendously expanded API. Sibling of [[PROC-18]]'s compile-time-fusion instinct: make the drift structurally visible rather than remembered. **The ratchet half shipped 2026-07-28 (The Sextant), the ratio half did not.** A committed profiler shaped like the client's session (`windows/scene/examples/profile_scene.rs`), falsification ceilings in the full gate (`cli/tests/scene_cost.rs`), and a `scene-profile` row in the append-only ledger — with the asymmetry this row asks for written into each constant's doc comment: lowering is free, raising is an explicit reviewed act recorded with its reason, because `graph_cost.rs`'s own upward re-baselines were each correct and the mechanism still only travels one way. What shipped nevertheless **gates milliseconds, not a ratio**, which is this row's open remainder: the ceilings sit ~2× above measured, and `graph_cost.rs:313-333` records a 6.6× swing for identical work under parallel load on this box — precisely the machine-and-load sensitivity a self-normalizing within-run ratio would remove. The finding that motivated it belongs to the profiler's *workload* rather than its statistics: 91.6% of a scene call is redundant re-derivation, visible only because the workload repeats calls | shipped (ratchet half) | med | brainstorm 2026-07-25; [The Sextant chronicle](../chronicle/the-sextant.md) |
| CLIENT-scribe | **The alive client — the interface as a diegetic entity.** The far end of the *animacy* substitution: not a pure function from snapshot to pixels but a witness with its own knowledge and its own errors — a scribe or familiar whose panes show *what it recorded*, which may lag, decay, or editorialize. Hornvale holds the machinery (the knowledge ledger, belief as a derived view, [[LANG-39]]'s doctrine filter-stack) so the scribe's distortions would be derived rather than authored. The quasi-alive midpoint is cheaper and already interesting: a pane that **fades what it has not re-seen** is doing epistemic work, not decoration. Generalizes [[CLIENT-alive-map]] from one pane to the frame around all of them; the self-writing Book [[LANG-35]] is its sibling on the prose side | raw | low | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 2, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-two-tier-position | **An entity's persisted position is its place; a finer coordinate exists only inside the presence bubble and is never serialized.** Nathan's model, and the load-bearing idea of the whole program: `Position(Room)` is the committed truth; `(x, y)` serves rendering, collision and pathfinding, and only while the player is there. Quantize-at-emit applied to space. **Now tested, not argued** — The Blocking gave a chamber a cell lattice and intra-chamber `go`, and asserts over the whole ledger that walking a room commits nothing, so "byte-identical BY CONSTRUCTION" carries a check | ratified (0069) | high | [decision 0069](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0069-fine-position-is-never-serialized.md); [The Blocking chronicle](../chronicle/the-blocking.md) |
| CLIENT-persistence-notation | **The persistence law, as a notation with five tiers — and it belongs in the spec verbatim, because the alternative is deciding it per-datum by accident.** Every piece of entity state declares which tier it lives in, what keys it, what its derivation reads, and how long it lasts: `COMMIT` (a ledger fact), `FOLD` (derived by folding the ledger — drives, affect, belief, knowledge, inventory), `SALT` (derived from stable identity, never stored yet stable forever), `FRAME` (transient, this turn only), `STRUCK` (removed by an explicit committed event). Thirteen data fill cleanly from shipped code; the two that would not fill were the findings ([[CLIENT-vitality-folds]] and [[CLIENT-action-clock]]). The tier split has a four-century-old prior vocabulary: theatrical **stage management** keeps a *script* (canon), a *prompt book* whose blocking is explicitly non-canonical annotation re-derived every production, and a *performance* — mapping onto ledger / derived views / this session's frame. Six of our concepts already have better names there: an actor's **mark** is the stable per-entity derived position, the props **preset** is exactly `SALT (object, room)`, **strike** is the STRUCK tier, **calling the show** off a cue sheet is the action clock, and an **understudy** is [[UNI-18]]'s transmigrating soul. Adopt the vocabulary | raw | high | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 4, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-vitality-folds | **Wounds commit; health folds. There is no HP counter anywhere.** The persistence notation's second unfillable slot, and possibly the larger of the two: a roguelike is substantially *about* damage, and Hornvale has no vitality model at all — no HP, no wounds, no condition. It is also the classic mutable-state-as-truth datum every ECS tutorial stores and mutates in place, so it is exactly where the architecture is likeliest to be violated by reflex. The notation determines the answer, since every other derived row is a fold: vitality is a function of the body's own constitution ([[BIO-1]]), the injury facts in the ledger, and elapsed healing time. That makes it the **fifth** instance of one pattern (drive = fold, belief = fold, affect = fold, inventory = fold, health = fold), and it inherits [[CLIENT-two-tier-position]]'s best property free — a wound is keyed to the entity and placeless, so it survives room transitions exactly as grievance does. The roguelike's most central mechanic therefore needs no new architectural concept, only a body model for the constitution term | raw | high | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 4, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-relational-fine-layer | **The fine layer as relations, not coordinates** — **ADOPTED (Amendment 1, 2026-07-25).** **REALIZED as an ANCHOR GRAPH (The Hearth, 2026-07-25): `windows/vessel/src/interior/`.** The fine layer is the coarse layer one scale down — rooms:ways :: anchors:relations — so within-room movement is a new `SearchSpace` over the EXISTING kernel planner (`kernel/src/astar.rs`, integer costs, UNI-19), needing **no lattice and no coordinate solve at all**. Vocabulary BORROWED not invented: RCC-8, whose eight relations are jointly exhaustive and pairwise disjoint with a published composition table — JEPD demanded as a criterion because a partition cannot be padded. Unimplemented composition entries return the UNIVERSAL set (a sound over-approximation) rather than a plausible guess. Anchors emit FIELDS reusing `alarm_field`'s emitter-sum shape, decaying over GRAPH distance (16× across a room). Ships INERT: no room derives an interior and no creature has an anchor position, so every live site passes `None` and the world is byte-identical — the follow-up campaign **The Threshold** makes it live. The activities the interface exists for (talking, letters, magic research, language learning, artifact reverse-engineering) need audience, privacy, reach, light, duration and SOCIAL SIGNIFICATION from space; not one is metric. Coordinates are not abolished, only demoted: relations are truth, the grid is SOLVED from them for display and local movement. The mapping is asymmetric — relational→grid is a solve, grid→relational a classification — so the round trip is not the identity and INCIDENTAL relations appear that no one asserted (accidental cover, blocked sightlines). Those are causal by owner decision; see [[CLIENT-room-grid-authority]]. — `near(hearth)`, `between(door, table)`, `behind(pillar)` — the *materiality* substitution pushed to its abstract end. Three properties the Cartesian version lacks: it is how prose already describes space; it is directly narratable, so the prose pane reads the fine layer instead of ignoring it; and it needs no lattice, no quadtree-neighbour problem, and no legibility tuning ([[CLIENT-choreographer]] becomes moot). Worse for tactical play, better for everything the Constitution privileges — and the only resolution to [[CLIENT-prose-primacy]] that works *by construction*, since prose and tiles would render one relational structure two ways. The opposite substitution is foreclosed rather than merely unattractive: continuous coordinates with real physics in the bubble is a chaotic forward-integrator seeded from quantized state, which the kernel's Lorenz guard-rail explicitly forbids — the fine layer's discreteness is protective, not a simplification. Sits beside [[MAP-perceive-apertures]] and the text-adventure lineage | raw | med | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 4, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-as-instrument | **An instrument that happens to be playable, rather than a game that happens to be inspectable.** Promoting a side effect to the main effect: the client's unbidden consequences are that it becomes the de facto way anyone *sees* Hornvale (competing with the Book, which [decision 0059](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0059-the-book-is-the-primary-artifact.md) makes primary), a shareable seed-URL performance surface, a second consumer raising the cost of every producer change — and that **it makes the sim's gaps visible, because a pane with nothing to show is an indictment.** Promote the last and three debts clear at once: the *teach* purpose an earlier pass left dangling, [[CLIENT-prose-primacy]] (an instrument's panes compete for the developer's eye), and the build order (the first pane should be whichever most exposes what the sim cannot yet do). Direct precedent: the Orrery shipped as an exhibit and then drove four campaigns of "what does the sim actually look like" — [[MAP-66]] The Massing's honest-voxel finding, that a renderer should draw one block per datum rather than invent a sub-cell gradient the sim never computed, is an instrument's discovery, not a game's | raw | med | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 3, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-tile-view | **The tile view and the room-graph map are two panes at two scales, and conflating them mis-prices both.** The *tile view* is one place plus whatever its apertures admit sight through — fine, tactical, the roguelike view proper. The *room-graph map* is many places, coarse, for orientation, and it is what the brainstorm's numbers actually priced; do not quote the second's figures for the first. **The Blocking shipped the first tile view** — a 1:1 ASCII floor plan of a structure's chambers, drawn through a verb, embedded in ~9 µs release. Sight through apertures is The Sighting's | shipped (the plan; sight is next) | high | [The Blocking chronicle](../chronicle/the-blocking.md); [The Blocking spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-28-the-blocking-design.md) |
| CLIENT-tiles-as-deeper-rooms | **Is a tile simply a longer `RoomAddr` path?** **REJECTED (The Rose Window Amendment 1, 2026-07-25).** Not for the quadtree-neighbour cost, which is survivable, but because a tile would then be the SAME RUST TYPE as a room — so a tile position would be expressible wherever a room position is, including `AGENT_AT`, and [[CLIENT-two-tier-position]]'s "byte-identical BY CONSTRUCTION" would degrade to by-policy (risk 4, persistence-tier drift, goes from impossible to merely policed). A distinct fine-layer type makes the law compiler-enforced. Affordable, elegant, and still wrong: it spends the one type distinction the position law rests on. `RoomAddr { face: u8 (0..20 icosahedral faces), path: Vec<u8> (child index 0..4 per refinement) }` is an icosahedral quadtree, packed into `RoomId`'s u64 as a frozen save-format contract with room for ~29 digits. So "one more level of refinement" is just a longer path, and the two-tier position model would collapse into **one level at two depths**: commit a *truncated* address, derive the remaining digits — literally quantize-at-emit applied to space, which is the discipline [[CLIENT-two-tier-position]] already invokes by analogy. Not free: a quadtree leaf is not a roguelike lattice (which wants 4-/8-connectivity), and neighbour lookup across parent boundaries is the fiddly case — [[MAP-70]] The Excursion hit exactly this at face boundaries and deliberately punted on cross-face adjacency, with corners (three faces meeting) harder than edges. Worth a real look before the grid is designed, not an assumption either way. **The rejection stands, and a distinction it did not draw matters (2026-07-27):** what is refused is making the *never-serialized* fine layer a `RoomAddr`. A **chamber** is a different thing — a committed place, `COMMIT`-tier, which is a room in every sense and is simply deeper ([[CLIENT-scale-bands]], [[CLIENT-depth-follows-content]]) — so committing a deeper address does not spend the type distinction, because the never-serialized cell layer inside it stays a distinct type. The two-tier law survives compiler-enforced, and gains a consequence: movement *within* a chamber is `FRAME`, movement *between* chambers is `COMMIT`, which hands the action clock its granularity for free. This is not a reopening of the rejected row; it is the observation that the row and the chamber question are about two different tiers | raw | med | brainstorm 2026-07-25; distinction drawn 2026-07-27 (The Blocking) |
| CLIENT-choreographer | **Determinism is not legibility: the fine-layer placement derivation needs an aesthetic criterion, and this project has a documented habit of shipping the first without the second.** Theatre authors its blocking by hand; Hornvale must derive it, so something must decide what makes a derived blocking *good* — readable, plausible, uncrowded, not every creature standing on the hearth. **The criterion, taken from the same analogy (Amendment 1, 2026-07-25): SIGHTLINES.** Theatre has derived-blocking practice four centuries deep — a script states relations, a production solves them into positions, the blocking binds for that run — and what it converged on is whether the audience can see who matters. "Every principal distinguishable from the viewpoint" is testable and is the named constant risk 2 asked for. Two corrections ride with it: **solve from scratch, never incrementally** (carrying state between solves makes layout path-dependent — the same constraints settle into different minima by route — a determinism hazard dressed as a stability fix; stability must come from tight constraints), and **the seed fills exactly the residual degrees of freedom** (parametric CAD's lesson: under-constrained sketches are the ones that wobble, so report the DOF — which turns "how much variety does this room have" into a number to assert on). Precedent for the failure mode is explicit and recent: [[MAP-68]] The Overworld shipped "built/correct/reviewed but the first-pass LOOK is not yet dialed in," and [[MAP-67]] The Diorama needed its relief exaggeration moved 120 → 800 because at 120 a 1000 m peak read flat. Therefore **budget a controller-driven visual tuning pass for the tile layer at the outset**, with the criteria as named constants, rather than discovering the need after it ships correct. Moot if [[CLIENT-relational-fine-layer]] is adopted | raw | med | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 4, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-replay-and-tenses | **Three cheap panes from negating "player-driven" and "shows the present."** Negating player-driven gives the **autonomous observer** — attract mode, spectator mode, and more interestingly the **replay**: driven by a recorded ledger rather than a hand, and since a save is a seed plus its marks ([decision 0007](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0007-seed-is-identity.md) plus The First Mark's played-world fold) a replay is tiny and shareable as a URL. Negating "shows the present" gives a **historiography pane** (the `why` window ships) and a **foresight pane** (The Foresight ships) — two panes needing *no new producer work at all*. Negating "one agent's vantage" gives the Walk metaplan's own scholar/ethnographer substitution. All three are the cheapest real enrichment in the atlas | raw | high | brainstorm + ideonomy pass 3, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-fine-layer-live | **The fine layer, made live — and its central prediction falsified.** The Hearth built a room interior nobody could reach. The Threshold crossed into it: `interior_of` (frozen at day 0), bubble-only `Occupancy` (0069), `MoveWithin`, and catch-up. **Keystone:** a threshold is one species of the seam between position scales, and a seam belongs to the room-graph EDGE, not either room's furniture. **The headline is a FALSIFICATION:** the preregistered warmth prediction came back null four times, each null eliminating a candidate. `room/furnishing/v1` is now a live determinism contract | shipped | high | [The Threshold chronicle](../chronicle/the-threshold.md); [spec](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-25-the-threshold-design.md) |
| CLIENT-furnishing-ladder | **Authored templates vs. a constraint solver is a false fork — they are rungs of one ladder, and the relational layer is identical at every rung** **CORRECTED and SHIPPED at rung 1 (The Hearth, 2026-07-25).** The ladder as first written had rung 1 as "authored room template, seeded pick" — which is exactly the [[CLIENT-language-not-catalogue]] failure arriving on the FIRST rung, since Alexander's patterns are SMALLER than a room and a room is a composition of them. Two corrections: the unit of authorship is a **pattern** (a relational fragment), and the ladder's axis is **who decides the RELATIONS**, not who decides the coordinates (coordinates are always solved, so old rungs 1–2 collapse — variation falls out of composition). Shipped rung 1: an authored 9-pattern inventory, per-room DERIVED selection (built/wild × cold/warm), and composition rules that carry the weight — each pattern declares WHERE it attaches (`Hub` / `Beside(kind)` / `Within(kind)`) and may declare another it COMPLETES (`requires`), so a bed BY THE FIRE is inadmissible where no fire was drawn. An **anti-hub test** demands a ≥3-hop route: hub composition (everything one step from the centre) leaves field decay nothing to decay over and reduces the demonstration to a single stride. The composed cold-built room runs threshold → ground → alcove ⊃ hearth → bed, four steps. (it reads objects and relations, never how they were placed). Rung 1 authored template + seeded pick (legibility GUARANTEED, variety low, culture explicit); 2 template + seeded variation; 3 constraint solve (legibility merely TUNED); 4 solve + historical wear; 5 creatures arrange their own space. **Start at 1–2** — the only band whose legibility is guaranteed rather than tuned, which is exactly what [[CLIENT-choreographer]] is anxious about, and constitutionally native (decision 0009, models author / dice roll: offline authoring whose output is committed and drift-checked). The inversion that decided it: **template REUSE is more accurate, not less** — real vernacular architecture is templated, a people's houses look alike, and a settlement whose rooms share a layout vocabulary is culture made VISIBLE, a signal a per-room solver destroys by making everything unique. Rungs 4–5 are not decoration: Alexander's argument that places made all at once are dead puts the quality being chased at the TOP of the ladder. Supersedes the metaplan's §6.3 grid-first campaign | spec'd | high | The Rose Window Amendment 1, ideonomy pass 6, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-position-as-statement | **Position is a social statement, not only physics** — the seating chart of any banquet is relational (host at the head, honoured guest to the right), solved into an arrangement, and sitting wrong is an OFFENSE. So the relational vocabulary should carry social predicates natively (`beside(host)`, `at_the_head`, `below_the_salt`) with CULTURE supplying the protocol and violations legible to everyone present. This is the linguistic/behavioural purpose of the interface landing directly in the spatial layer, and it is a far better demonstration of why the program exists than a tile grid would have been — the first thing worth building on [[CLIENT-relational-fine-layer]]. Couples the fine layer to culture derivation and to the social graph ([[SOC-9]]) | raw | high | The Rose Window Amendment 1, ideonomy pass 6, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-furnishing-reads-time | **A room's contents must derive from `(room seed, settlement state at t, history)`, not the room seed alone.** Nothing in the metaplan lets a room change across deep time: a tavern in a thriving settlement and the same tavern three centuries into its ruin would furnish identically, because both derive from one static seed. Hornvale is a deep-time world whose settlements rise and fall, so furnishing is a function of when. Also the first obvious CONSUMER for The Vestige's residue-density and forgotten-fraction metrics, which shipped without one. Interacts with [[CLIENT-furnishing-ladder]] (a template is selected by culture AND era) and with the reserved desire-path idea below | raw | med | The Rose Window Amendment 1, ideonomy pass 5, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-language-not-catalogue | **A template vocabulary is a pattern language only if the COMPOSITION RULES carry the weight.** The deliberate reading is Alexander (*A Pattern Language* / *The Timeless Way of Building*): patterns stated as RELATIONS, indexed by what people DO, authored and small while the buildings they generate are many and large — which is decision 0009 and `World { seed, ledger }` in another domain, since a pattern language IS a compression scheme and a compression scheme is always a theory of what matters. The cautionary half is the load-bearing one: when software borrowed Alexander, design patterns became a CATALOGUE OF SOLUTIONS rather than a generative language, and that is when the idea died. **The test for any later spec: if its substance is template COUNT it has gone wrong; if it is adjacency and composition it is right.** Deeper root, and why legibility and computability keep coinciding here: both are consequences of SPARSITY — a room with 8 objects has ~8 anchors and ~20 relations that matter against the grid's ~10⁴ pairwise distances, and sparsity is simultaneously what makes a description sayable in a sentence and cheap to fold, cache and diff | raw | high | The Rose Window Amendment 1, ideonomy pass 6, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-epoch-granularity | **Epoch granularity is DECLARED, not discovered — split a churny derivation's labels by blast radius before the first bump.** With derived geometry causal ([[CLIENT-room-grid-authority]]) the placement algorithm is a determinism contract, and the owner expects MANY epochs. So declare two narrow versioned labels from the first commit: `room/furnishing/vN` (WHAT objects a room has — rare, large radius, contents move) and `room/layout/vN` (WHERE the solver puts them — frequent, SMALL radius: future outcomes only, since history is committed). There are only 20 seed-derivation labels workspace-wide today and most carry no version suffix; `room/child` / `room/face` are structural and must never move. Companion discipline: **pin invariants, not values** — every epoch moves what preregistered studies pin, and decision 0016's guard forbids editing a study to match its new result, so frequent epochs mean frequent owner calls unless pins are epoch-durable (precedent: the health null-control abandoned `prevalence < 0.02` for chronicity-stays-zero + every-run-recovers). Generalizes beyond the client to any subsystem intended to churn | raw | high | The Rose Window Amendment 1, ideonomy pass 5, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-semilattice-caution | **`RoomAddr` is a tree and Alexander argues places are semi-lattices — the project already pays that tax without naming it.** "A City is Not a Tree" (1965): real places have overlapping, non-hierarchical structure, and designers keep imposing trees because trees are tractable. `RoomAddr { face, path }` is literally an icosahedral quadtree, and the symptom is already on record as an engineering annoyance rather than a modelling error — cross-parent neighbour lookup, "the fiddly case The Excursion punted on at face boundaries." A relation graph IS a semi-lattice (a thing belongs to several groupings at once), which argues for treating [[CLIENT-relational-fine-layer]] as the truth and the quadtree as an INDEX over it. Not a decision Amendment 1 takes; a standing tension later work should expect, and a general tell worth keeping: **when a fiddly case resists every attempt at elegance, suspect the tractable structure is wrong rather than the code** | raw | med | The Rose Window Amendment 1, ideonomy pass 6, 2026-07-25 |
| CLIENT-desire-paths | **Rooms accumulate the wear of how creatures actually move through them** — the urban-planning desire path: people do not walk where the paths were drawn, they wear their own. Feeds, and is fed by, The Vestige's residue machinery and [[CLIENT-furnishing-reads-time]]; the piecemeal-growth end of Alexander's argument, where he claimed the quality actually lives | raw | low | The Rose Window Amendment 1, ideonomy pass 6, 2026-07-25 |
