## The domain map — capabilities that want their own crate

The architectural companion to the frontier map: which latent capabilities
want to become their own **domain crate** (kernel-only, meeting siblings only
at the `worldgen` composition root) rather than staying a feature of an
existing domain. DOM-1 is the framing — the decomposition lens and the three
structural gaps (the empty living middle, the absent self-driven layer, the
missing decay engine); DOM-2 onward are the individual crate proposals,
priority-ordered by leverage × readiness. Each proposal **realizes** feature
rows already in the registry; the DOM row records the *crate decision* (what
it owns, near/long-term, what it splits from), never restating the idea. A
feature is not a crate, and no split is scheduled — each earns its spec only
when a campaign pulls for it (frontier §domain-map).

| ID     | Idea                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Status     | Conf          | Where                                                                            |
| ------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------- | ------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DOM-1  | The domain-decomposition lens + the three structural gaps — reading the nine domains along animacy / autonomy / direction. The gaps: the **living middle is nearly empty** (physical and abstract poles are full, but the *alive* layer is one thin authored `species` crate — no biosphere, ecology, demography, disease); **nothing is self-driven yet** (every domain is externally driven by physics or a one-shot genesis pass; institutions/markets/polities that run themselves over time are unbuilt); **there is no decay engine** (the world only accretes — the memory economy is the designed eroder and is unbuilt). Most registry rows are *features*; a minority are latent *crates*. Reframes the whole registry by where each idea should live | elaborated | — (framing)   | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-2  | **biosphere** crate — vegetation and fungal cover as fields over (space × climate), fauna on the fast–slow life-history axis: the missing living substrate beneath the species-peoples. Realizes BIO-6/BIO-7. Highest near-term; already the next roadmap build                                                                                            | raw        | high          | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-3  | **ecology** crate — trophic dynamics, carrying capacity, competitive exclusion and coexistence-under-viability (the clearing/allocation machinery) priced over the biosphere. Realizes MAP-22/BIO-7; may share the biosphere crate until MAP-22's mechanisms land. Near-term                                                                               | raw        | high          | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-4  | **demography** crate — population as a field, settlements as condensations, era-ticked genesis history; extracted from `settlement` (decision 0047). **Shipped** (campaign *The Gathering*): the carrying-capacity field and flow-condensation half of MAP-7 lives here now, plus the migrated MAP-22 founder floor; era-ticked genesis history remains unbuilt, riding on a later history campaign. The settlement-facing water *network* proper (named rivers/lakes as entities, flow/water-table fields, flood/spring phenomena) and the crate split itself remain unbuilt — SEQ-2's people-infrastructure layer is still ahead, now with a real salt/fresh truth and real siting to build on | elaborated | high          | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate); [The Freshet chronicle](../chronicle/the-freshet.md); [The Confluence chronicle](../chronicle/the-confluence.md) |

Ore *point-deposits* (not just the prospectivity field) and the settlement-layer half of "gating the underworld and technology" remain unbuilt, so MAP-10/TECH-3 stay `raw` below · **Ore bodies + karst/caves-as-features shipped** (The Lode, 2026-07-22, inside domains/terrain); a standalone crate + the settlement-layer half remain. | elaborated | med           | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate); [The Ground chronicle](../chronicle/the-ground.md) |

Long-tail                                                                                                                                                                                                    | raw        | med           | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-17 | **metaphysics** crate — the pin-selected rule-system (magic as a second physics): the single gate for the whole speculative cluster, one crate rather than a sprawl. Realizes UNI-2. Taste-gated                                                                                                                                                           | raw        | med           | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-18 | **thanatology / undead** crate — the death-animate ruleset as a generative axis-space (not a bestiary), a sourced-and-sunk stock; a `metaphysics` output built only behind the UNI-15 gating discipline. Realizes UNI-4…UNI-15. Metaphysics-gated                                                                                                          | raw        | med           | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-19 | **prophecy / fate** crate — the future-facing epistemic layer, fate rigidity as one metaphysical scalar. Realizes MAP-13…MAP-16. Metaphysics-gated; furthest out                                                                                                                                                                                          | raw        | med           | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-20 | **Modes are not crates — the domain map's taxonomy is missing a bin.** The map sorts every row into *feature* (lives inside one existing domain) or *crate* (a new kernel-only domain) and applies one test: "can be added without editing any sibling." **DOM-17 (`metaphysics`) and DOM-19 (`prophecy / fate`) both fail it, and neither is a feature** — magic lives inside six domains at once, terrain needs a magic-aware carve, and domain→domain dependency is constitutionally forbidden, so a metaphysics *crate* could never inform the thing it most needs to inform. They are a third kind, for which this document's own prophecy section already coined the word on the spot — **a mode** ("this cluster may be a *mode* of the religion-and-epistemic stack rather than a new domain") — and which the domain map has no bin for. One section of the frontier needed a category the other section's taxonomy lacks. **Why the map cannot see it:** it reads the nine along animacy / autonomy / direction, all axes of *subject matter*, and a mode has no subject matter — only a relationship to it. That is a limit of the instrument, not a gap in the analysis, and it is structurally the same error as MAP-54's overloaded floor: **a cross-cutting property cannot be located by a per-subject taxonomy, exactly as an aggregate property cannot be enforced by a per-element rule.** **The fix is already constitutional:** a mode is a **kernel-level rule base + dial, consumed as magic-aware provider tiers across many domains** — "provider tiers coexist; worlds choose" is the licensed sprawl, and `potency` living in `kernel/src/ecology.rs` rather than in any domain is the shipped precedent that this is already how it goes. DOM-17's instinct ("one crate rather than a sprawl") diagnoses correctly and prescribes wrongly: magic *must* sprawl. **Four kinds, not two** — feature / domain / mode / window — and DOM-aesthetics is probably the fourth: its own row reads "perception + culture + language", and a domain cannot depend on three domains, so the layering alone makes it a window | raw | med | ideonomy session 2026-07-16; [frontier §magic](frontier.md#magic-as-a-graph-grammar--the-debt-economy) |
| DOM-aesthetics | **aesthetics** crate — the generative output of expressive artifacts (text / image / music / built space) from a small per-culture vector behind a content→render seam; a structural sibling of `language` (a per-culture generative grammar) and the missing *output* twin to DOM-13 `perception`'s *input*. The whole expressive-culture cluster has no crate home today; this is it. Surfaced by reading the map for broken symmetries (perception-in without expression-out). Realizes EXP-1/EXP-2/EXP-3a; reads perception + culture + language. Strong near-to-mid | raw | high | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-taphonomy | **taphonomy** crate — the *physical* decay-and-preservation engine: what survives, what is buried, the settlement that becomes a tell and the organism that becomes a fossil, and the *bias* of the surviving record; the physical sibling of DOM-7 `memory` (the *cultural* decay engine) and the evidence substrate DOM-12 `epistemics` infers over. Fills the one empty animacy×direction cell (physical × decays); splits preservation-as-process from paleoclimate's strata. Realizes the physical half of MAP-8/MAP-2's evidence base. Mid | raw | med | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-agroecology | **agroecology** crate — the coupled people-managed ecosystem (the anthropogenic biome, niche construction): domestication, artificial selection, the agricultural carrying-capacity lift, the landscape a subsistence strategy remakes; homeless today between `culture`'s subsistence, DOM-2 biosphere, DOM-8 technology, and DOM-10 genetics, none of which owns the *coupling*. Realizes part of TECH-1 / BIO-4. Mid | raw | med | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-transmission | **transmission** crate — population-level spread dynamics of traits, rumours, innovations, and styles through minds (dual-inheritance replicator dynamics, diffusion of innovations): the informational twin of DOM-9 `epidemiology`'s disease contagion. MAP-4 (myth phylogenetics), LANG-1 (contact), and DOM-7 memory each *touch* it but none owns the diffusion mechanism; weakest of the four and may prove a shared field rather than a crate. Realizes the MAP-4/LANG-1 mechanism layer | raw | low | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-ocean | **oceanography / hydrosphere** crate — the open ocean as a circulating physical field (thermohaline flow, gyres, upwelling, an interannual oscillation) driving climate, marine productivity, and MAP-11's water-volume habitat; distinct from `hydrology` (DOM-5, freshwater and settlement-facing). **Contested:** captured today as the climate feature `CLIM-ocean-currents`, and a kernel-only crate conflicts with the layering rule (it must read climate's wind field and feed its SST). Records the promote-to-crate *question* — warranted only if the ocean outgrows a current field into marine ecology + 3D thermal structure | raw | low | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-cryosphere | **cryosphere / glaciology** crate — ice sheets, glaciers, sea ice, permafrost as fields that store water, sculpt terrain (moraines, U-valleys, fjords), and gate habitability. **Contested:** captured today as `CLIM-cryosphere`; as a standalone crate it must read climate (temperature/precipitation) and feed terrain, conflicting with kernel-only layering. The promote-out question, warranted if glacial landform-sculpting outgrows a climate water-store. `paleoclimate` cycles the ice (MAP-6) but owns no flowing-ice field | raw | low | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-fire | **pyrogeography / fire-regime** crate — wildfire as a recurring disturbance field (fire-return interval, fuel load), fire-adapted biomes, and the human fire-management coupling (fire-stick farming). **Contested:** captured today as `CLIM-hazards` (the fire-return term) and sibling to `geohazards` (DOM-15); a standalone crate would depend on climate, biosphere, and agroecology at once (it is at once physical process and human tool), so it reads more as a cross-domain field than a kernel-only crate. **Generalized by BIO-36 (2026-07-16):** this row's own "reads more as a cross-domain field" verdict is that row's premise, widened from one contributor to the field itself — fire is one writer among many (glaciation, volcanism, clearing, grazing, strife) into a single composed disturbance field that vegetation reads. The reason a fire crate cannot exist is the reason the *disturbance* hub cannot either; both compose at the composition root instead | raw | low | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-air | **aeronomy / atmospheric composition** crate — the *chemistry* of the air (O₂, CO₂, aerosols) as a pinnable, deep-time-evolving, biology-coupled parameter gating fire behaviour (DOM-fire), maximum body size (BIO-2), and respiration for arriving (MAP-17) or aquatic (MAP-11) species. **Contested:** the greenhouse knob is captured as `CLIM-greenhouse`; the composition-as-upstream-parameter reading (a field others read, depending on nothing) is the one part with a plausible kernel-only case, the rest a climate/paleoclimate feature | raw | low | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-ethology | **ethology / faunal behaviour** crate — the behavioural repertoire of *non-sapient* fauna (migration, swarming, territoriality, predation, herding) emitted as phenomena the observation stack renders. **Contested:** BIO-7 already owns fauna as predator/prey/forage *fields* and `psychology` (DOM-6) owns the *peoples'* decision vector; behaviour-as-observed-event is most likely a biosphere feature (the migrating herd as a phenomenon over BIO-7's fields), not a separate crate | raw | low | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |
| DOM-symbiosis | **symbiosis / mutualism** crate — beneficial cross-species coupling (pollination, seed dispersal, nitrogen fixation, mycorrhizae, gut flora) as a carrying-capacity *multiplier*: the constructive counterpart to `epidemiology` (DOM-9, parasitic coupling) and the positive pole `ecology` (DOM-3) / MAP-22 competitive-exclusion underweights (its commensal tenancy is between *peoples*, not organisms). **Contested:** the one genuine content gap, but mutualism is a trophic-interaction type that belongs *inside* `ecology` — a feature that crate should own, flagged here at DOM level per request. Gunkel's *proseases* move applied to the map | raw | low | [frontier §domain-map](frontier.md#the-domain-map--which-capabilities-want-their-own-crate) |

## Process and knowledge architecture

| ID     | Idea                                                                     | Status          | Conf | Where                                                                |
| ------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------- | ---- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| PROC-1 | Campaigns drop the "Year" naming (sequence number + name, forward-only)  | ratified (0017) | —    | [decision 0017](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0017-campaigns-drop-year-naming.md)     |

| PROC-3 | This idea registry — a thin retrieval layer over the frontier essays. The "thin" was aspirational for a long while: rows accreted campaign narrative until the mean Idea cell ran to 833 characters. [The Shelf-Mark](../chronicle/the-shelf-mark.md) made it enforceable — a 600-character cap landed as a ratchet, plus four other row-form checks | shipped         | —    | this file; [The Shelf-Mark](../chronicle/the-shelf-mark.md)                                                            |
| PROC-4 | The docs map (`docs/README.md`) — the doc-of-docs and knowledge pipeline | shipped         | —    | [docs/README.md](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/README.md)                                       |
| PROC-5 | The Confidence Gradient is a re-scored map, not frozen — `book/src/open-questions.md` re-axised onto checkability/autonomy (a bet's confidence = whether the world can score itself on it, self-scorable via the Lab vs taste-gated); re-scored in the DoD freshness sweep when a campaign resolves a bet, and CI link-checks the chapter so its evidence pointers can't rot silently | ratified        | —    | [decision](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0030-the-confidence-gradient-is-re-scored-not-frozen.md) |
| PROC-6 | The result-quieting guard — a CI check that rejects a newly-added `#[ignore]` (or a loosened pinned constant / new `#[allow]`) on a preregistered calibration test unless the diff cites a decision, encoding ADR 0016 mechanically. The exact near-miss recurred across two campaigns (Y2-3 and The Words): an implementer's first pass `#[ignore]`d an honestly-failing preregistered row rather than reporting it. Turns the retrospectives' standing question — "does this diff quiet a result rather than pin it?" — into a grep | shipped | high | ideonomy session (workflow/tooling); `windows/lab/tests/preregistration_guard.rs` |
| PROC-7 | The mechanical Definition-of-Done gate — a PR check asserting the DoD artifacts a merged campaign owes actually exist: a chronicle entry (`book/src/chronicle/`), a retrospective (`docs/retrospectives/`, PROC-2/decision 0020), and a re-scored Confidence Gradient when the campaign moved a bet (PROC-5). Encodes decision 0013 (DoD includes the book) as a check rather than prose obligations a controller must remember at merge | raw | med | ideonomy session (workflow/tooling) |
| PROC-8 | Knowledge-base drift linters — mechanize the freshness sweep that is currently human-driven and was under-scoped mid-campaign twice: (a) a book-chapter staleness scan mapping each chapter to the crate/domain it describes and warning when the code's mtime outruns the chapter's, and (b) a decision-log consistency check — every `decision X` cited in a code comment resolves to a file in `docs/decisions/`, and orphaned decisions nothing references are surfaced. The `docs_consistency` drift-check is the natural host | shipped | med | ideonomy session (workflow/tooling); `cli/tests/docs_consistency.rs` decision-cite check (book-staleness scan deferred: mtimes do not survive checkout; needs a git-log design) |
| PROC-9 | The layering diagram emitted from its enforcer — generate the crate-dependency / constitutional-layering diagram (`kernel → domains → windows → cli`) from the same rules `cli/tests/architecture.rs` already enforces, and drift-check it, so the picture in the book is authored by the checker rather than hand-drawn and left to rot out of sync with the enforced graph | shipped | med | ideonomy session (workflow/tooling); `cli/tests/architecture.rs` → `book/src/reference/layering.md` |
| PROC-10 | `just doctor` / the repo self-map — one command consolidating the orientation knowledge now scattered across CLAUDE.md, the memory files, and the decision log (the layering, the cost-ordered gate TOOL-14, where each committed artifact comes from, the determinism contracts), so a fresh session or a cold-started subagent orients in one read instead of re-deriving it every time | shipped | med | ideonomy session (workflow/tooling); `scripts/doctor.sh` (`make doctor`) |
| PROC-11 | The unmix doctrine — durable knowledge is either *derivable from source* (regenerate; drift-check; never hand-edit) or *irreducible* (authored once; makes no factual claim that can rot), and the doc-mass pain is that the two are stored in the **same** artifacts, so each is hand-maintained at the cadence of its fastest-changing fact (a MIXED memory file rotted its push-state within hours; the book mixes generated reference pages with authored essays). The move is to *unmix*, not to "derive everything": pull derivable facts out of authored artifacts so the authored half shrinks to the irreducible. Carries a **drift-rate → mechanism** rule: store only geological-rate facts; derive-on-read anything faster (push/merge state ← query git, never store), invalidate-on-write for day-scale drift (CI drift-check), a live claim for instantaneous collision. The abstraction-lift: all three process pains are one shape — a cache with no invalidation protocol (Karlton). The concrete done-ness instrument is PROC-7 sharpened from "artifacts exist" to a **trial balance that foots to zero** (merged/pushed/gate/drift/chronicle/retro/re-score/registry/census as line items; done ⇔ balance zero), which would have made The Reckoning's census debt visible at close instead of buried in prose | raw | high (workflow) | ideonomy session 2026-07-17 (process re-evaluation) |
| PROC-12 | Regenerate-on-conflict for generated artifacts — the mechanical half of parallel-session collision pain is *entirely* generated-artifact collision. Determinism forces those files committed, and committing them is what makes them collide; a driver resolving by **rerunning the generator** makes the class a no-op. **Shipped:** research found two shapes, not one — append-only lists get git's builtin `merge=union`, zero custom code; reference dumps get a custom driver. Two deliberate exclusions: census data, and keystone fixtures — where a conflict is the exact signal the fixture exists to produce | shipped | high (workflow) | [The Standing Offer](../chronicle/the-standing-offer.md) |
| PROC-13 | The anti-vacuity gate for type-level proofs — a type-level proof of a behavioral property is worse than no proof if it is *vacuous* (a phantom witness or unconstraining bound that passes silently forever; cf. measure-don't-narrate's four-green-tests). The discipline: distinguish **construction** proofs (make the illegal state unrepresentable — non-vacuous by definition, since there is no representable-but-excluded state) from **recognition** proofs (a witness of a property the code allegedly has — vacuity-prone). Every construction proof ships a `compile_fail` doctest that *exhibits* the state it forbids — the std-only, zero-dep "consideration" (contract-law re-instantiation: an illusory type forbids nothing and is void). Global/relational properties (byte-identity across two runs; pin-consumes-same-draws) are inherently MEASURED — faking a type there is the vacuous-witness trap. Mutation-check both kinds (the guard must flip red). Lintable as the type-level twin of PROC-6; leaves a proof-strength ladder (exhibited-construction > construction > measured+mutation > witness > prose) that is also the unmix ladder — climbing a rung turns a rotting prose claim into an enforced one | raw | high (correctness) | ideonomy session 2026-07-17 (fork 1: proofs into behavior) |
| PROC-14 | The Precise/Quantized typestate — the Lorenz guardrail (decision 0033: quantize at the emit boundary ONLY, never the compute path; never seed a chaotic integrator from quantized floats) made a *type error* rather than a remembered rule. Two float newtypes: `Precise(f64)` (compute path — noise, sculpting, orbits) and `Quantized(f64)` (emit currency), with `quantize: Precise->Quantized` the one legal crossing, `serialize` accepting only `Quantized`, compute fns only `Precise`, and deliberately NO `Quantized->Precise`. "Serialize a full-precision float" and "feed a quantized float back into the forward integrator" become compile errors. The concrete proof-of-concept for fork 1 (construction proof of a genuine behavioral invariant); byte-identity must be shown preserved (the wrapper is compute-only, zero-cost). Anti-vacuity exhibit (PROC-13) is natural: a `compile_fail` doctest on the missing reverse crossing | raw | med (correctness) | ideonomy session 2026-07-17 (fork 1) |
| PROC-15 | The Book as a coverage metric on the world model — Nathan's "the Book enforces discipline on what we can imagine and implement" made mechanical. The set of committed Facts / concept-predicates that CANNOT yet be rendered (no entry in the predicate→frame construction table, or no Common lexeme) IS the world-model's completeness to-do list; render-coverage % (fraction of fact-predicates with a construction + a Common realization) is a drift-checkable completeness metric. **Inverts the doc relationship** ([[PROC-11]] unmix): the self-writing Book ([[LANG-35]]) isn't a description OF the world, it's a proof-obligation ON it — an un-renderable fact is a *modeling gap surfaced by CI*, not a prose lag. The sharpest expression of the [[UNI-28]] View discipline: coverage of the derived view measures completeness of the source | **shipped (C1)** | med (workflow) | [The First Sentence](../chronicle/the-first-sentence.md) — `hornvale book` emits the coverage report |
| PROC-16 | The correspondence invariant — cross-ledger conceptual completeness *by construction*. Lifts [[PROC-15]]'s coverage metric to the whole loop **compute → concept → lexeme → percept → cognition**: a modeled quantity posts a correspondent to every ledger or files a typed void, making *modeled-but-unnamed* uncompilable, not a CI-surfaced lag. The void-list *is* the backlog, an instance of the [[UNI-28]] View discipline. **Shipped:** the `Manifest` choke-point is the sole concept-registration path and worlds stay byte-identical; `concepts --manifest` renders a coverage ledger that foots | shipped | med (workflow) | [The Correspondence](../chronicle/the-correspondence.md) |
| PROC-17 | Centralize seed-derivation labels at every `.derive()` call site. `Seed::derive` took a bare `&str` and is pure FNV-1a over the label's bytes, so a typo'd inline literal does not fail to compile — it silently derives an unrelated but valid stream. 139 raw-literal call sites, ~112 in production genesis code. **Shipped:** `StreamLabel<'a>` replaces the bare parameter, buildable only via `from_static` or `dynamic`, so a bare literal no longer compiles; a `type-audit` check flags `from_static(<literal>)` outside a `streams.rs`. Pure call-site migration — no string value changed, no epoch bump | shipped | high (correctness) | [The Given Word](../chronicle/the-given-word.md) |
| PROC-18 | A `stream_labels!` declaration macro unifying "declare the per-leg constant" with "register it in `stream_labels()`" — surfaced by [[PROC-17]]'s own ideonomy pass. Hand-documented path patterns were prose decoupled from the `.derive()` calls building them: the desync mode that recurred across three campaigns. **Shipped:** the macro fuses constant declarations with their manifest entries; all 8 drawing crates migrated, and research found the bug already live on `main` in two of them. Astronomy's hand-maintained cross-check test retires — the drift it watched is now structurally impossible | shipped | high (correctness) | [The Single Saying](../chronicle/the-single-saying.md) |
| PROC-19 | Migrate `domains/history` to `stream_labels!` — surfaced by [[PROC-18]]'s own close: the crate landed on `main` mid-campaign using the pre-macro pattern, and its shape fits neither macro form, mixing a root+leg group with already-qualified flat entries in one file. **Shipped:** a third arm declaring both together and generating one combined `stream_labels()` — pure addition, zero risk to the 8 already-migrated crates. A real side effect, found mid-execution and accepted: this crate held the workspace's last root+leg pair published under bare manifest keys, now corrected to match convention | shipped | high (correctness) | [The Compound Word](../chronicle/the-compound-word.md) |
| PROC-20 | Verify-before-assert for spec/plan claims about generated output or tool behavior — surfaced by [[PROC-12]]'s own retrospective: a claim of the shape "this generated artifact won't change (much)" or "this tool behaves this way on failure" was asserted in a spec or plan, then found wrong by a subagent's own empirical check, FOUR separate times across three consecutive campaigns in one session ([[PROC-18]]'s manifest-diff claim; [[PROC-19]] hitting the identical claim's own incomplete correction; [[PROC-12]]'s test-setup build-break assumption and its git-merge-driver-fallback assumption, the latter recurring even in the CORRECTION to the former). Every instance was caught before merge (by a task implementer or the final whole-branch review) and cost nothing functionally, but four in a row is a real gap, not one campaign's carelessness. The concrete fix: a spec/plan-review checklist item requiring that any claim of this shape name the exact command that was run to verify it (not just the value asserted) — e.g. "diffed the regenerated output against the committed file: <diff>" or "reproduced the failure path directly: <transcript>" — rather than accepting a claim inferred from reasoning about what "should" happen. **Ratified:** extended `campaign-autopilot`'s own skill file directly (`.claude/skills/campaign-autopilot/SKILL.md`) with a new "Verify generated-artifact and tool-behavior claims" section plus a matching red-flags row, folded into G2's existing self-review — the lighter of the two candidate fixes, since a self-review pass already runs before every spec-consistency gate and this is one more concrete thing for it to check (the heavier option, a new standalone skill an author would need to remember to invoke separately, was rejected: all four instances were caught by a *reviewer*, never the drafting session, so the review-side gate is where this was actually missing). No campaign wrapper — a direct skill-file edit, matching the precedent of prior direct edits to this same file | ratified | high (workflow) | `.claude/skills/campaign-autopilot/SKILL.md` |
| UNI-28 | The View discipline — the lattice of Hornvale's recurring structures has one top: a *derived, pure, deterministic, drift-checkable projection over a canonical collection* (a fold, a sort, or a filter, recomputed-and-diffed). It structurally subsumes the permutation indexes (sorted view of the fact set), UNI-21's reflection (view of the concept registry), code-as-docs (view of source), the done-ness trial-balance (view of git+fs+CI), and the softmax/argmax family (folded view of a weighted map). "Unify instead of split" buys ONE current-ness question — recompute and diff — answered identically over every collection. **Guard (the over-unification knife-edge):** unify the SHAPE (a `View` trait, open for extension / closed for modification) never the CONTENT (no god-enum of view kinds — that recouples every domain to the hub, violating "adding a domain never edits an existing one"). **Antichain — keep OUT of the core:** `Field`s (functions over an infinite domain, sampled not enumerated — not a fold) and irreducible wisdom (projects no source); and never symmetrize the trace protocol's three deliberately-distinct reads (Facts/Phenomena/Fields) — Phenomena being its own read is what decouples the consumer from the producer | raw | med | ideonomy session 2026-07-17 (fork 2: unify not split) |
| UNI-29 | The self-describing program — the north star unifying [[UNI-21]] (the self-reflective ledger), [[LANG-35]] (the self-writing Book), and [[PROC-11]] (unmix): the same generative engine that renders the WORLD's self-description (the gazetteer) ultimately renders the PROGRAM's self-description. The frontier, chronicle, and studies become derived views the engine writes / considers / explores / deletes according to its current knowledge and abilities, not hand-authored prose — "the program creates the worlds and manifests its understanding of itself." **Near-term (the metaplan's C1):** THE Book becomes the primary published artifact and the frontier + chronicle move to appendices. **Long-term:** they too are generated. The endgame of the View discipline ([[UNI-28]]) — every durable text, world-facing or self-facing, is a derived view over a canonical collection (the fact graph for the world; the capability schema of [[UNI-21]] for the self). The irreducible residue shrinks to the taste that authors the *grammar and the schemas*, never the sentences | raw | high | brainstorm 2026-07-17 (metaplan scoping) |
| UNI-30 | The living present — the whole timeline is one derivable *social field* (Fields over space×time); forward-evolution is *replay of the genesis epoch-engine from the seed* (re-derivation at a larger T, never integration from saved state → Lorenz-safe by construction). "Now" is not a clock reading but the *frontier of intervention*: an untouched region's whole timeline is pure-seed-derived and freely scrubbable; the player's first action is a ledger delta the replay reads, branching the future. Autonomy resolves by lazy evaluation — *defined* over all time everywhere (the cycle turns while you're away), *computed* only where observed. Agency = perturbing a predictable cycle. The [derived-view architecture](frontier.md#the-derived-view-architecture--the-game-layer-as-readouts-over-one-ledger) extended to the time axis; elaborated with [[SOC-10]]. | elaborated | med | [frontier §living-community](frontier.md#the-living-community--history-as-a-baked-derivable-biography) |
| UNI-39 | **World-identity neutrality is not census neutrality** — a campaign that leaves every committed fact byte-identical can still move the census, because the census reads the coexistence **shadow** (`demography_report`'s derived densities and `stack_settlements`), not the ledger. The Waterline demonstrated both halves at once: seed 42's world JSON was `cmp`-identical throughout, and `composition-variance` moved on **all 1000 census rows** — because ~97 of 494 DERIVED settlements sit on ocean cells and their species mix changed. The practical consequence is that `lens_purity` passing tells you nothing about whether the census carve-out is needed, and the two must be checked independently. Worth a mechanical check at close: which committed artifacts read derived views rather than facts? | raw | med | [The Waterline chronicle](../chronicle/the-waterline.md) |
| MAP-31b | Max-weight categorical inheritance IS the beta->infinity limit of the K^beta softmax (MAP-31) — locale/biome's argmax corner-inheritance and the coexistence stack's Boltzmann share are the same `WeightedCategorical<K>::reduce(beta)` at two temperatures (MAP-31 already notes beta->inf collapses to the retired argmax; this names the locale/biome call sites as its infinity-instance). A shippable dedup: the argmax sites become `reduce(inf)`, the softmax sites `reduce(beta)`; safe because the two are comparable (a limit, not a forced marriage). Cross-links [[UNI-28]] as a concrete instance of the folded-view-over-a-weighted-map node | raw | low | ideonomy session 2026-07-17 (fork 2) |

The judgment-half companion of PROC-7: when its mechanical gate ships, this keeps the half CI cannot score | shipped | med | ideonomy follow-up session (boundary rituals); `.claude/skills/closing-a-campaign/` |
| PROC-campaign-autopilot | Campaign autopilot — an overlay skill auto-resolving routine Superpowers gates (G1/G2/G4/G5) against Nathan's standing policy via a decision ledger; G3 and G6 stay hard stops, and the fidelity/census/destructive-action carve-outs stay manual | shipped | — | `.claude/skills/campaign-autopilot/`; spec [2026-07-14-campaign-autopilot-design](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-14-campaign-autopilot-design.md) |
| PROC-constitution-coverage | The constitution coverage matrix — `docs_consistency` proves the docs' link graph but nothing proves the spec's claim graph; a drift-checked index mapping every constitutional constraint to its named guard test(s), failing when a clause has no guard — the PROC-8 discipline lifted from links to claims | raw | med | ideonomy session (test system, 2026-07-11) |

The ratchet prevents new bloat and the list can only shrink, so this is genuinely incremental: any campaign touching a waived row can compact it and drop its line. The remaining work is judgment, not deletion — a long `raw` or `elaborated` row is usually mislabeled, so the remedy is relocating real argument into `frontier.md` and flipping the status, not trimming prose until it fits | raw | high (mechanics) | [The Shelf-Mark](../chronicle/the-shelf-mark.md) |
| PROC-waiver-ceiling | A length waiver should be a ceiling, not a blank cheque — [The Shelf-Mark](../chronicle/the-shelf-mark.md)'s cap exempts waived rows *unconditionally*, so a row already over budget can grow without limit. Found on its own merge: a parallel campaign appended to MAP-27, taking it 1887 → 2704 characters, and nothing complained. The ratchet stops new rows exceeding the cap and stops a compacted row keeping its waiver, but has no third tooth. Fix: record each waiver's length beside its ID and fail on growth — worth landing before [[PROC-registry-burndown]] | raw | high (mechanics) | [The Shelf-Mark](../chronicle/the-shelf-mark.md) |
| PROC-registry-triage | Triage the registry's `raw` backlog — 323 rows are `raw` ("captured but not yet elaborated"), the largest block of text in the file, and nothing records whether an old capture is still live. No check can decide this: liveness is a judgment, not a property of the row. The question is whether `raw` is one status doing two jobs — a fresh capture awaiting elaboration, and a direction quietly abandoned — and whether the second wants its own status (`lapsed`?) so the index distinguishes *not yet argued* from *no longer wanted*. Raised, unanswered, by [The Shelf-Mark](../chronicle/the-shelf-mark.md) | raw | med | [The Shelf-Mark](../chronicle/the-shelf-mark.md) |
| PERF-genesis-terrain-rebuilds | Genesis re-derives the full terrain sculpting pipeline ~9× per world across its stages (paleoclimate, habitability, the peopling stage, …), which dominates both debug and release generation time. Surfaced while fixing [[CLIM-felt-weather]]'s phenomena-source perf regression — that regression had added ~62 *more* full climate+terrain rebuilds (naming/religion observe the world per settlement and per candidate deity, and each observation rebuilt the climate); that half is fixed (sources built once per generation, genesis 52s → ~10s). The remaining pre-existing waste — thread ONE built terrain/climate through every genesis stage instead of re-deriving — is byte-identity-risky (it touches the composition root broadly), so it was deliberately deferred. **Shipped ([The Single Sculpt](../chronicle/the-single-sculpt.md)):** reconnected the terrain flow — the terrain stage keeps its sculpted globe, and `climate_from`/`paleoclimate_from` (plus almanac `_lines_from` variants) thread the pre-built value (the existing `_from` idiom), `_of` wrappers kept for standalone callers. Genesis 4→1 terrain sculpts (~10s→6.7s), almanac render ~7→1 (~8s→2.9s), **byte-identical** (the kept globe is provably `terrain_of`'s). | shipped | high (perf) | [The Single Sculpt](../chronicle/the-single-sculpt.md) |
| PERF-atomic-publish | `publish()` writes goldens in place — remove-stale, then summary/rows/schema/hundreds of SVGs — so two concurrent publishers to one study directory can interleave into a torn set that is individually well-formed and so drift-check-invisible. Write to a temp dir and rename instead: tearing becomes impossible rather than excluded. Discarded from The Turnstile's G1 as larger than the hazard exclusion already covers; revisit if a second writer ever legitimately shares a goldens directory. | raw | med | [The Turnstile](../chronicle/the-turnstile.md) |
| PERF-build-artifact-sink | The discarded branch of [The Hoist](../chronicle/the-hoist.md)'s G1: rather than the build RETURNING its byproducts, the caller passes a sink that receives each artifact as its stage produces it. Rejected — it inverts control flow for every caller to serve one, and the Single-Sculpt `*_from` idiom wants a value in hand, not a callback. But it is the shape that generalizes: stage timings, per-stage determinism probes, streaming consumers. Revisit if a second consumer needs mid-build artifacts. | raw | med | [The Hoist](../chronicle/the-hoist.md) |
| PERF-lab-metric-rebuilds | The lab-metric sibling of [[PERF-genesis-terrain-rebuilds]] — the census metric path re-sculpted terrain pervasively, each buried inside a worldgen readout the metric called by name — invisible to a grep, found only by a flamegraph. **Shipped:** six profile-driven rounds of the Single-Sculpt idiom, each byte-identical. **285 → 5.8 CPU-s per world (~49×)**, the full census 4 h → **~7 min**. One round was reverted for zero measured gain: **a cost breakdown is a hypothesis until profiled**. Its deferred view-chain double sculpt shipped as The Hoist: **-24.3%**, byte-identical | shipped | high (perf) | [The Local Census](../chronicle/the-local-census.md), [The Hoist](../chronicle/the-hoist.md) |

## Rejected and foreclosed — do not reopen without new information

The registry's core job. Each row is a question that was asked and answered
`no`; a fresh opinion is not new information.

| ID    | Idea                                                                                                     | Status                     | Why                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Where                                                         |
| ----- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- |
| REJ-1 | Procedural macros for boilerplate (newtypes, predicate/label registration)                               | rejected → ratified (0019) | `syn`/`quote` breaks the serde-only allowlist; the candidates are save-format contracts whose value is being plain-text; `macro_rules!` is the ceiling. (0019 governs *workspace* crates; decision 0027 carves out non-workspace offline dev tools, which may use `syn`.)                                                                    | [decision 0019](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0019-no-procedural-macros.md), [`…parser-libraries`](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0027-non-workspace-dev-tools-may-use-parser-libraries.md)    |
| REJ-2 | An alignment axis / `evil: bool` / morally-typed species                                                 | rejected → ratified (0021) | conduct is a derived situation; "race" is a manufactured output — an alignment field collapses the whole interaction system into a lookup table                                                                           | [decision 0021](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0021-no-alignment-axis.md)       |
| REJ-3 | Splitting the frontier into multiple topic files                                                         | rejected                   | the doc's value is its interconnections (the unification thesis is a section); topic files turn edges into rotting cross-file links, and no clean cut avoids the shared spine. Use this registry + drain-to-specs instead | this conversation; [frontier preamble](frontier.md)           |
| REJ-4 | YAML for config files                                                                                    | rejected → ratified (0012) | JSON is already a dependency; a second format is redundant surface                                                                                                                                                        | [decision 0012](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0012-config-is-json-not-yaml.md) |
| REJ-5 | New runtime dependencies (rand, chrono, clap, thiserror, a PNG/image crate, dimensional-analysis crates) | rejected → ratified (0004) | randomness comes from the kernel `Seed`/`Stream`; time is `WorldTime`; parsing is std-only; images/units are hand-rolled                                                                                                  | [decision 0004](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0004-no-new-dependencies.md)     |
| REJ-6 | Any ML model in the simulation core                                                                      | rejected → ratified (0009) | models author offline; the runtime is deterministic and seeded (models author, dice roll)                                                                                                                                 | [decision 0009](https://github.com/hornvale/hornvale/blob/main/docs/decisions/0009-models-author-dice-roll.md) |
